We propose LAMP, Layerwise Adaptive Mixing of Predictors, a training-free framework that combines global and regional information from multiple encoder layers. A single forward pass per view supplies all descriptors. Global and support-calibrated regional kernel predictors independently fit the same label residuals. Labeled validation data select their mixture in class-score space, preserving native feature dimensions without learned projections. LAMP-F further adapts text-anchored class prototypes, using a cached response to update the fixed-kernel residual predictions exactly. Experiments cover eleven datasets and ViT and ResNet backbones. LAMP achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracy among the compared published training-free ResNet-50 methods at 4, 8, and 16 shots, reaching 78.73% at 16 shots. Averaged across five support budgets, LAMP improves over a paired global-feature-only baseline by 1.14 and 1.59 percentage points on ViT-B/16 and ResNet-50; LAMP-F raises these gains to 1.96 and 2.54 points. LAMP-F reaches 16-shot accuracies of 83.57% and 79.10%, respectively. Controlled experiments identify multi-layer integration as the main source of improvement and examine pooling, calibration, validation labels, and computational cost. These findings show that combining layer-specific predictions can exploit complementary features from frozen encoders to improve few-shot classification.

</details>


### [105] [EVA-Cap: Optimizing Audiovisual Video Captioning via Event-Centric Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22186)
*Jiapeng Shi,Qiuxia Hou,Jiaqi Leng,Junke Wang,Yanhao Zhang,Haonan Lu,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EVA-Cap框架，将音视频视频描述分解为原子化音视频事件，把无结构文本监督转为事件中心对齐，通过SFT与GRPO训练，在事件级评测上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Omni Language Models的视频描述把生成当作无结构文本，忽视视频固有的语义结构，导致细粒度音视频对齐不佳。

Method: 将caption分解为原子音视频事件，构建事件中心质量控制的数据集EVA-Data；先SFT再GRPO，GRPO阶段以三项事件中心对齐目标优化：全局事件完整性、事件内归因、事件间同步。

Result: 事件级评测显著优于现有方法（ChronusAV平均+9.0%），通用音视频描述基准也有竞争力（WorldSense +3.3%）。

Conclusion: 事件中心的结构化对齐是可提升OLM音视频描述细粒度质量的有效范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in Omni Language Models (OLMs) provide a unified solution for audiovisual video captioning. Nevertheless, existing approaches predominantly treat captioning as an unstructured text generation process, neglecting the inherent semantic structure of videos and leading to suboptimal fine-grained audiovisual alignment. To bridge this gap, we propose EVA-Cap, a novel framework that decomposes captions into atomic audiovisual events and transforms unstructured text supervision into event-centric alignment. We train EVA-Cap via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on a self-curated dataset, EVA-Data, which is built with event-centric quality control. During the GRPO phase, the model is explicitly optimized against three event-centric alignment targets: global event completeness, intra-event attribution, and inter-event synchronization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EVA-Cap significantly outperforms existing methods in event-centric evaluation (e.g., +9.0% average gain on ChronusAV), while achieving competitive performance on general audiovisual captioning benchmarks (e.g., +3.3% on WorldSense).

</details>


### [106] [OpenGCUA: Toward a Fully Open Stack for Generalist Computer Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22200)
*Hanzhang Zhou,Long Li,Quyu Kong,Lei Wang,Panrong Tong,Long Chen,Tianyu Xia,Gongjie Zhang,Xu Zhang,Chen Liu,Jianan Zhang,Chenglin Cai,Zhengwen Zeng,Steven Hoi,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出开放通用计算机使用智能体项目 OpenGCUA 首版，核心是代码执行与"harness-to-model 决策转移"设计，在 OSWorld-v2 上使 8 个模型的平均部分分提升 19.6 分、每任务报告步数减少 53.0%，并发布真实设备 harness 与跨设备 SDK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用计算机使用智能体（GCUA）需在视觉与程序化接口间自主决定"如何观察、如何行动、如何组织工作"，但前沿实现（如商业闭源 CUA 系统）均未公开，导致其设计无法被系统研究。因此需要社区共建全栈开源方案。

Method: 以代码执行为核心行动原语，使中间结果驱动后续操作并复用模型既有编程能力；提出 harness-to-model 决策转移，把原本由固定 harness 规则承担的部分观察选择与任务状态决策交给模型；构建真实设备 harness 与跨机器/移动端/浏览器的统一 SDK。

Result: OSWorld-v2 上完整配置在全部 8 个受评模型（含 GPT-6 Astra、Claude Opus 5）上均提升任务分数，平均部分分 +19.6，报告步数平均减少 53.0%（对比批量纯 GUI 执行）；真实 macOS 四项任务中，部分 Qwen3.8-Max 运行报告推理 token 用量为 GPT-6 Astra 的 19.4 倍。

Conclusion: 开放全栈（实现、提示词、8 模型评测轨迹、真实设备 harness 与跨设备 SDK）可支撑 GCUA 的系统性研究；真实设备执行暴露了基准未覆盖的挑战与显著的模型效率差异。

Abstract: Computer use has reached a turning point: the focus is expanding from GUI-only interaction to systems that complete tasks on users' computers through visual and programmatic interfaces. We define a generalist computer-use agent (GCUA) as a system in which the model chooses how to observe, act, and organize its work across visual and programmatic interfaces. Yet frontier implementations, such as GPT-6 Astra's computer-use system, remain largely closed, limiting systematic study of their design. We introduce Open Generalist CUA (OpenGCUA), an ongoing project to develop and open the full stack for GCUAs with the community. This report presents the first release of OpenGCUA. Code execution is a key feature of our agent design: it lets intermediate results determine subsequent operations and enables models to apply their existing programming capabilities directly to computer interaction. We also propose harness-to-model decision transfer, moving selected observations and task-state decisions from fixed harness rules to the model. On OSWorld-v2, our full configuration improves task scores across all eight evaluated models, including GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5, raising mean partial score by +19.6 points and reducing reported steps per task by an average of 53.0% relative to batched GUI-only execution. We release our implementation and prompts, evaluation trajectories from all eight models, and preliminary versions of a real-device harness and a cross-device SDK. The harness addresses real-device challenges not fully captured by benchmarks, while the SDK provides a common programming interface across computers, mobiles and browsers. Across four real macOS tasks under our real-device harness, selected Qwen3.8-Max runs use 19.4 times as many reported reasoning tokens as GPT-6 Astra, highlighting substantial differences between models in real-device execution efficiency.

</details>


### [107] [TRACE: Topology-Aware Reconnection via Prediction-Adaptive Connectivity Enforcement for Thin-Structure Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22210)
*Junho Moon,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE：一种拓扑感知、预测自适应的骨架监督目标，通过为每条骨骼连接寻找当前预测下最易修复的替代重连路径并联合监督其瓶颈，提升细长结构分割的连通性与拓扑正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长结构（血管、道路、神经纤维等）对连通性错误极度敏感，少数局部误分割即可切断原本连续的路径。现有基于骨架的监督方法固定监督中心线位置，无法利用其他同样合理的局部绕行路线来恢复连通性，因此对断裂过于脆弱。

Method: 提出TRACE目标：(1) 用目标骨架定义所需的局部连接；(2) 对每个连接，在当前预测中识别"最易实现的有效重连"，并将该重连路径上的瓶颈点与对应骨架点联合监督，使监督随预测演化而自适应而非固定于中心线；(3) 引入拓扑感知的骨架载体（skeleton carrier），同时保持前景拓扑与类别特定拓扑，以保证连接定义的可靠性。

Result: 在六个2D与3D基准上开展大量实验，TRACE在保持较强分割精度的同时显著改善了连通性与拓扑质量，且训练开销低。代码将在接收后公开。

Conclusion: 将骨架监督从"固定中心线回归"转为"预测自适应的最易重连监督"，并配合拓扑保持的骨架载体，是提升细长结构连通性的有效途径，跨2D/3D任务具有泛化性。

Abstract: Thin, elongated structures are particularly sensitive to connectivity errors, as even a few local segmentation mistakes can disrupt otherwise continuous paths. Existing skeleton-based objectives typically supervise fixed centerline locations and do not account for alternative local routes that may restore connectivity. We propose TRACE, a topology-aware, prediction-adaptive objective that uses a target skeleton to define required local connections. For each connection, TRACE identifies the easiest valid reconnection under the current prediction and jointly supervises its bottleneck with the corresponding skeleton point. The supervision thus adapts to the evolving prediction rather than remaining fixed to the centerline. We further introduce a topology-aware skeleton carrier that preserves both foreground and class-specific topology, enabling reliable definition of these connections. Extensive experiments across six 2D and 3D benchmarks show that TRACE improves connectivity and topology while maintaining strong segmentation accuracy with low training overhead. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [108] [FutureLift: Lifting Trajectory Proposals into Bidirectional Future Graphs for Motion Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22215)
*Daniel Grimm,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FutureLift 是一个骨干无关的多模态轨迹精化器，将每条预测提案建模为异质未来图，一次前向即可用双向时序边实现全局关系化修正，在 Argoverse2/WOMD 上稳定提升冻结提案并取得 SOTA 级 minADE/minFDE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精化器要么压缩整条提案、要么按片段顺序更新，导致完整未来轨迹无法作为显式关系上下文参与修正；同时不同预测骨干结构差异大，缺少统一的精化接口。

Method: 把每条多模态提案表示为异质未来图：空间锚定的时序节点与智能体历史、地图元素、备选模态以及联合预测下其他智能体未来相连；双向时序边使后段证据可在单次前向中修正前段；更新后的节点回归坐标与置信度残差。仅需提案、分数与场景上下文，可即插即用于任意冻结骨干。

Result: 在 Argoverse2 和 WOMD 上一致提升冻结提案，边际与联合预测均优于原生及专用精化器；WOMD 榜单标准输入方法中 minADE 0.5049m、minFDE 0.9942m 排名第一，Soft mAP 0.4874，且不使用 LiDAR 与集成。

Conclusion: 以图结构显式承载完整未来上下文，为多模态与联合运动预测提供通用、轻量、可插拔的精化范式，证明"全局关系化单次修正"优于压缩或顺序式更新。

Abstract: Accurate motion prediction is critical for safe autonomous driving, yet even strong forecasters leave residual errors in their multimodal proposals. Existing refiners compress complete proposals or update their segments sequentially, preventing the full proposed future from serving as explicit relational context for correction. We introduce FutureLift, a backbone-agnostic refiner that represents each multimodal proposal as a heterogeneous future graph, with spatially grounded temporal nodes connected to agent histories, map elements, alternative modes, and, for joint prediction, other agents future trajectories. Bidirectional temporal edges allow later evidence to revise earlier segments in a single pass, and the updated nodes predict residual coordinate and confidence corrections. Requiring only proposals, scores, and scene context, FutureLift provides a common refinement interface for structurally different forecasting backbones. On Argoverse2 and the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, it consistently improves frozen proposals and outperforms native and specialized refiners across marginal and joint forecasting. On the WOMD motion-prediction leaderboard, FutureLift ranks first in minADE (0.5049m) and minFDE (0.9942m) among the compared standard-input methods, without LiDAR or ensembling, while achieving a competitive 0.4874 Soft mAP.

</details>


### [109] [Skill-Decoupled On-Policy Expert Distillation for Unified Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22217)
*Yuqi Xiong,Chunyi Peng,Zhipeng Xu,Qing Yang,Zhenghao Liu,Zulong Chen,Yu Gu,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SCOPED，通过训练能力专精的专家模型并经奖励引导反思提取显式技能，再进行在策略蒸馏，使统一学生模型在无显式技能推理的条件下内化多能力，在八个多模态基准上优于骨干与混合能力RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型中的强化学习可激发专门视觉能力，但异构目标联合优化会产生跨能力的梯度冲突；独立训练专家虽可缓解干扰，却易将专门知识与奖励劫持偏差纠缠在一起。因此需要一种既能避免梯度冲突、又能避免奖励偏差污染的知识整合机制。

Method: SCOPED 分三步：（1）为各能力独立训练专家模型以规避跨能力梯度冲突；（2）通过奖励引导的反思从专家中抽取显式技能；（3）在策略蒸馏中，用这些技能指导专家监督，但学生模型在推理时不接触显式技能，从而内化程序性知识。核心在于技能解耦与推理期技能移除。

Result: 在八个多模态基准上，SCOPED 稳定优于骨干模型与混合能力 RL 基线。分析进一步揭示了联合优化中的能力干扰，并表明技能增强能促进更一致的专家轨迹、更强的任务相关视觉证据注意力，以及统一学生模型中对专门能力的更好保留。

Conclusion: 技能解耦的在策略专家蒸馏是一种有效的多能力整合范式，可在避免梯度冲突与奖励偏差的同时，将独立获得的专门能力迁移至统一模型，且推理阶段无需显式技能输入。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can elicit specialized visual capabilities in vision-language models (VLMs), yet jointly optimizing heterogeneous objectives often introduces cross-capability gradient conflicts. Training experts independently mitigates this interference, but direct reward optimization may entangle specialized knowledge with reward-hacking biases. This paper proposes Skill-Decoupled On-Policy Expert Distillation (SCOPED), which trains capability-specific experts and extracts explicit skills through reward-guided reflection. During on-policy distillation, these skills guide expert supervision while remaining unavailable to the unified student, encouraging it to internalize procedural knowledge without explicit skills at inference time. Across eight multimodal benchmarks, SCOPED consistently outperforms the backbone and mixed-capability RL, demonstrating the effectiveness of skill-decoupled on-policy expert distillation for integrating independently acquired capabilities. Further analyses reveal interference during joint capability optimization and suggest that skill augmentation promotes more consistent expert trajectories, greater attention to task-relevant visual evidence, and better preservation of specialized expertise in the unified student. All code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [110] [How Much Physics Can Vision Learn from Audio? Probing and Distilling Damping Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22221)
*Zainab Afolabi,Vadim Selyutin,Zhao Gao,Babs Khalidson,Mohamed Ghoneim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用音频编码的阻尼/品质因子Q作为监督信号，通过将冻结的音频教师蒸馏到冻结的CLIP视觉骨干（训练投影头），提升视觉模型对材料物理属性的感知能力，在留一材料协议下四帧输入将相关系数从0.234提升至0.338。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理理解（如阻尼）对决策至关重要，但视觉模型难以从图像推断材料的动态物理属性；而音频在撞击响应中直接编码了阻尼信息（可通过衰减测量）。作者希望探明该物理信号的来源，并评估视觉能从中恢复多少。

Method: 用Spearman秩相关ρ评估冻结编码器对品质因子Q的排序能力；以冻结音频编码器为教师，通过训练投影头蒸馏到冻结CLIP视觉骨干；采用留一材料协议，将留出材料同时从投影头与探针中排除；并对比单帧与跨撞击时刻四帧视觉输入。

Result: 音频嵌入达到ρ=0.73，且在未见材料上保留大部分优势；单帧CLIP仅ρ=0.28。蒸馏可恢复部分音频优势。留一材料协议下，四帧输入蒸馏将相关系数从0.234提升至0.338（相对匹配四帧基线+0.053，17种材料中14种提升）。扩大视觉输入至四帧即可获得其中约一半改进且无音频代价，说明单帧遗漏的部分是可见动态。

Conclusion: 音频在阻尼等材料物理属性上提供强信号，视觉可经跨模态蒸馏部分恢复该能力；时间动态（多帧）是视觉获取此类物理属性信息的重要补充，仅靠类别级策略无法解释该增益。

Abstract: Physical understanding is crucial for decision making, yet vision models struggle to infer properties such as damping that govern how materials behave. Audio encodes such properties directly, because an object's acoustic response to impact reveals its damping through measurable decay. We probe frozen encoders for the quality factor $Q$, a dimensionless measure of how many oscillations an object completes before its ring dies away, and report how well each embedding orders impacts by $Q$ using the Spearman rank correlation $\rho$, for which $\rho=1$ is a perfect ordering and $\rho=0$ an unrelated one. Audio embeddings reach $\rho=0.73$ and retain most of that advantage on materials withheld from training, while a single video frame taken at the moment of impact, encoded by CLIP, supports only $\rho=0.28$. Having established where the signal lives, we ask how much of that gap vision can recover. We distill a frozen audio teacher into a frozen CLIP backbone through a trained projection head and find that this recovers a useful fraction of the audio advantage. Because pooled correlations on this task are dominated in practice by category identity, we also evaluate under a leave-one-material-out protocol in which the held-out material is withheld from the projection head as well as the probe. Distillation with a four-frame input improves the correlation there from 0.234 to 0.338 (+0.053 over the matched four-frame baseline, 14 of 17 materials), a gain that no category-level strategy can produce. Widening the visual input to four frames spanning the impact supplies half of that improvement at no acoustic cost, which indicates that part of what a single frame misses is visible dynamics.

</details>


### [111] [WaveLLM: Wavelet-Domain LLM Entropy Modeling for Lossless Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22232)
*Yue Yu,Bowei Kang,Changsheng Gao,Dong Liu,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WaveLLM，将图像先做多级小波子带分解，再用LLM（Llama-3.2-3B+LoRA）自回归建模各子带系数，配合数值接口（值嵌入+系数预测头）与任务自适应提示，实现无损图像压缩的熵建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based无损压缩主要强调LLM建模能力，却直接把图像序列化为像素或残差序列，导致异构信号模式纠缠在单一预测流中。作者认为有效熵建模不仅取决于模型容量，还取决于输入表示的可预测性。

Method: 1）小波域分解：将图像拆分为多级小波子带，把异构预测问题转化为多个更结构化、统计更同质的子问题；2）数值接口：值嵌入（value embedding）+系数预测头，衔接数值编码符号与LLM文本token空间；3）任务自适应提示策略，使单一共享模型统一适配不同子带；4）基于Llama-3.2-3B做LoRA微调。

Result: 在Kodak、Tecnick（原文链接缺失）、DIV2K上分别达到2.51、2.24、2.27 bpsp；相比使用相同小波变换的PixelCNN编解码器分别提升0.43、0.36、0.46 bpsp，并在三个基准上均超过近期LLM-based无损压缩方法。

Conclusion: 输入表示的可预测性与模型容量同等重要；小波子带分解+LLM熵建模能显著改进无损图像压缩，且统一模型可适配多子带。

Abstract: Lossless image compression fundamentally relies on accurate entropy modeling, driving a recent shift toward large language model (LLM)-based methods. However, existing approaches mainly emphasize the modeling capacity of LLMs while directly serializing images into pixel or residual sequences, leaving heterogeneous signal patterns entangled within a single prediction stream. In this paper, we argue that effective entropy modeling depends not only on model capacity, but also on the predictability of the input representation. Motivated by this insight, we propose WaveLLM, a wavelet-domain LLM-based entropy model for lossless image compression. By decomposing an image into multi-level wavelet subbands, WaveLLM converts a heterogeneous prediction problem into a set of more structured and statistically homogeneous subproblems that are more amenable to autoregressive probability estimation. To bridge numerical coding symbols with the LLM's native text token space, we introduce a dedicated numerical interface featuring value embeddings and a coefficient prediction head. Furthermore, a task-adaptive prompting strategy is designed to enable a single shared model to adapt to diverse subbands within a unified framework. Built on Llama-3.2-3B with LoRA adaptation, WaveLLM achieves 2.51, 2.24, and 2.27 bpsp on Kodak, this http URL , and DIV2K, respectively, outperforming the PixelCNN-based codec using the same wavelet transform by 0.43, 0.36, and 0.46 bpsp, and surpassing recent LLM-based lossless compressors on all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [112] [MMSearch-OPD: Easy Made Hard, Hard Made Learnable for Long-Horizon Multimodal Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22240)
*Huanyao Zhang,Jiepeng Zhou,Jiaye Lin,Ontai Tang,Jingtian Hao,Mugeng Liu,Ru Peng,Lang Mei,Xiaohan Yu,Liyan Liu,Chong Chen,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MMSearch-OPD，通过可控数据精炼 + 强化学习与在线策略蒸馏联合训练，提升多模态长程深度搜索智能体能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态深度搜索正从短查询转向长程、持续获取证据的调查型任务，但训练面临两大瓶颈：缺乏高难度训练问题，以及过程级反馈不足。

Method: Refine 数据管线：对已有简单多模态问答的推理链做定向扩展，通过参数化变换控制搜索深度与跨模态证据需求，复用原图-问对。训练端：用答案奖励与轻量行为惩罚驱动 RL，并以奖励差距调制 token 级教师指导（作用于不完美学生轨迹）；蒸馏系数指数衰减，训练后期降低对教师的依赖。SFT 后接联合训练。

Result: 实验显示数据精炼带来增益，SFT 后的联合训练进一步提升，长程多模态深度搜索能力增强。

Conclusion: 数据精炼与 RL+OPD 联合训练可有效缓解训练数据与过程反馈的约束，是提升长程多模态搜索智能体的可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal deep search is moving from short queries to long-horizon investigations that continually acquire evidence. However, training such agents remains constrained by limited challenging training questions and insufficient process feedback. We introduce MMSearch-OPD, combining controllable data refinement with joint reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). Our Refine pipeline performs targeted expansion of reasoning chains derived from existing simple multimodal questions. Parameterized transformations control search depth and cross-modal evidence requirements while reusing existing image--question pairs. For training, answer rewards and lightweight behavior penalties guide RL, while the reward gap modulates token-level teacher guidance on imperfect student trajectories. An exponentially decaying distillation coefficient reduces teacher dependence as training proceeds. Experiments show gains from data refinement and further improvements from joint training after SFT, indicating stronger long-horizon multimodal deep search capabilities.

</details>


### [113] [FPG-Seg: Annotation-free Retinal Vessel Topology Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22259)
*James Willoughby,Irina Voiculescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需人工标注的视网膜血管分割管线FPG-Seg，融合血管增强滤波、贝叶斯深度学习与拓扑感知图重建，在四个基准上达到全监督基线86–98%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜血管分割依赖耗时且需临床专家参与的精细标注；作者希望构建完全无标注（含超参调优）的分割方案，以降低标注成本。

Method: 管线组合三部分：经典vesselness滤波（提供先验血管响应）、贝叶斯深度学习（不确定性驱动的伪标签/训练）、拓扑感知图重建（保证血管连通性）；训练与调参均不使用人工标注。

Result: 在四个常用视网膜血管分割基准上clDice为0.74–0.78，达等价全监督基线的86–98%；并首次给出无标注血管分割方法在FIVES上的性能基准。

Conclusion: 无标注管线可在拓扑指标上接近全监督水平，但绝对性能仍有差距；该方法属医学影像领域，未涉及机器人或具身智能任务。

Abstract: State-of-the art approaches to retinal vessel segmentation typically require extensive annotations, a highly time-consuming process, reliant on clinical expertise. We present FPG-Seg, an annotation-free pipeline for retinal vessel segmentation combining classical vesselness filtering, Bayesian deep learning and topology-aware graph reconstruction to segment retinal vessel topology without manual labels. Tested across four widely used benchmark datasets for retinal vessel segmentation, FPG-Seg attains a clDice of $0.74{-}0.78$, representing $86{-}98\%$ of the performance of an equivalent fully-supervised baseline, despite using no manual annotations for training or hyperparameter tuning. As part of this work, we also provide, to our knowledge, the first performance benchmark of an annotation-free vessel segmentation method on FIVES.

</details>


### [114] [SCOPE: Semantic Bounded Credit Optimization for Prompt Engineering in Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22264)
*Yufei Jiao,Jinfa Huang,Zeyue Xue,Tianyu Xie,Yuexiao Ma,Rongrong Ji,Xiawu Zheng,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPE: 一种基于GRPO的强化学习目标，通过对提示词重写器生成的语义分段进行组内对数概率池化，为每个语义片段分配共享更新系数，提升T2I生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的T2I提示词重写方法，通常将源自图像的单一日标信号直接应用到各个子词token上，但物体、数量、属性、关系等实际构成连贯的语义片段（span），这种token级与语义级不匹配削弱了信用分配的合理性。

Method: 提出SCOPE目标（基于GRPO）。重写器联合生成增强提示词及其语义划分；SCOPE在每个语义span内对策略对数概率的变化量进行池化，得到该span所有token损失的共享系数，同时为每个completion保留单一advantage。无需额外的span奖励，也无需额外图像渲染。

Result: 在Qwen-Image、SD3、FLUX.1-dev上，相比vanilla GRPO，GeneVal平均相对提升9.48%，T2I-CompBench提升7.62%。消融实验表明语义边界与span内池化均对增益有贡献。

Conclusion: 语义结构可作为强化学习提示词工程中一种有效的归纳偏置，在不增加额外奖励或渲染开销的前提下提升T2I生成。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables language models to improve text-to-image (T2I) generation by rewriting prompts for a frozen image generator. Existing methods typically apply a single signal derived from the image to individual subword tokens, although objects, counts, attributes, and relations form coherent semantic spans. We propose SCOPE, an objective based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) that aligns update weighting with this structure. The rewriter jointly generates an enhanced prompt and its semantic partition. SCOPE pools shifts in policy log probabilities within each span to derive a shared coefficient for its token losses while retaining a single advantage for each completion. It requires neither separate span rewards nor additional image renders. Across Qwen-Image, SD3, and FLUX.1-dev, SCOPE achieves average relative gains over vanilla GRPO of 9.48% on GeneVal and 7.62% on T2I-CompBench. Ablation studies show that both semantic boundaries and pooling within spans contribute to these gains, supporting semantic structure as a practical inductive bias for prompt engineering with RL.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Pairwise Repulsion: Collective Diversity via Determinant Maximization in Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22267)
*Kwanyoung Lee,SeungJu Cha,Yebin Ahn,Sungho Koh,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DetNO，通过在初始噪声上最大化RBF Gram矩阵的log-determinant（即批次体积/RKHS体积），实现T2I扩散模型批次级多样性优化，无需训练或修改采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引导的T2I扩散模型会把独立采样的噪声收敛到近乎相同的视觉原型，损害需要分布覆盖的任务（尤其是合成数据增强）。多样性是批次的集体属性，现有成对噪声优化与排斥目标无法应对批次坍缩到低维子空间的高阶失效模式。

Method: 将多样化生成形式化为初始噪声上的RKHS体积最大化：对一步Tweedie预测构建RBF Gram矩阵并最大化其log-determinant，该集合级泛函等于批次体积平方，坍缩时排斥最强、多样后自衰减；加入基于anchor的保真项，通过拉格朗日松弛得到仅含单一超参数λ的两项目标，无需训练或改动采样器。

Result: 在SD1.5、SDXL、SD3三个骨干上于VendiScore与Mean Self-Similarity上取得领先的批内多样性；少样本分类下游实验显示更宽的模式覆盖带来统计显著的精度提升。固定流程仅更换批次目标时，两种RBF聚合器均优于InfoNCE，且log-determinant在两个基准上取得最佳下游精度，其相对InfoNCE的多样性优势随批次增大而扩大。

Conclusion: 集合级（行列式/体积）目标比成对式目标更能有效防止批次多样性坍缩，且可直接作为即插即用模块提升合成数据增强的下游效用。

Abstract: Strong guidance in text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models concentrates sampling toward high-density modes of the conditional distribution, collapsing independently sampled noise vectors onto nearly identical visual prototypes. This limits utility for tasks that demand distributional breadth, most critically synthetic data augmentation. Diversity, however, is a collective property of the whole batch: a set can preserve every pairwise distance yet still collapse onto a low-dimensional subspace. Existing sum-of-pairwise noise-optimization and repulsion objectives respond only weakly to this higher-order failure. We propose DetNO (Determinant Noise Optimization), which formulates diverse generation as RKHS volume maximization on the initial noise, maximizing the log-determinant of an RBF Gram matrix over one-step Tweedie predictions. This set-level functional equals the squared batch volume; its repulsion is strongest when the batch is most collapsed and self-attenuates as diversity is achieved. An anchor-based fidelity term keeps the optimized noises near plausible regions, and Lagrangian relaxation gives a two-term objective with a single hyperparameter $\lambda$ governing the diversity-quality trade-off, without training or sampler modification. Across three backbones (SD1.5, SDXL, SD3), DetNO achieves leading intra-batch diversity in VendiScore and Mean Self-Similarity, and a downstream few-shot classification experiment confirms that broader mode coverage brings statistically significant accuracy gains for synthetic data augmentation in the low-shot regime. Fixing the pipeline and varying only the batch objective, both RBF-based aggregators outperform InfoNCE, and the log-determinant attains the best downstream accuracy on both benchmarks. Its diversity advantage over InfoNCE widens as the batch grows. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [116] [Unsupervised Clustering of Public Transport Vehicles Using Deep Morphological Features and Color Histograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22270)
*Juan Irving Vasquez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 使用冻结DINOv2形态特征与手工HSV颜色直方图加权早融合，配合UMAP与K-Means，在墨西哥城约1460小时监控视频中无监督聚类公交车辆，分离不同线路与车型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市道路监控摄像头积累大量无标注视频，可用于公共交通监测；但不同线路公交车身结构相似、主要差别在涂装配色，人工标注代价高，缺乏有效的无监督车辆分类手段。

Method: 以冻结DINOv2 ViT提取形态特征，与手工HSV颜色直方图按权重早融合（65%形态+35%颜色），再经UMAP降维与K-Means聚类，构建无监督公交聚类流水线。

Result: 在7,972张公交图像上得到14个聚类，可区分不同线路与车型；相比仅用形态特征，目标簇（RTP公交网络）精确度提升，表明颜色特征有效补充自监督表征，但不占主导。

Conclusion: 颜色信息在不过度主导表征的前提下，能补充自监督视觉特征用于线路级公交聚类；该流程为无标注监控视频的公共交通监测提供可行方案。

Abstract: Surveillance cameras along urban roads produce large volumes of unlabeled video that could support the monitoring of public transport, but grouping the observed vehicles into meaningful categories without manual annotation remains challenging, since buses from different routes often share the same body structure and differ mainly in their color schemes. This study presents an unsupervised clustering methodology for public transport vehicles that combines morphological features extracted with a frozen DINOv2 Vision Transformer and handcrafted HSV color histograms through weighted early fusion, followed by UMAP projection and K-Means clustering. The method was evaluated on 7,972 bus images extracted from about 1,460 hours of video of an arterial road in Mexico City, with the goal of isolating the buses of the city's public bus network (RTP) without supervision. With a fusion of 65\% morphological and 35\% color features, the pipeline produced 14 clusters that separate distinct routes and vehicle types, and it increased the precision of the target cluster with respect to morphological features alone. These results show that color complements self-supervised features for route-level clustering, provided that it does not dominate the representation.

</details>


### [117] [MJev-Spatial: System One Spatial Intelligence via Native Attention Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22276)
*Luozheng Qin,Yuhan Zhu,Changhai Zhou,Qian Qiao,Jun Gao,Yuhua Zhou,Harry Zhang,Xiangyu Zeng,Yuxuan Liu,Kaijie Chen,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MJev-Spatial 将三类空间理解任务（关系选择、距离回归、2D 点预测）统一为分类问题，用 VLM 主干预训练 Q/K 投影直接打分候选类，单次前向、零解码 token，在 12 个基准上综合得分第一并比最快自回归基线快 2.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间智能中的判断（空间关系、距离、放置位置）需要在交互速率下完成，但输出空间很小（几个选项、一个标量、一个 2D 点）。VLM 却用自由文本生成来做这些事，付出了自回归解码和答案解析的代价，效率与任务结构严重不匹配。作者希望用 System 1 式的一次前向分类替代生成式回答。

Method: 提出 MJev-Spatial：(1) 将每个候选视为一个类别，直接用主干预训练 query/key 投影对候选打分，不新增参数；(2) 选择任务候选为给定选项，数值与点预测则分别通过粗到细分类对数值分箱与图像区域逐步细化；(3) 推理时每次分类仅一次前向、不生成任何答案 token；(4) 训练上用 teacher forcing 在单次前向中联合监督所有粗到细阶段，并配合长度感知全局批处理与跨 GPU 负载均衡，训练吞吐提升 30.8%。基于 Qwen3.5-4B，在 12.3M 由现有空间数据集转换的问答对上训练。

Result: 在八个被测模型中取得最高综合得分 65.64%，在 12 个基准中的 7 个排名第一，在全部 12 个基准上均优于未修改的自回归基座模型；推理速度比最快的自回归基线快 2.5 倍，比基座模型快 5.6 倍。

Conclusion: 将紧凑输出空间的空间理解任务重构为统一分类范式，可在不增加参数的前提下同时提升精度与推理效率，说明"判别式打分 + 粗到细分类"是空间决策任务上优于自由文本生成的有效路线；但工作偏基准评测与模型效率，尚未涉及机器人实机部署或具身闭环验证。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence lets an agent perceive and act in the physical world, and many of its judgments must be made at interaction rates: which spatial relation holds, how far an object is, or where to place it. Vision-language models (VLMs) produce these answers by free-form text generation, paying for autoregressive decoding and answer parsing even though the output space is compact: one of a few options, a scalar, or a 2D point. In light of this, we introduce MJev-Spatial, a System 1 multimodal decision model that tackles all three types of spatial understanding tasks within a unified classification framework. Specifically, MJev-Spatial treats each candidate as a class and scores it using the backbone's pretrained query and key projections, without adding parameters. The candidates are the given options for choice tasks, or value bins and image regions refined through coarse-to-fine classification for numerical and point prediction, respectively. At inference, each classification decision takes a single forward pass and generates no answer tokens. To train on heterogeneous spatial data efficiently, we supervise all coarse-to-fine stages jointly with teacher forcing in one forward pass, and combine this with length-aware global batching and cross-GPU load balancing, raising training throughput by 30.8% over the original batching strategy. Built on Qwen3.5-4B and trained on 12.3M question-answer pairs converted from existing spatial datasets, MJev-Spatial achieves the highest overall score (65.64%) among eight tested models, ranks first on seven of twelve benchmarks, and outperforms its unmodified autoregressive base model on all twelve, while running 2.5 times faster than the fastest autoregressive baseline and 5.6 times faster than the base model.

</details>


### [118] [Routing Policy Optimization for Online Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22282)
*Qiuhong Shen,Yiding Yang,Yuxin Zhang,Xinwei Huang,Minxuan Lin,Bo Liu,Hao Kang,Haotian Yang,Yizhi Wang,Chongyang Ma,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Routing Policy Optimization (RPO)，通过将注意力路由变为随机探索动作，在保留原始确定性采样器的前提下用得分函数目标对齐视觉生成器，训练效率较 Flow-GRPO 类方法提升近 4 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有得分函数方法的随机性来自对反向去噪轨迹的扰动，需要昂贵的 rollout 优化。作者希望在不改变生成器原始采样器的前提下，把随机探索引入生成器内部。

Method: 在若干选定去噪层级，每个 query block 保留自身 block 并按生成器原生注意力质量采样额外 key block，或保持稠密注意力；采样路由扰动该过渡的 velocity，用围绕稠密 velocity 的高斯似然以得分函数目标给扰动打分；再用受控双 rollout 估计器（共享 prompt、初始潜变量、路由层级与稠密锚层，仅路由不同）以奖励差作为优势信号。

Result: 在文本到图像与文本到视频生成上，RPO 提升生成质量，同时训练效率比 Flow-GRPO 风格方法高近 4 倍；推理时恢复稠密注意力并保留原始确定性采样器。

Conclusion: 将注意力路由作为随机探索动作，可在保持原有采样器的同时实现高效、有效的 RL 对齐，且路由移除后学到的更新仍然有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can align visual generators with reward signals beyond their pretraining objectives, but existing score-function methods typically introduce stochasticity by perturbing the reverse denoising trajectory, leading to costly rollout-based optimization. We instead ask whether stochastic exploration can be introduced inside the generator while preserving its original sampler. Routing Policy Optimization (RPO) answers this question by turning attention routing into a stochastic exploration action. At a few selected denoising levels, each query block keeps its own block and samples further key blocks in proportion to the generator's native attention mass, or keeps dense attention. The sampled route perturbs the velocity of that transition, and a Gaussian likelihood around the dense velocity scores the perturbation with a score-function objective. RPO further uses a controlled two-rollout estimator: two rollouts share the same prompt, initial latent, routed denoising levels, and dense anchor layers, differing only in their sampled attention routes. Their reward difference provides an advantage signal for the routed transitions. Because the likelihood differentiates the dense velocity itself, the learned updates remain effective after routing is removed. At inference, RPO restores dense attention and retains the original deterministic sampler. Experiments on text-to-image and text-to-video generation show that RPO improves generation quality while achieving nearly 4x higher training efficiency than Flow-GRPO-style methods. Please see our project page at this https URL for visual results.

</details>


### [119] [Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22295)
*Jay N. Paranjape,Jurijs Nazarovs,Rohan Sarkar,Yue Guo,Lipeng Ke,Sankar Venkataraman,Vishal M. Patel,Nataraj Jammalamadaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GHADAR新任务（基于人物位置的开放集人物特征与活动描述识别），构建基于AVA-Actions的数据集，通过在VLM训练中约束交叉注意力掩码提升性能，并提出基于VLM的整体评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多人场景理解数据集将问题简化为接地组活动识别、单人活动识别或闭集活动识别，限制了模型泛化能力，无法满足真实场景中对每个人特征与活动的开放集描述需求。

Method: 定义Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition任务；从AVA-Actions派生新数据集，生成人物描述与活动的开放集caption；在VLM训练中约束cross-attention mask以有效利用grounding信息；提出基于VLM的整体评估schema，直接在视频与预测间比较概念。

Result: 所提方法在该任务上优于SOTA VLM；实验表明现有评估指标过度依赖人工标注和精确文本匹配，存在局限；基于VLM的评估schema提供了更贴近视频内容的度量。

Conclusion: 工作构建了GHADAR的完整框架（数据集、方法、评估schema），为该方向的后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Understanding characteristics and activities of individuals in complex multi-person environments is crucial for real-world applications. However, existing datasets often simplify this problem to grounded group activity recognition, single-person activity recognition, or close-set activity recognition, thus limiting the generalizability of models trained on these datasets. In this work, we introduce the task of Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition (GHADAR), which involves describing the characteristics and activity descriptions of \textbf{every} person in a video, provided the location of the person in the video, which is more practical for real-world applications. To facilitate this, we introduce a new dataset derived from AVA-Actions by generating open-set captions for the person description and activity. In addition, we propose a novel method to effectively utilize the information contained in grounding during training by constraining the cross-attention masks during training in VLMs to improve performance for this task. Our experiments show that our method outperforms SOTA VLMs on this task. Finally, we demonstrate the limitations of existing evaluation metrics, which are overly reliant on human-annotations and exact text-text matching. As an added video-based evaluation, we propose a holistic VLM-based evaluation schema that compares concepts {\em directly} between the video and the generated predictions. Thus, in this work, we develop a complete framework for GHADAR, including a dataset, a novel method and an evaluation schema, thereby establishing a strong foundation for future research in this domain.

</details>


### [120] [ARC-SORT: Adaptive, Robust, Compensated Multi-Object Tracking with Interacting Multiple Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22306)
*Girish Kumar Adari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ARC-SORT：一种免训练、实时的跟踪-检测式多目标跟踪器，用 IMM 卡尔曼滤波的预测协方差作为逐轨迹搜索区域大小（不确定性自适应 EIoU），在交叉后依据代价与外观证据进行一次纠错身份交换，掉帧场景离线重识别放弃运动门控仅用外观；SportsMOT 测试集 81.82 HOTA、DanceTrack 62.1 HOTA，约 50 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育转播中的多目标跟踪存在两个难点：运动员快速移动且急变向，同时摄像机持续平移与变焦。现有 SORT 类跟踪器多用固定或硬阈值门控（如 Mahalanobis 门限）处理关联，无法适应运动预测可靠性的动态变化；轨迹交叉时的错误关联也缺少可控的纠正机制；运动员长时间出画时运动外推失效。

Method: 设计原则是"跟踪器度量自身不确定性，并由该度量决定其行为"。用 IMM 卡尔曼滤波为每条轨迹给出预测协方差，并将其转化为逐轨迹搜索区域：运动预测越不可靠，允许匹配的检测区域越大（不确定性自适应 Expansion IoU，EIoU）。关联代价本身用于报告交叉决策是否接近，对这类轨迹对保留复核，在交叉后外观证据表明首次决策有误时最多执行一次身份交换纠正。对长时间出画的运动员，离线重识别放弃常规运动门控，仅按外观连接 tracklet。全程无训练、实时运行。

Result: 在相同输入下，连续化的协方差耦合是关键：把同一协方差用作硬 Mahalanobis 门控在所扫描的每个阈值上都更差，更丰富的协方差汇总也无可见增益。交叉后纠错在 SportsMOT 与 DanceTrack 测试集各带来 +1.0 HOTA，代价低于每帧 1 ms。SportsMOT 测试集达 81.82 HOTA（截至 2026 年 8 月 69 个榜单条目中身份切换最少），MOTA 97.09 并列第三；DanceTrack 测试集 62.1 HOTA，且使用相同关联设置。在 Deep-EIoU 的公开检测与嵌入上达 80.31 HOTA，较 Deep-EIoU 提升 +3.1。笔记本 RTX 3060 上约 50 FPS。

Conclusion: ARC-SORT 表明，将跟踪器自身的预测不确定性作为连续、逐轨迹的关联区域控制信号，比固定阈值门控更有效；结合交叉后的保守身份纠错与外观主导的离线重识别，可在体育与舞蹈等强运动、强相机运动场景中取得领先精度并保持实时性。方法免训练、无需额外模型，易于嵌入现有跟踪-检测流程。代码将开源。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking in sports broadcasts is hard for two reasons: athletes move fast and change direction abruptly, and the camera pans and zooms at the same time. We present ARC-SORT (Adaptive, Robust, Compensated SORT), a tracking-by-detection tracker that trains nothing and runs in real time. Its design rule: the tracker measures its own uncertainty, and that measurement decides how it acts. An Interacting Multiple Model (IMM) Kalman filter gives every track a predictive covariance, and we turn it into a per-track search region: the less reliable the motion prediction, the larger the region in which a detection may match (uncertainty-adaptive Expansion IoU, EIoU). On identical inputs, this continuous coupling is what matters: the same covariance used as a hard Mahalanobis gate hurts at every threshold we swept, and richer covariance summaries add nothing measurable. When two tracks cross, the assignment cost itself reports whether the decision was close. ARC-SORT keeps such pairs under review and applies at most one corrective identity swap once post-crossing appearance evidence shows the first decision was wrong; this adds +1.0 HOTA (higher order tracking accuracy) on both the SportsMOT and DanceTrack test sets at under a millisecond per frame. For players who leave the frame for seconds, motion extrapolation carries no information, so our offline re-identification drops the usual motion gate and links tracklets by appearance alone. ARC-SORT reaches 81.82 HOTA on the SportsMOT test set with the fewest identity switches of the 69 leaderboard entries as of August 2026 and tied-third multi-object tracking accuracy (MOTA, 97.09), and 62.1 HOTA on DanceTrack test with the same association settings. On Deep-EIoU's public detections and embeddings it reaches 80.31 HOTA, +3.1 over Deep-EIoU. It runs at about 50 frames per second on a laptop RTX 3060 GPU. Code will be released.

</details>


### [121] [Velox: Spatial Memory Makes World Models Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22311)
*Qiuhong Shen,Shizun Wang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Velox 是一种免训练的区域级注意力路由器，利用流式几何中的视锥板块对应关系选择历史上下文，在分块视频扩散世界模型中实现随 rollout 长度近似恒定的注意力开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型将动作与相机运动转化为长时视觉 rollout，作为交互式仿真接口需要响应式生成；但分块视频扩散模型通常把历史上下文存入持续增长的 KV 缓存，导致后续 chunk 越来越慢，成为长 rollout 的核心效率瓶颈。

Method: 把历史 token 块表示为带深度区间的相机视锥板块，通过"新近优先多 warp"获得当前块的深度；对覆盖良好的区域，用视锥板块重叠度软性调制每层模型导出的块重要性，未覆盖区域保留模型原路由不加几何偏置；固定 token 块粒度对齐 FlashAttention 式分块执行，且视锥板块构建与去噪在同一 H200 上异步并行。

Result: 在第 48 个 chunk 处，注意力加速 4.36×，去噪加速 2.43×；在完整 158 例 WBench 导航划分上，与稠密 LingBot-Fast-Cam 的平均差距控制在 0.15 分以内（API-free 指标）。

Conclusion: 几何感知的区域级上下文选择可在几乎不损失生成质量的前提下，把视频世界模型的注意力开销从线性增长转为近似恒定，为长时交互式仿真提供了实用的免训练加速路径。

Abstract: Video world models turn actions and camera motion into long visual rollouts, making video a natural interface for interactive simulation. Such interaction requires responsive generation, yet coherent rollouts must retain visual memory of what has already been generated. Chunked video diffusion models usually keep this context in growing KV caches, so later chunks become progressively slower. To address this bottleneck, we introduce Velox, a training-free attention router for region-level context selection using frustum slab correspondences from streaming geometry. Velox represents historical blocks as camera frustum slabs with depth intervals and obtains current-block depth through recency-priority multiwarp. For well-covered regions, frustum slab overlap softly modulates each layer's model-derived block importance. Uncovered regions retain the model-derived route without geometric bias. The fixed token-block granularity matches FlashAttention-style tiled execution and reduces the attention term that grows with rollout length. Frustum slab construction runs asynchronously with denoising on the same H200. At chunk 48, Velox accelerates attention by $4.36\times$ and denoising by $2.43\times$. On the full 158-case WBench navigation split, it remains within 0.15 API-free average points of dense LingBot-Fast-Cam. Please see our project page at this https URL for more details.

</details>


### [122] [Not Just a Subject: Capturing Adaptive Scene-Specific Variance for Visual Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22320)
*SeungJu Cha,Ye-Chan Kim,Kwanyoung Lee,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EOS-Story：免训练框架，通过文本空间 Cross-Contextual Bridging 与视觉空间 Adaptive Identity Preservation，协调身份一致性与场景自适应，用于视觉叙事的多图生成，并扩展 Consistory+_+ 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉叙事（text-to-image 序列生成）方法过度聚焦人物/主体身份保持，难以适应多样场景变化，导致语义错位与结构僵硬（身份 token 被反复迁移产生结构伪影）。

Method: 免训练框架。(1) 文本空间：将叙事解耦为身份子提示与场景子提示以避免信息稀释，并用 Cross-Contextual Bridging 将短语级外观残差软注入场景的 <EOS> 嵌入，弥补场景缺少外观线索的问题。(2) 视觉空间：指出身份与场景主体尺度不匹配导致少数身份 token 被重复迁移，提出 Adaptive Identity Preservation，在场景特定尺度上锚定身份特征（Identity Anchoring），并通过 Identity-Aware Self-Attention 融合。

Result: 在扩展后的 Consistory+_+（含多样取景/构图场景）与 Consistory+ 上取得有竞争力或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过文本—视觉双空间的显式解耦与注意力级注入，可在无需训练的情况下更好平衡身份一致性与场景自适应，为视觉叙事生成提供了可插拔式改进思路。

Abstract: Recent advancements in diffusion models have significantly improved text-to-image generation, enabling their application to visual storytelling, where coherent image sequences are generated from narrative prompts. However, existing methods primarily focus on identity preservation and often fail to adapt to diverse scene variations, resulting in semantic misalignment and structural inflexibility. We propose EOS-Story, a training-free framework that harmonizes identity consistency with scene-specific adaptation across text and vision spaces. In the text space, we decouple the narrative into identity and scene sub-prompts to prevent information dilution, but this leaves the scene without access to appearance cues. To bridge this gap, we introduce Cross-Contextual Bridging, which softly injects a phrase-level appearance residual into the scene's <EOS> embedding. In the vision space, we find that scale mismatch between identity and scene subjects causes a few identity tokens to be repeatedly transferred, producing structural artifacts. We address this with Adaptive Identity Preservation, which anchors identity features at the scene-specific scale (Identity Anchoring) and integrates them via Identity-Aware Self-Attention. To evaluate performance under varied spatial compositions, we extend the Consistory+ benchmark with diverse framing scenarios (Consistory+_+). Extensive experiments show that EOS-Story achieves competitive or superior performance on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [123] [AVC-Reward: Event-Centric Audio-Video Consistency Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22321)
*Yuruo Wu,Kaihang Pan,Kai Shen,Jiacheng Li,Shuyuan Tu,Jisheng Dang,Li Tuoyu,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以事件为中心的音视频一致性评估框架AVC-Reward与AVC-Benchmark，实现粗到细三级一致性度量并对齐人类判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合音视频生成模型已能产生多事件组合、多层次音频混合与非对称跨模态关系等复杂场景，但现有评估沿用早期简单单向任务的指标，整段评估且依赖预定义简单关系，无法适配复杂场景。

Method: 在事件层面解耦音频与视觉事件，独立分析每个事件，跨模态交叉验证关系，再聚合为粗到细三级一致性度量（全局叙事和谐、事件级时序同步、细粒度属性对齐）；实例化为可训练的音视频奖励模型AVC-Reward，并构建AVC-Benchmark（由多种音视频生成模型产出的正负样本构成）。

Result: AVC-Reward在自动方法中与人类判断的对齐达到SOTA，可给出可解释的失败定位理由，并作为在线强化学习信号提升联合生成模型的音视频一致性。

Conclusion: 事件中心的分层一致性度量可作为通用音视频一致性评估范式，兼具奖励模型与可解释诊断能力，适用于多样生成设置与跨模态关系。

Abstract: As joint audio-video generation models become more capable, they can generate audio-visual scenes exhibiting three forms of complexity: multi-event composition, multi-layer audio mixture, and asymmetric cross-modal relations. However, current evaluations largely reuse metrics from earlier simple and unidirectional tasks. They typically evaluate an audio-video clip as a whole and rely on simple predefined relations, making them unsuitable for these increasingly complex scenes. We propose an event-centric evaluation framework that disentangles audio and visual events at the event level, analyzes each event independently, cross-validates relations across modalities, and aggregates the judgments into a coarse-to-fine, three-level consistency metric. The metric assesses global narrative harmony, event-level temporal synchronization, and fine-grained attribute alignment, providing a general formulation for audio-video consistency evaluation across diverse generation settings and cross-modal relations. We instantiate the metric as AVC-Reward, a trainable audio-video reward model. To evaluate the agreement between automatic judgments and human perception across generation paradigms and relation complexities, we construct AVC-Benchmark, which is primarily built from diverse positive and negative samples generated by various types of audio-video generation models. AVC-Reward achieves state-of-the-art alignment with human judgment among automatic methods, provides interpretable rationales that localize failures, and improves the audio-video consistency of a joint generation model when used as an online reinforcement-learning signal. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [124] [Dense4D: Pixel-Level 4D Human Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22325)
*Rıza Alp Güler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dense4D：首个无人体模板的前馈式模型，从单目RGB图像集直接回归Human Point Maps（HPM），实现跨身体、衣物、头发的稠密4D对应与3D轨迹追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D人体运动重建的难点在于：关节运动、非刚性形变与遮挡导致同一表面点难以跨帧关联。已有方法多依赖人体模板、需预测相机位姿或估计大幅场景位移，缺乏对衣物与头发等非模板区域的稠密对应能力。

Method: Dense4D以人物为中心的裁剪图连同已知的image-to-crop变换作为输入，联合预测每帧的Human Point Map与深度。HPM将每个像素映射到统一参考时刻的3D表面位置，从而支持amodal对应（参考帧中被遮挡的点可由其他帧观测定位）。跨裁剪匹配HPM坐标即可建立对应，无需预测相机位姿或场景位移；再由逐帧深度将匹配提升为3D轨迹。

Result: 在真实人物数据上，追踪精度显著优于所评估的基线，且与扫描表面的一致性更好；不同帧的点能拼接为参考姿态下连贯的人体几何，直观证明了在关节运动与衣物形变下稠密对应的一致性。论文附带定性结果、视频与交互式查看器。

Conclusion: Dense4D证明无需人体模板与相机位姿估计，即可通过前馈HPM预测实现跨身体、衣物与头发的稠密4D对应，为单目动态人体重建提供了新的表达范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing human motion in 4D requires identifying the same surface points through articulation, deformation and changes in visibility. We introduce Dense4D, to our knowledge the first feed-forward model dedicated to dense 4D correspondence across the body, clothing and hair from a set of monocular RGB images, without a body template. Trained specifically for human geometry, Dense4D jointly processes person-centred crops with known image-to-crop transforms to predict Human Point Maps (HPMs) and depth for each image. An HPM assigns each person pixel the 3D position of its observed surface point at a common reference time. These maps support amodal correspondence: points hidden in the reference image can be localised from observations in other frames. Matching HPM coordinates across crops establishes correspondence without predicting camera poses or large scene displacements; per-image depth then lifts the matches into 3D tracks. Experiments on real people demonstrate substantially higher tracking accuracy and closer agreement with scanned surfaces than the evaluated baselines. Points from different frames assemble into coherent human geometry in the reference pose, providing visible evidence of consistent dense correspondence through articulation and clothing deformation. Additional qualitative results, videos, and an interactive viewer are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [125] [ACEdit: Stable Multi-Turn Image Editing via Asymmetric Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22328)
*Sara Dorfman,Nir Goren,Gal Barak,Daniel Cohen-Or,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种训练方案，使多轮图像编辑在累积分布漂移下保持稳定：核心是让编辑器在训练时作用于自己前几轮生成的输出（Self-Forcing），并用教师编辑器蒸馏提供可靠监督信号，同时采用非对称条件——学生顺序编辑自身输出，教师锚定原始干净图像并接收复合编辑指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次图像编辑效果出色，但多轮编辑质量会持续退化。每轮编辑引入细微分布偏移，逐轮累积后，推理时的输入分布逐渐偏离编辑器训练时所见分布，造成误差累积与质量下降。

Method: 将 Self-Forcing 思想引入图像编辑：训练时强制编辑器处理自己前一轮的输出，暴露于自生成轨迹。由于自生成轨迹偏离真值轨迹，直接监督不可靠，故采用蒸馏目标，用教师编辑器在模型作用于自身输出时提供可靠训练信号。并提出非对称条件方案：学生基于自身输出顺序编辑，教师则始终锚定原始干净图像并接收复合编辑指令。

Result: 实验表明，在长多轮编辑序列中质量退化显著降低，同时保持编辑质量与指令遵循能力。

Conclusion: 通过自生成输出上的训练与非对称蒸馏监督，可有效缓解多轮图像编辑中的累积分布漂移，实现稳定多轮编辑，且不牺牲单轮编辑质量与指令遵循。

Abstract: Image editing models produce impressive results for individual edits, but their quality often deteriorates over multiple editing turns. Each editing turn introduces small distribution shifts that accumulate over successive edits, creating a gap between the input distributions encountered during the editor's original training and multi-turn inference. We introduce a training scheme that makes multi-turn image editing stable under this accumulating distribution shift. A key is to force the editor to operate on its own outputs from previous editing turns during training, bringing the principle of Self-Forcing to image editing. The main challenge is that self-generated editing trajectories deviate from ground-truth trajectories, making direct supervision unreliable. We address this challenge through a distillation objective that leverages a teacher image editor to provide a reliable training signal when the model operates on its own previous outputs. We introduce an asymmetric conditioning scheme: while the student performs edits sequentially on its own outputs, the teacher remains anchored to the original clean image and receives a compound editing instruction. Experiments show substantially reduced degradation over long multi-turn sequences while maintaining editing quality and instruction adherence.

</details>


### [126] [3DCodeVerse: Exploring 3D Worlds as Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22341)
*Yipeng Gao,Ziyao Zeng,Jinfa Huang,Yijiang Li,Haolin Xiong,Wang Qin,Xinlei Yu,Youheng Yao,Jie Zhang,Kun-Yu Lin,Rong Liu,Yinyuan Zhao,Yajie Zhao,Meiqi Guo,Jiebo Luo,Lei Shu,Yunhao Ge,Xinyu Hu,Laurent Itti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 3DCodeVerse：面向3D编码智能体的大规模可执行代码数据集（2M）、多智能体DAG生成/验证框架与系统性实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D内容生成多依赖专用表示（网格、点云、隐式场、NeRF/3DGS等），产物难以编辑、难以验证、缺乏可复现的执行环境。作者希望用"可执行3D代码"作为统一表示，让编码智能体能够规模化地生成、编辑并自动验证3D内容。

Method: (1) 构建3DCodeVerse-2M数据集：汇集开源数据、转换专用3D表示、并用编码智能体生成新程序；每个程序打包源码、依赖资产、描述与验证记录，保证在声明环境中可执行。(2) 3DCodeVerse Harness：多智能体有向无环图（DAG）框架，通过执行检查、空间度量与视觉反馈自动评估并迭代修正生成程序（无人工反馈）。(3) 系统实证：微调开源模型建立基线，并对Harness各组件做消融分析以提炼工程指导。

Result: 在数据集上微调可显著提升开源模型的3D编码能力；由Claude Opus 5.5驱动时，3DCodeVerse Harness在成对人工评测中对Claude Design（3D Object）取得90%胜率。

Conclusion: 可执行代码是3D内容生成与验证的有效统一载体；"数据+多智能体闭环验证+实证指导"的组合能显著增强智能体的3D创作能力，为可编辑、可验证的3D内容规模化生产提供基础设施。

Abstract: We introduce 3DCodeVerse, a research ecosystem that enables coding agents to create editable and verifiable 3D content at scale. 3DCodeVerse comprises large-scale, high-quality datasets of executable 3D code, a coding harness for spatial reasoning and automated verification, and practical guidance for 3D coding. First, we construct 3DCodeVerse-2M, a large-scale curated dataset of standalone 3D executable code. We build this corpus by collecting open-source data, converting specialized representations, and generating new programs with coding agents. Each program is packaged with the source files and associated assets required for execution in its declared environment, along with available descriptions and verification records. Second, we develop the 3DCodeVerse Harness, a multi-agent directed acyclic graph framework for generating and refining 3D content. It uses execution checks, spatial measurements, and visual feedback to automatically evaluate and revise generated programs without human feedback during generation. Third, we conduct systematic empirical studies of 3D coding, establishing baselines with fine-tuned open models and deriving practical guidance through component-level analyses of our harness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that fine-tuning on our dataset significantly improves the 3D coding capabilities of open-source models. Notably, our 3DCodeVerse harness achieves a 90% win rate against Claude Design (3D Object) in pairwise human evaluations, when both are utilized by Claude Opus 5.5.

</details>


### [127] [GeoState: Learning View-Decoupled Geometry States for 3D World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22343)
*Hongchang Liu,Jian Liu,Yujuan Ding,Liang Peng,Xun Jiang,Fei Ma,Xing Xu,Yi Bin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoState 将几何基础模型特征解耦为视角无关的"场景状态"与相机相关观测，用自回归世界模型预测未来场景状态，相机运动单独建模，从而支持任意指定相机下的解码并在 Waymo/KITTI/nuScenes 上取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何基础模型（GFM）特征虽含丰富几何先验，但具有视角依赖性，导致场景动态与相机引起的观测变化纠缠，使世界模型的预测状态演化更难学习。需要一种解耦表示，把场景状态演化与相机观测分离。

Method: 提出 GeoState：场景分词器将多视角 GFM 特征聚合为视角无关的场景状态（scene state）；几何解码器通过相机射线查询该状态以生成观测；自回归世界模型预测未来场景状态，相机运动作为独立模块建模。预测状态可在预测相机或外部指定相机下解码，且整个 rollout 保持同一上下文推导的尺度，无需逐帧尺度对齐。

Result: 在 Waymo 上优于既有 3D 世界模型，支持几何一致的跨视角查询，并能零样本泛化到 KITTI 与 nuScenes。

Conclusion: 视角解耦的几何表示可作为预测式 3D 世界模型的有效状态空间，兼顾几何一致性与泛化性，为跨数据集、可控相机的世界建模提供可行路径。

Abstract: Recent 3D world models increasingly use features from geometry foundation models (GFMs) as predictive world states, benefiting from their rich geometric priors. However, the view dependence of these features entangles scene dynamics with camera-induced variation, making the resulting state evolution harder to predict. We propose GeoState, a view-decoupled geometry representation for predictive 3D world modeling that separates scene-state evolution from camera-dependent observation. Specifically, a scene tokenizer aggregates multi-view GFM features into a view-decoupled scene state, which a geometry decoder queries using camera rays. An autoregressive world model then predicts future scene states, while camera motion is modeled separately. The predicted states can therefore be decoded under either predicted or externally specified cameras, while retaining a single context-derived scale throughout the rollout without per-frame scale alignment. Experiments show that GeoState outperforms prior 3D world models on Waymo, supports geometrically consistent cross-view querying, and generalizes zero-shot to KITTI and nuScenes. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [128] [Speculative Forcing for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22347)
*Hyunwook Choi,Seungryong Kim,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Speculative Forcing 框架，将少步自回归视频生成中每个 token 的中间干净估计视为草稿，通过验证器判断其可提前"接受"并冻结为最终输出，从而跳过后续去噪步，在保持全步采样质量的同时显著降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步自回归视频生成器虽支持流式生成，但对所有 token 一律执行到最后一步去噪，计算浪费严重。作者观察到许多 token 在初始几步就已接近其最终全步输出，而仅少数 token 需要持续精修，因此统一计算并非必要。

Method: 将中间干净估计当作草稿，逐 token 预测能否安全提前接受。被接受的 token 冻结为最终输出、不再去噪，其 KV 缓存继续作为后续 token 的上下文。验证器有两种设计：(1) 免训练验证器，基于与干净内容的相似度及跨步稳定性；(2) 学习型验证器，读取冻结生成器的中间特征来估计该 token 距离全步输出的剩余差距。

Result: 在短视频、长视频与世界生成任务上，均保持全步采样质量并大幅减少计算。LongLive 上达到每 token 约 1.7 步去噪，匹配四步生成器质量，提速超 1.5 倍（41.7 / 37.8 fps）；Lingbot-World 2.0 上（相机运动持续改变可见内容）每 token 约 2.5 步，质量保持，提速 1.35 倍（24.5 fps）。

Conclusion: Token 级别的自适应提前接受可有效打破少步自回归视频生成中"统一计算"的冗余，在不损失生成质量的前提下带来稳定的推理加速，适用于短视频、长视频及交互式世界生成等流式场景。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video generators enable streaming generation, yet they run every token through the final denoising step, regardless of how much refinement it needs. We find this uniform computation is often unnecessary: many tokens already closely match their eventual full-step outputs in the initial steps, while others require refinement until the end. We introduce Speculative Forcing, a framework that treats intermediate clean estimates as drafts and predicts when individual tokens can be safely accepted early. Once accepted, a token is frozen as its final output and undergoes no further denoising, while its cached keys and values remain available as context for the remaining tokens. We explore two alternative verifier designs: a training-free one based on similarity to clean content and stability across steps, and a learned one that reads the frozen generator's intermediate features to estimate each token's remaining gap to its full-step output. Across short-video, long-video, and world generation, Speculative Forcing preserves the quality of full-step sampling while substantially reducing computation. On LongLive, it matches the four-step generator with ~1.7 denoising steps per token, running over 1.5x faster at 41.7 and 37.8 fps. On Lingbot-World 2.0, where camera motion continually changes the visible content, it maintains quality with ~2.5 denoising steps per token and runs 1.35x faster at 24.5 fps.

</details>


### [129] [SUPRA-TEX: 3D Generation with Super-Aligned Texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22352)
*Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Fanpeng Meng,Zihao Huang,Jichen Pang,Guanjun Wu,Zhikuan Bao,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SUPRA-Tex，通过解耦可见材质估计、表面对齐与观测引导的3D补全，实现单图驱动的可重光照PBR材质生成，在可见区域达到"所见即所得"保真度并保持未见区域一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图3D材质生成面临两大痛点：多视图预测不一致融合时引入伪影；隐式图像特征条件化难以保留细节。核心动机是将任务分解，使受限于3D数据稀缺的生成器专注保留可见证据并补全未观测表面。

Method: 三步解耦流程：(1) 训练基于MLLM的图像编辑模型，在参考视角预测光照分离的PBR贴图；(2) 将这些贴图投影到可见表面，用预训练材质VAE编码器编码为与生成目标同潜空间的native observation latents；(3) 采用交错flow transformer，将这些latents作为固定只读锚点，交替执行未观测区域更新与全表面更新，生成完整材质latents。

Result: 仅在公开数据集上训练，在几何条件纹理化与端到端资产生成两项任务上均达到SOTA，在in-the-wild图像上匹敌甚至超越领先商业系统。

Conclusion: 解耦式设计实现了与参考图像的"超对齐"，兼顾可见区域保真与未观测表面连贯补全，验证了将MLLM编辑能力与原生潜空间锚定结合的范式有效性。

Abstract: Single image 3D generation aims to dress a given 3D mesh with relightable materials while faithfully preserving the visual details of a reference image. Existing methods either introduce artifacts when fusing inconsistent multi-view predictions or struggle to preserve fine details through implicit image-feature conditioning. To resolve these challenges, we introduce SUPRA-Tex, built on the principle of decoupling visible-material estimation, surface alignment, and observation-guided 3D completion. This division of labor allows the 3D generator, trained on limited 3D data, to focus its capacity on preserving visible evidence and completing unobserved surfaces. Specifically, we first train an MLLM-based image editing model to predict lighting-separated PBR maps in the reference view. We then project these maps onto the visible surface and encode them with the pretrained material VAE encoder into native observation latents that share the generation target's latent space. An interleaved flow transformer holds these latents as fixed, read-only anchors while alternating unobserved-region and full-surface updates to generate complete material latents. This design achieves super-alignment with the reference, delivering What You See Is What You Get fidelity on visible regions and coherent completion on unobserved surfaces. Trained solely on public datasets, SUPRA-Tex achieves SOTA performance in both geometry-conditioned texturing and end-to-end asset generation, matching or even exceeding leading commercial systems on in-the-wild images. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [130] [DK-VLM: Geometry as Position for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22356)
*Han Su,Meng Tian,Tianyu Huang,Mingyu Gao,Lennox Wei,Dongxu Li,Jianhua Han,Hang Xu,Xiaohe Wu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DK-VLM 用双核注意力把几何信息作为位置（而非融合的重建特征）注入注意力机制，并辅以参考系组合监督，在多个空间基准上取得最优成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在多视角3D空间关系理解、以及观察者移动后更新空间判断方面仍然薄弱。常见做法是把3D重建特征融合进视觉token，但在空间监督有限的情况下，这类特征与预训练语义表征难以对齐。

Method: 提出DK-VLM双核注意力：注意力同时由视觉token的输入位置与其在场景中的几何位置计算。语义核保留预训练模型原生注意力与位置编码；几何核利用估计的3D点与相机位姿，通过点位移和相对视角变换直接调制注意力；再用学习到的门控融合两路输出，使几何以"位置"形式进入注意力。另提出Reference-Frame Composition (RC) 监督，由经过验证的单步标注导出逐步转向与朝向目标，训练模型在连续判断中更新参考系。

Result: DK-VLM-8B在六个空间基准上取得最高平均分，在VSI-Bench（74.33）与ReVSI-32（58.28）上取得最高总分。RC监督进一步提升多步空间推理能力，完整模型同时保持通用视频理解性能。

Conclusion: 将几何以位置形式注入注意力、并显式监督参考系更新，是提升VLM跨视角空间推理的有效途径，且不牺牲通用视频理解能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models still struggle to understand 3D spatial relations across views and update spatial judgments as the observer moves. A common approach fuses 3D reconstruction features into visual tokens, but aligning these features with pretrained semantic representations remains challenging under limited spatial supervision. We propose DK-VLM with Dual-Kernel Attention, which computes attention from both a visual token's input position and its geometric position in the scene. A semantic kernel retains the pretrained model's native attention and positional encoding, while a separate geometric kernel uses estimated 3D points and camera poses to directly modulate attention through point displacements and relative view transformations. Then, a learned gate fuses their outputs. Geometry thus enters attention as position rather than as fused reconstruction features. We further introduce Reference-Frame Composition (RC) supervision to train the model to update its reference frame across successive judgments. It provides stepwise turn and heading targets derived from verified single-step annotations. Among the compared methods, DK-VLM-8B achieves the highest average score across six spatial benchmarks and the highest overall scores on VSI-Bench (74.33) and ReVSI-32 (58.28). RC supervision further improves multi-step spatial reasoning, while the full model preserves general video understanding. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [131] [Grounding Generative Verification in 3D Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22360)
*Shuanglin Li,Jiaying Lin,Shuquan Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出以"可验证反馈"为核心的可靠3D系统框架，构建3DVerifierBench（10项任务，评测判定、拒绝解释与规格/候选受控变化的响应），并给出多视角事实抽取+受控3D编辑+原生标注的可扩展数据管线（14,126条带矛盾解释与反事实的训练样本），进一步验证了验证机制在3D生成候选选择与递归细化中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM与3D生成能力快速提升，但超越视觉保真度的"任务相关正确性"仍缺乏3D证据支撑。作者主张可靠3D系统不仅需要更强的生成与推理，还需要能识别未满足需求并解释违反原因的验证反馈，且该反馈须整合多视角观测、区分真实不一致与投影依赖的外观差异。

Method: 1) 提出3DVerifierBench，覆盖10类任务，评估判定、拒绝解释以及对规格与候选受控变化的响应，并开展人类研究；2) 在细粒度事实类别上分析验证，暴露数值与目标绑定错误，构建多视角事实抽取+受控3D编辑+原生标注的可扩展管线，生成14,126条含矛盾解释与关联反事实的训练样本；3) 研究验证如何通过候选选择与递归细化支撑3D生成。

Result: 人类研究显示人类与最强被测模型GPT-6 Astra之间存在21.6个百分点的差距，解释感知分析揭示拒绝可靠性薄弱；在人工标注的16候选池中，oracle选择将完全事实成功率从26.7%提升至90.0%；在8输入研究中，人类引导的递归细化对每个输入均满足全部7项要求，而并行Oracle@8仅满足3项。

Conclusion: 基准、分析与数据管线共同为模型通过可靠验证反馈对3D内容进行反思与细化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and 3D generation have expanded 3D understanding and synthesis. However, beyond visual fidelity, task-specific correctness grounded in 3D evidence remains a major challenge. We argue that building reliable 3D systems requires not only stronger generation and reasoning, but also verification feedback that identifies unmet requirements and explains their violations, allowing systems to reflect on and refine their outputs. Such feedback must integrate multi-view observations and distinguish genuine inconsistencies from projection-dependent appearances. We make three contributions. First, we introduce 3DVerifierBench, a benchmark spanning 10 tasks that evaluates decisions, rejection explanations, and responses to controlled changes in specifications and candidates. A human study reveals a 21.6 percentage-point gap between humans and the strongest tested model, GPT-6 Astra, while explanation-aware analysis exposes weaknesses in rejection reliability. Second, we analyze verification across fine-grained factual categories, exposing errors in values and target binding. We develop a scalable pipeline combining multi-view fact extraction, controlled 3D editing, and native annotations to produce 14,126 training examples with contradiction explanations and linked counterfactuals. Third, we examine how verification could support 3D generation through candidate selection and recursive refinement. In human-labeled sixteen-candidate pools, oracle selection improves complete factual success from 26.7% to 90.0%. In a separate eight-input study, human-guided recursive refinement satisfies all seven requirements for every input, compared with three under parallel Oracle@8. Together, our benchmark, analysis, and data pipeline establish a foundation for models to reflect on 3D content through reliable verification feedback.

</details>


### [132] [Perceive, Predict, and Plan: a Unified World Action Model for Egocentric Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22363)
*Zirui Wu,Chen Guo,Tianjian Jiang,Zeren Jiang,Zeming Li,Lingchen Yang,Longteng Duan,Ying Xue,Handi Yin,Shenyuan Gao,Martin R. Oswald,Jun Zhang,Ping Tan,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 P³-WAM 统一世界动作模型，通过 Perceive/Predict/Plan 三模式联合训练，将结构化 3D 动作表示注入预训练视频扩散 Transformer，实现第一视角灵巧操作的感知、预测与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角灵巧操作需要同时具备 3D 手部运动理解、动作条件视觉预测与目标导向规划能力，但现有方法通常分而治之，单一模态训练难以在三项任务间共享表征。

Method: 采用 mixture-of-transformers 架构，共享基础权重、模态专用适配层与双向视觉-动作注意力，将紧凑结构化 3D 动作表示注入预训练视频扩散 Transformer；通过随机模态可见性让视频与 3D 动作在感知、预测、规划三种模式中可互换地作为条件或预测目标。

Result: 统一训练在三个能力上均优于单模式训练，规划质量在 VLM 评测下提升；在四个数据集上超过专门基线（3D 手部重建、动作条件视频预测），并生成指令遵从性与动作进展更优的目标导向操作视频。

Conclusion: 统一的世界-动作建模在三模式间形成正迁移，为第一视角灵巧操作提供可扩展的通用框架。

Abstract: We introduce P$^{3}$-WAM, a unified world action model for egocentric dexterous manipulation that learns 3D perception, action-conditioned prediction, and goal-directed planning through three complementary modes: Perceive, which reconstructs articulated hand motion and camera trajectories from video; Predict, which generates visual futures under prescribed actions; and Plan, which jointly generates action trajectories and their anticipated visual consequences conditioned on an initial image, a language instruction, and an optional visual goal. Our model integrates a compact, structured 3D action representation into a pretrained video diffusion transformer through a mixture-of-transformers architecture with shared base weights, modality-specific adaptation, and bidirectional visual-action attention. Randomized modality visibility lets video and 3D actions serve as either conditioning context or prediction targets across the three modes. Experiments show that unified training improves all three capabilities over single-mode training, including gains in planning quality under a vision-language model evaluation. Through unified training, our model outperforms specialized baselines on 3D hand reconstruction and action-conditioned video prediction across four datasets, while producing goal-directed manipulation videos with stronger instruction adherence and action progression than video-generation baselines. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [133] [SoftCache: Adaptive Step Size Caching for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22369)
*Shimon Malnick,Nadav Orenstein,Ohad Fried,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SoftCache 提出"软缓存"机制：利用动态缓存中阈值余量（headroom）连续预测复用误差，让缓存步长自适应跳步，从而在图像/视频扩散与流模型上实现 3.5×–6.9× 加速且全面优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流模型生成成本主要由每个迭代步的完整大网络前向决定。免训练缓存通过复用近期激活来加速，但现有动态缓存只做"硬缓存"：探针累积成的陈旧度信号仅作为二值开关，决定刷新还是复用，且无论刷新或复用都按固定步长前进，浪费了信号中蕴含的误差信息。

Method: 作者观察到探针信号距阈值之差（headroom）可预测复用误差。SoftCache 据此做软缓存：以 headroom 连续调节每个缓存步的前进距离——余量大时跳过多个调度节点，临近刷新时步长收缩回原始步长；跳步可能落在调度节点之间，使采样器偏离固定网格。方法免训练、无需按骨干网络调参。

Result: 在图像模型 FLUX 及视频模型 HunyuanVideo、Wan2.1 上，加速比 3.5×–6.9×，在全部四项保真度指标上均优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 将缓存决策信号从二值开关升级为连续步长控制，可显著提升扩散/流模型采样的加速-质量权衡，且即插即用、无需训练与逐骨干调参。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow models generate a sample over iterative steps, each a full pass through a large network, and that pass dominates generation cost. Training-free caching accelerates them by reusing activations from recent evaluations instead of recomputing them. Dynamic caching methods decide when to refresh by accumulating a cheap probe of the network input into a staleness signal and spending a fresh evaluation once it crosses a threshold. They perform hard caching: the signal is a binary switch between refresh and reuse, and every step, fresh or cached, advances by the prescribed step size. We observe that the signal carries much more than this switch. How far it sits below the threshold, its headroom, predicts the error induced by reuse. We propose SoftCache, which performs soft caching: instead of a binary switch, the headroom sets how far each cached step goes. Where the headroom is large, the sampler jumps past several scheduled nodes, and as a refresh nears, its steps shrink back toward the original step. These adaptive jumps may land between the scheduled nodes, taking the sampler off its fixed grid. SoftCache requires no training and adds no per-backbone settings: on FLUX for images and on HunyuanVideo and Wan2.1 for video, at speedups from $3.5\times$ to $6.9\times$, it is ahead of every baseline on all four fidelity metrics.

</details>


### [134] [Which Tokens to Merge? Diffusion Dynamics for Efficient Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22370)
*SeungJu Cha,Ye-Chan Kim,HyunGee Kim,Sungho Koh,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的动态感知Token合并框架DaTMe，利用Tweedie去噪估计构建动态重要性图，在给定压缩率下自适应区分锚点与安全合并候选，实现扩散模型加速且保持全图视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Token合并方法依赖"文本相关性"准则，只保护提示词对齐的前景区域，忽略未被提示词提及的背景物体与局部细节，导致结构连贯性下降。作者希望用扩散过程本身的去噪动态来决定Token重要性。

Method: DaTMe为免训练框架：从Tweedie去噪估计中构建动态感知重要性图，融合两个信号——时间信号（预测干净图像在相邻去噪步间的变化，衡量Token是否已收敛）与结构信号（局部高频内容，如边界与细节）。基于该图在给定压缩率下自适应分配角色：重要Token保留为锚点，同时满足"时间稳定+结构同质"的Token作为安全合并候选。

Result: 在PixArt-α与FLUX上的实验表明，DaTMe在加速生成的同时能维持整幅图像的视觉保真度，避免了仅保护前景区域带来的背景结构退化。

Conclusion: 扩散动力学可作为有效的Token级合并决策依据，为高效扩散生成提供了不依赖文本对齐先验的加速思路。

Abstract: Token merging has emerged as an effective training-free strategy for accelerating diffusion models by reducing redundant token computation while minimizing generation degradation. A key challenge is determining which tokens can be safely merged and which should be preserved for subsequent computation. Existing method addresses this by preserving prompt-aligned foreground regions, thereby protecting semantically relevant structures. However, this text-relevance criterion overlooks background objects, local details that are not explicitly mentioned in the prompt, leading to degraded structural coherence. In this work, we propose Dynamics-aware Token Merging (DaTMe), a training-free framework that grounds token merging in the underlying diffusion dynamics. Our key insight is that token importance should reflect whether a token remains unresolved during denoising and whether it carries structural information. To this end, DaTMe constructs a dynamics- aware importance map from the Tweedie denoised estimate. The map combines a temporal signal, measuring inter-step changes in the predicted clean image, with a structural signal, capturing local high-frequency content such as boundaries and fine details. Using this map, DaTMe adaptively assigns tokens into merging roles under a given compression ratio: important tokens are preserved as anchors, while tokens that are both temporally stabilized and structurally homogeneous are selected as safe merge candidates. Experiments on PixArt-a and FLUX demonstrate that DaTMe maintains visual fidelity across the entire image, highlighting diffusion dynamics as an effective token-level merging decision in efficient generation. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [135] [Gen4P: Video Generators are 4D Perceivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22371)
*Yitong Xia,Zeren Jiang,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gen4P将预训练视频生成器改造为前馈4D感知器，无需迭代去噪，单次编码即可从单目视频恢复4D几何与运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频生成模型蕴含丰富的时空先验，但现有方法多依赖迭代去噪，推理昂贵且难以灵活查询；作者希望以更高效、更灵活的方式将生成式预训练知识迁移到统一的4D感知任务上。

Method: 提出on-demand querying机制：对干净视频latent做单次encoder前向，再用轻量cross-attention decoder预测所需4D属性；支持灵活的空间采样；提出连续时间查询获得时间维度灵活性；将预训练video-depth LoRA作为冻结的几何先验以插件方式注入，不增加可训练参数。

Result: 在3D点追踪、视频深度和相机位姿估计任务上达到SOTA或具备竞争力；在in-the-wild视频上可追踪复杂运动并保持细致结构。

Conclusion: 结果展示了视频生成式预训练在统一4D感知中的潜力，前馈式、可查询的框架是高效复用生成先验的可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce Gen4P, a unified framework for recovering 4D geometry and motion from monocular video. We show that a pretrained video generator can be repurposed into a feed-forward 4D perceiver without iterative denoising. Building on on-demand querying, Gen4P processes clean video latents in a single encoder pass and predicts the requested 4D attributes through a lightweight cross-attention decoder. The on-demand query formulation enables flexible spatial sampling, and our proposed continuous-time queries further provide temporal flexibility. To strengthen geometric knowledge, we propose a straightforward plug-in solution by incorporating a pretrained video-depth LoRA as a frozen geometry prior, improving overall performance without adding extra trainable parameters. Gen4P achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance on tasks of 3D point tracking, video depth and camera pose estimation. Qualitative results on in-the-wild videos illustrate its capability of tracking complex motion while maintaining the delicate structure. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of video generative pretraining for unified 4D perception. See this https URL for more visual results.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Causal Analysis of Robot Foundation Models Enables Efficiency Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20750)
*Aiden Swann,Mathilde Papillon,Mozes Jacobs,Siddharth Boppana,Tal Haklay,Eric Bigelow,Andrew Lee,Conor Watts,Thomas Icard,Mac Schwager,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Atticus Geiger,Thomas Fel,Matthew Kowal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对三个前沿VLA模型（π0.5、MolmoAct2、MolmoBot）进行机制可解释性分析，发现图像与指令信息通过"动作条件接口"（中后段交叉注意力层的窄带）影响动作专家；据此裁剪条件层可将MolmoBot推理FLOPs降低约33%而成功率基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型是通用机器人操作的重要路线，但其内部VLM表征如何转化为动作这一问题研究不足；理解这一机制既有助于解释性，也可能直接带来效率改进。

Method: 机制分析：在π0.5、MolmoAct2、MolmoBot上使用激活patch（activation patching）追踪图像与指令到动作的因果路径；逐层、逐token分析中后段交叉注意力层的信息内容；对比不同架构中空间信息在prompt token（文本/图像token）中的存储位置；并将结论用于限制动作专家的条件层数。

Result: 三者共同存在"动作条件接口"（中后段交叉注意力窄带）；目标名词token的早中段表征决定物体选择，中后段文本token携带可重定向运动的空间信息；MolmoAct2在指令后文本位置形成任务条件表征，π0.5额外在图像token位置存储；MolmoBot仅用36层中3层条件，FLOPs降33%（约33%），3000次评估成功率46.7% vs 46.9%，原理可迁移至π0.5真机部署。

Conclusion: VLA的动作生成依赖一个狭窄的中后段交叉注意力接口，该机制性发现可直接转化为推理效率优化，并在仿真大规模评估与真机部署中保持任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are a promising approach to general purpose robot manipulation. While the internal computation of vision-language models, which compose the backbone of VLAs, has been studied extensively, less is known about how their perceptual and semantic representations are transformed into action in VLA models. We study this question mechanistically in three frontier VLA models ($\pi_{0.5}$, MolmoAct2, and MolmoBot) by analyzing the causal pathways through which the input image and the instruction shape actions. Across the three models, activation patching shows that causally relevant information influences the action expert (AE) through a narrow band of mid-to-late cross-attention layers, which we term the action-conditioning interface. The content of this information changes with token and layer within the model. Early-to-mid layer representations of the target noun token determine which object is selected, whereas mid-to-late layers carry the spatial information in text tokens that redirects the movement when patched. Where spatial information is stored in prompt tokens various based on the architecture. In MolmoAct2 the model forms task-conditioned representations at the text positions that follow the instruction, whereas $\pi_{0.5}$ additionally stores this information at image token positions. We show that these findings can be translated directly into improving model efficiency. We find that restricting MolmoBot's action expert conditioning to only 3 out of 36 layers reduces inference FLOPs by up to $33\%$ while maintaining comparable task success with the default model across 3,000 evaluation episodes ($46.7\%$ vs. $46.9\%$), and the same principle carries over to real-robot deployment of $\pi_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [137] [Multi-Dimensional Featurizers Yield Fine-Grained Steering and Data Shaping in Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20767)
*Aiden Swann,Mozes Jacobs,Mathilde Papillon,Siddharth Boppana,David Chanin,Dron Hazra,Usha Bhalla,Curt Tigges,Mac Schwager,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Atticus Geiger,Thomas Fel,Matthew Kowal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用块稀疏特征器(BSF)从VLA模型中提取多维任务相关特征，实现细粒度因果操控与数据归因，定位不到1.4%的关键训练token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型中的多维特征结构启发作者探索：将特征器与表征几何匹配，能否为VLA模型提供可解释的操控接口，并用于数据侧的行为塑造。

Method: 采用块稀疏特征器（BSF），将每个特征表示为低维子空间，从VLA模型激活中恢复多维特征；通过定向修改视觉感知、语义表征与空间定位进行因果验证；再用基于特征的数据归因定位训练数据中特定行为（失败恢复）来源。

Result: BSF恢复出感知、语言、本体状态、episode阶段、控制变量等多维任务相关特征；定向干预可预测地改变下游动作预测；数据归因识别出少于1.4%的训练token，掩蔽后足以去除失败恢复行为。

Conclusion: 将特征器与表征几何结构匹配，可为理解和改变机器人行为提供更有效的工具，兼具内部干预与训练数据塑造两条路径。

Abstract: Can better feature discovery improve control over robot behavior through internal interventions and training data? Recent work in vision and language models reveals multidimensional features, motivating block-sparse featurizers (BSFs), which represent each feature in a low-dimensional subspace. We investigate whether recovering this structure provides an interpretable interface for steering vision-language-action models and shaping the data from which they learn. We show that BSFs recover several multi-dimensional task-relevant features underlying the model's computation (e.g., perception, language, proprioceptive state, episode phase, and control variables). We causally validate these features through fine-grained steering: targeted changes in visual perception, semantic representation, and spatial localization produce predictable changes in downstream action prediction. Finally, we use feature-based data attribution to isolate where a specific behavior (failure recovery) arises in the training dataset, identifying fewer than $1.4\%$ of training tokens whose masking is sufficient to remove the targeted behavior. Overall, our results show how matching featurizers to representation geometry can provide more effective tools for understanding and changing robot behavior.

</details>


### [138] [Navigation by Skylight Polarization via Rayleigh Model Optimization on a Road Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20797)
*Ben Potter,Eslam Mounier,Aboelmagd Noureldin,Yahia Antar,Muhammad Alam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出天窗偏振导航方法：用分焦平面偏振相机，基于瑞利天空模型与太阳位置算法合成候选位姿的偏振角图像，以偏振度加权的RMSE全局优化航向；三条城市道路轨迹上RMSE偏航误差0.718°–0.889°，相较特征法与模型法最高降低87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路车辆导航中的绝对航向参考（磁、惯导、GNSS）易退化，需要一种不依赖这些参考的鲁棒绝对航向估计手段；现有偏振导航多依赖特征提取，在遮挡与低置信度像素条件下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 结合针孔相机模型、单次散射瑞利偏振模型与太阳位置算法，为候选车辆状态合成全图像偏振角分布；以偏振度加权的均方根误差为目标函数，对比实测与仿真偏振模式，实现全局优化航向估计；低偏振度像素被降权以抑制云、建筑、植被等去偏振遮挡的影响。

Result: 使用车顶偏振相机与高精度双天线GNSS/INS作为参考，在三条城市道路轨迹上取得亚度级RMS偏航误差（0.718°–0.889°）；与代表性特征法、模型法偏振导航方法相比，RMS航向误差最多降低87%，对部分天空遮挡的鲁棒性更好。

Conclusion: 将偏振图像的整体信息纳入全局优化，并以偏振度加权抑制遮挡干扰，是一种有效的绝对航向估计方案；但在城市道路场景、有限轨迹下验证，开阔天空与动态遮挡下的泛化性仍待考察。

Abstract: Reliable absolute heading estimation is critical for road-vehicle navigation, particularly when magnetic, inertial, or GNSS-based references are degraded. This paper presents a skylight polarization navigation method that estimates vehicle heading by globally optimizing a Rayleigh sky model against measurements from a division-of-focal-plane polarimetric camera.
