The proposed framework combines a pinhole camera model, a single-scattering Rayleigh polarization model, and a solar position algorithm to synthesize the expected full image angle of polarization distribution for candidate vehicle states. A degree of polarization weighted root mean square error objective function compares measured and simulated polarization patterns, allowing the estimator to use all available sky information while attenuating low confidence pixels caused by clouds, buildings, foliage, and other depolarizing occlusions. Experimental evaluation is performed using three urban road vehicle trajectories collected with a roof mounted polarimetric camera and a high precision dual-antenna GNSS/INS reference system. Results show sub-degree RMS yaw error across all trajectories, ranging from $0.718^{\circ}$ to $0.889^{\circ}$. Compared with representative feature-based and model-based polarization navigation methods, the proposed approach reduces RMS heading error by up to 87% and demonstrates improved robustness to partial sky occlusion.

</details>


### [139] [Feasibility Ceilings Before Learning: Sizing and Timing Bounds for Drone-Mesh Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20823)
*Dmytro Babarytskyi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究多智能体拦截器覆盖预测落点的问题，给出覆盖率与不确定性闭式界，证明分离约束下平面覆盖率上限90.69%（体积74.05%），并指出允许重叠可用Kershner最薄覆盖密度突破该上限；同时揭示预测偏差对覆盖的脆弱性与PPO学习的低效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体机器人中，N个覆盖半径r圆盘的智能体需按预测落点概率密度分布布撒，以最大化拦截覆盖概率；现有工作缺乏在策略选定之前就能界定性能上限的解析工具，且常忽视分离约束带来的覆盖率天花板，导致学习实验结果缺少可比较的基准。

Method: 采用最小二乘方差传播加delta方法处理到达时间不确定性，推导噪声线性轨迹外推落点的不确定性闭式；对密排网格推导覆盖率闭式及体积类比；结合分离约束（智能体间距≥2r）推导平面/空间覆盖率上限，并对比Kershner最薄覆盖密度、五种排列（含六边形密排）与质心Voronoi配置覆盖控制；用Monte Carlo验证；最后以解析布撒与集中式PPO对比，并用配对bootstrap检验。

Result: 不确定性闭式与仿真误差在1.8%以内；覆盖率闭式绝对误差0.007；分离约束下平面覆盖率不超过90.69%、体积不超过74.05%，该上限源于分离约束而非问题本身，允许重叠后可采用Kershner最薄覆盖密度并在p=0.80交叉点以上更优；六边形密排在五种排列中最优；质心Voronoi覆盖控制在低预算下仅达其覆盖率的一半；高斯假设在重尾和各向异性下保守，但对偏差脆弱，1σ瞄准点偏移损失14个百分点覆盖；解析布撒达配置允许上限的85%，而集中式PPO在三个训练种子下平均仅15%，配对bootstrap置信区间在每个种子下均含零。

Conclusion: 应在任何学习实验之前计算此类覆盖率上限，并在每个结果旁标注该上限；PPO等学习方法在该任务上远未逼近解析可达性能，解析布撒显著优于学习策略，作者呼吁将性能天花板作为标准报告项。

Abstract: A mesh of interceptors defending a fixed site must spread over the predicted arrival point of an approaching object. This is a recurring task in multi-agent robotics: N agents, each covering a disc of radius r, spread over a probability density so that a point drawn from it falls within some agent's disc. We give closed forms for the quantities that bound performance before any policy is chosen, and validate each by Monte Carlo. The uncertainty of a point predicted by extrapolating a noisy linear track follows from least-squares variance propagation plus a delta-method term for the uncertain arrival time, agreeing with simulation to within 1.8%. The coverage probability of a packed mesh admits a closed form accurate to 0.007 absolute, with a volumetric analogue. A naive area ratio misses a ceiling: while agents stay at least 2r apart, no mesh exceeds 90.69% coverage in the plane or 74.05% in a volume. This ceiling belongs to the separation constraint, not to the problem - permitting overlap substitutes Kershner's thinnest-covering density and removes it, the better regime above a crossover at p=0.80. Hexagonal packing is optimal among five arrangements; the centroidal-Voronoi configuration coverage control supplies reaches only half its coverage at small budgets. The Gaussian assumption is conservative under heavy tails and anisotropy but fragile under bias: a one-sigma aim-point offset costs 14 points of coverage. A repositioning team must also choose a commit time, and the admissible window can be empty. Against the applicable ceiling, analytic placement attains 85% of what the configuration permits while centralised PPO averages 15% over three trainer seeds, with a paired bootstrap against a null policy containing zero at every seed. We argue such ceilings should be computed before any learning experiment and quoted alongside every result.

</details>


### [140] [Persistent Profile-Guided Reset-Free Recovery Improves Final Gait Selection after Irreversible Leg Damage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20838)
*Minjoong Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PGRR（持续profile引导的无重置恢复）算法，通过预测多次步态执行结果并用保守不确定性估计指导在线短探针，在108个MuJoCo损伤任务上优于GP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短试验排名出的步态在持续执行中未必有效；且无重置恢复中每次试验会改变下一次继承的物理状态，现有方法忽略了这种跨试验物理状态耦合。

Method: PGRR：对重复步态执行的多结果进行预测，采用保守的、不确定性感知的估计值，在不重置机器人的情况下指导短在线探针（online probes）。

Result: 108个匹配MuJoCo任务（截肢、远端骨折、关节锁死）：最终质量+0.106，SRQ+0.044，低质量失败减少27.8个百分点；三类损伤均有最终质量提升；消融显示保守不确定性处理是主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: PGRR能在连续物理状态下识别更强的损伤后步态；最终步态质量与恢复汇总性能提供互补评估证据。

Abstract: Damage-recovery algorithms often rank gaits from short trials, yet a gait that performs well briefly may not remain effective during continued execution. In reset-free recovery, each trial also changes the physical state inherited by the next. We introduce persistent profile-guided reset-free recovery (PGRR), which predicts multiple outcomes over repeated gait execution and uses conservative uncertainty-aware estimates to guide short online probes without resetting the robot. Across 108 matched MuJoCo tasks spanning leg amputation, distal fracture, and joint lock, PGRR improves final quality by 0.106 and slot-averaged recovery quality (SRQ) by 0.044 relative to an IT&E-inspired Gaussian-process baseline, while reducing low-quality failures by 27.8 percentage points. Final-quality gains occur in all three damage classes. Against no adaptation, PGRR also improves final quality and reduces low-quality failures, whereas the aggregate SRQ difference is inconclusive. Development ablations identify conservative uncertainty handling as the clearest supported contributor to the observed gains. These results show that PGRR can identify stronger post-damage gaits under continuous physical state and that final-gait quality and recovery-summary performance provide complementary evidence for evaluating reset-free recovery.

</details>


### [141] [A Phenomenology of Neural Geometry in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20840)
*Mozes Jacobs,Aiden Swann,Mathilde Papillon,Siddharth Boppana,Fenil R. Doshi,Usha Bhalla,Thomas Icard,Matt Feiszli,Thomas McGrath,Vasudev Shyam,Demba Ba,Leon Bergen,Nina Miolane,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Atticus Geiger,Matthew Kowal,Thomas Fel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控扫描、自然rollout、线性探针与激活干预，构建了VLA模型中七个任务相关概念的"实现几何"分类法，发现概念在世界模型内部多呈多维几何形状（受物理约束塑形），且几何信息可用于激活引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有VLA研究证明任务相关变量可从激活中线性读出，但"可读性"只说明变量存在，不能刻画其取值所描绘的几何形状（可能弯曲、多维）。作者希望回答策略内部世界的形状问题，弥补可读性分析的不足。

Method: 采用受控扫描（controlled sweeps）、自然rollout、线性探针（linear probes）和激活干预（activation interventions）四种手段，对VLA模型中的七个任务相关概念进行系统刻画，建立实现几何分类法，并验证几何结构的跨模型可复现性与可操作性。

Result: 实现几何以多维为主，并受策略所处物理约束影响：关节限位使腕部朝向呈开放弧而非完整圆；目标距离这一标量在粗细两把"尺子"对应的两个正交子空间中分别实现（接近阶段粗尺、抓取阶段细尺）。相同几何在两个额外VLA模型及真实机器人数据上重现，说明非模型特异、非仿真伪影；沿几何方向进行激活引导可产生行为效果。

Conclusion: 论文给出VLA策略"实现几何"的初步现象学，指出概念的几何形状与其可读性同等重要，并将几何结构转化为可操作的激活引导手段。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is inherently geometric, but what shape does the world take inside the policy that performs it? Prior work on vision-language-action (VLA) policies establishes that task-relevant variables are linearly readable from activations, yet readability certifies only that a variable is present and says nothing about the shape its values trace, which may be curved and multi-dimensional. Using controlled sweeps, natural rollouts, linear probes, and activation interventions, we build a taxonomy of seven task-relevant concepts in VLA models. We find that realized geometry is predominantly multi-dimensional and is shaped by the constraints under which the policy acts. Physical joint limits, for example, leave wrist orientation an open arc rather than a circle, and distance to the target, a single scalar, is realized in two orthogonal subspaces, a coarse ruler for the approach and a fine ruler for the grasp. The same geometries reappear in two further VLA models on real-world robot data, indicating that they are neither model-specific nor artifacts of simulation. We demonstrate how the findings are actionable by steering along the geometry a concept realizes. Overall, this work lays out an initial phenomenology of realized geometry in VLA policies and demonstrates that a concept's shape carries as much importance as its readability.

</details>


### [142] [Clarity-Driven Adaptive Mission Planning for a Solar-Powered Autonomous Surface Vessel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20871)
*Kavin M. Govindarajan,Kaleb Ben Naveed,David Li,Devansh R. Agrawal,Mary Maceda,Matthew Bryant,Dimitra Panagou,Chris Vermillion*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以"清晰度"(clarity)最大化为数学基础的太阳能自主水面艇(ASV)自适应任务规划方法，通过清晰度驱动的遍历搜索与在线更新目标清晰度图，在可再生能源约束下持续重规划以优先采集高兴趣区域信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学任务中"最大兴趣位置"无法先验已知，只能通过现场测量逐步识别，因此需要在线自适应规划；同时ASV受限于太阳能等有限可再生能源，机动能力受限，必须在能量约束下最大化信息采集效率。

Method: 以与微分信息熵严格关联的"清晰度"(clarity)作为信息度量，构建目标清晰度图，并依据现场测量在线更新该图以反映区域优先级；采用遍历(ergodic)路径规划器结合ASV速度限制进行持续重规划，形成清晰度驱动的遍历搜索框架。方法辅以仿真与实验验证。

Result: 在美国北卡罗来纳州Jordan Lake开展了太阳能ASV的仿真与实地实验，结果表明所提自适应任务规划策略能够有效优先采集高兴趣区域信息，并在可再生能源机动约束下实现信息采集最大化。

Conclusion: 清晰度作为一种与微分熵严格关联的数学量，可作为信息驱动任务规划的目标函数；将在线清晰度图更新与遍历路径规划结合，可在可再生能源限制下实现ASV科学任务的自适应规划，并已在真实水域实验中验证有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive methodology, backed by simulation and experimental results, for an adaptive mission planning strategy that is mathematically grounded in the maximization of the information-driven quantity of clarity. In particular, the methodology introduced in this work addresses the use of sustainably powered autonomous surface vessels (ASVs) to perform scientific missions that prioritize locations of maximum interest. Because these locations of maximum interest are not known a priori but are rather identified based on in-situ measurements, online adaptation is essential for mission planning. Furthermore, the reliance on a limited-power renewable resource requires an adaptation strategy that maximizes information gathering subject to mobility restrictions imposed by the renewable resource. Our approach addresses these challenges through a clarity-driven ergodic search, where clarity represents a mathematical quantity that has been rigorously linked to differential (information) entropy. In our approach, a target clarity map is adapted online to reflect the prioritization of certain areas based on in-situ measurements, and an ergodic path planner continually re-plans based on the target clarity map and ASV speed limitations (driven by the renewable propulsion source). Focusing on a solar-powered ASV, we present simulations and experiments conducted at Jordan Lake, North Carolina, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [143] [Race to Recovery: Designing Advanced Safety Systems for Autonomous Driving via Learning Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20873)
*Teodor Ilie,Kleber Cabral,Sidney Givigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过强化学习先训练竞速漂移策略，再以最小时间奖励继续训练迁移为失控恢复策略，并定义相平面稳定平衡态作为恢复判据，配套Gym-Khana仿真环境与1:10 F1TENTH实车验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量产安全系统（如ESC）只针对失稳预防，无法处理结冰、水滑或紧急避障导致的极限失控；专家驾驶员靠漂移重获控制权。现有自主恢复方法存在三方面不足：仅在理想场景验证；对"已恢复"状态的经验性、不一致定义；依赖离线规划轨迹而非在线决策。

Method: 先用强化学习训练一条竞速策略，使漂移作为时间最优机动被习得；再通过带最小时间奖励的持续训练迁移至恢复任务。以相平面上的稳定平衡态定义恢复，提出Gym-Khana用于控制器训练与评估。消融对比验证是竞速预训练而非更宽的初始状态暴露带来增益。实车侧采用系统辨识、执行器滞后建模与域随机化部署到1:10 F1TENTH。

Result: 在294个初始失控状态下，迁移策略相比Stanley路径跟踪器恢复率提升186.8%，优于非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC）与从零训练的恢复专用策略，且在基线本可处理的状态上恢复更快。实车上对所有测试失控状态均优于Stanley，其中一个基线从未恢复的困难状态可达到50%成功率。

Conclusion: 将竞速漂移技能作为预训练先验，可持续训练高效迁移为在线最小时间失控恢复策略，配合相平面稳定平衡态定义与仿真-实车迁移流程，在仿真与实车均显著超越经典跟踪与MPC基线。

Abstract: Vehicles that lose control at the handling limit through icing, hydroplaning, or an abrupt evasive maneuver fall outside the operating envelope of production safety systems such as electronic stability control, which focus on preventing instability rather than recovering from it. Expert drivers regain authority in these conditions by drifting. Existing autonomous recovery approaches are validated on idealized scenarios, define "recovered" states empirically and inconsistently, or rely on offline-planned trajectories rather than acting online. We address these gaps by first training a racing policy with reinforcement learning that learns drifting as a time-optimal maneuver, then adapting it to recovery through continued training with a minimum-time reward. We define recovery relative to a phase-plane-grounded stable equilibrium and introduce Gym-Khana for controller training and evaluation. Across 294 initial loss-of-control states, the transferred policy improves recovery by 186.8% over a Stanley path tracker and outperforms both a nonlinear model predictive controller and a recovery-only policy trained from scratch, while also recovering faster on states the baseline can already handle. An ablation shows that racing pretraining, rather than broader initial-state exposure alone, drives this gain. Deployed on a physical 1:10-scale F1TENTH vehicle using system identification, actuator-lag modelling, and domain randomization, the transferred policy outperforms Stanley on every tested loss-of-control state, including recovering 50% of the time from a hard state the baseline never recovers from.

</details>


### [144] [Optimally Trading Energy for Information through Speed Trajectory Optimization of Sustainably Powered Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20875)
*Kavin M. Govindarajan,Dimitra Panagou,Chris Vermillion*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将可持续供能移动机器人的持久观测任务建模为周期最优控制问题，以信息论"清晰度"为信息度量，推导最优速度轨迹的必要条件与专门性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量采集型移动机器人可长时间执行观测任务，但能源有限且随路径位置动态变化，需要自主决策如何分配能量以在时间上最大化信息质量。既有工作多聚焦瞬时信息增益或单次轨迹，缺乏对无限时域下周期速度轨迹的系统刻画。

Method: 将机器人沿固定闭合路径反复行进的持久速度轨迹优化表述为周期（无限时域）最优控制问题，其中推进代价为超线性（super-linear），信息增益与衰减动态耦合。采用信息论量"清晰度（clarity）"作为信息指标，先推导最优控制的必要条件（PMP型条件），再推导若干专门性质：包括路径点重要性不等与相等两种情形下最优控制的本质，以及当能量储备趋于无穷时所逼近的高速信息上限。最后在简单示例上展示最优速度剖面的关键趋势，并基于Chesapeake Bay盐度持久监测进行仿真研究。

Result: 给出周期最优控制的必要条件及一系列结构化性质：刻画了非均匀重要性与均匀重要性路径点下最优控制形态，并得出能量无限时的渐近高速信息上限；示例展示优化速度剖面的趋势，Chesapeake Bay盐度监测仿真表明方法具有实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 该工作为能量受限持久观测机器人提供了信息最优速度轨迹的理论框架与可操作性质，可在真实环境监测场景中指导速度规划；但结论主要依赖理论推导与仿真，尚缺实物机器人验证。

Abstract: Sustainably powered mobile robots enable long-duration observational missions by harvesting energy from the environment. However, effective autonomy requires the robots to make decisions as to how to allocate this energy to maximize information quality over time. In this paper, we study a persistent (infinite-horizon) speed trajectory optimization problem that is posed as a periodic optimal control problem in which a robot repeatedly traverses a fixed closed path, coupling a super-linear propulsion cost with dynamic information gain and decay. Using the information-theoretic quantity of clarity as an information metric, we first derive the necessary conditions for optimal control. We then derive a number of specialized follow-on properties, which characterize (i) the nature of the optimal control when points on the path are of varying importance and when they are of equal importance, along with (ii) a high-speed information limit that is approached when energy reserves approach infinity. We illustrate the information-optimal speed trajectories on a simple example in order to showcase key trends that are followed under the optimized speed profile. This is followed by a simulation study based on the persistent characterization of salinity in the Chesapeake Bay, illustrating the practical merit of applying the results from this work.

</details>


### [145] [BubbleCBF: Constructive Safety for Second-Order Systems and Application to Autonomous Robot Navigation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20895)
*Mani Amani,Mohammed Alyaseen,Zhirui Dai,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Nikolay Atanasov,Jorge Cortes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BubbleCBF：从SDF构造位置空间无碰撞气泡，利用其二次几何与重叠关系构造速度相关安全约束，以二次规划安全滤波器实现二阶控制仿射系统的可行安全控制，并在差速机器人上验证未知环境自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶动力学系统的位置安全约束可能要求不可行减速度，高阶CBF虽能表达安全条件，但其可行性依赖系统动力学与控制权限。因此需要一个可构造、可保证可行性的安全约束设计方法。

Method: 从有符号距离函数（SDF）出发，在位置空间构造无碰撞球体（bubble），利用其二次几何与重叠关系构造速度相关的安全约束；将安全集表示为若干交集之并，通过二次规划（QP）安全滤波器施加约束；在动力学、气泡重叠与设计参数的温和假设下给出理论分析。

Result: 证明所构造的QP控制器良定义、对系统状态连续，并使安全集前向不变；推导有界输入下的可行性充分条件；在差速驱动机器人上实现未知环境中的安全自主导航，并公开视频与结果。

Conclusion: BubbleCBF为二阶控制仿射系统提供了一种构造性、可证明可行的安全约束设计框架，兼顾几何直观性与QP实现效率，适用于实际机器人避障导航。

Abstract: Satisfying safety constraints for systems with second-order dynamics is challenging because a position constraint may require infeasible deceleration. Higher-order control barrier functions (CBFs) provide an approach for expressing safety conditions for such systems but feasibility depends on the system dynamics and available control authority. We develop a constructive approach for designing feasible safety constraints for second-order control-affine systems. Starting from a signed distance function (SDF), we construct collision-free spheres, i.e., bubbles, in position space, and use their quadratic geometry and overlap to construct a velocity-dependent safety constraint. The associated safe set is represented as a union of intersections and enforced through a quadratic-program safety filter. Under mild assumptions on the dynamics, bubble overlap, and design parameters, we prove that the quadratic-program-based controller is well defined, continuous in the system state, and renders the set forward invariant. We also derive sufficient conditions for feasibility under bounded inputs. We demonstrate that our BubbleCBF, applied to a differential-drive robot, achieves safe autonomous navigation in unknown environments. Videos and results are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [146] [TGrasp6D: Task-Informed Vision-Language Priors and Pose Refinement enable Zero-Shot Task-Oriented 6DoF Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20901)
*Manav Kulshrestha,Apoorva Vashisth,Damon Conover,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TGrasp6D：基于视觉-语言模型零样本生成任务导向6DoF抓取位姿，先在TaskGrasp上达94%成功率，再在Franka Research 3实机演示泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向抓取（TOG）需依任务选抓取区域（如切面包握刀柄、递刀需留出手柄）。现有方法局限于2D平面抓取、依赖任务无关抓取生成器，或受限于有限的人工标注任务特定抓取数据集，无法覆盖所有抓取-任务组合。

Method: TGrasp6D：给定任务文本与单张RGB-D图像，用视觉-语言模型推理任务对应的语义可供性与物体几何，生成初始任务先验；再通过6DoF位姿优化进行碰撞避免细化，同时保持任务一致性，实现零样本任务导向6DoF抓取生成。

Result: 在TaskGrasp数据集上成功率94%（最佳基线74%）；在Franka Research 3机器人上部署，展示对真实物体的零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言+VLM先验结合6DoF位姿优化的零样本范式显著优于依赖任务无关生成器或标注数据的既有方法，并具备实机泛化能力。

Abstract: Task-Oriented Grasping (TOG) has emerged as an important skill for purposeful robotic manipulation. Given the task of using a knife to "slice bread," a robot must choose to grasp from the handle, whereas for "hand[ing] the knife over," it is preferable for the handle to remain unobstructed for safe handoff. Current approaches for TOG either focus on 2D planar grasping, remain limited by their reliance on task-agnostic grasp generators, or are constrained by reliance on finite, manually-annotated, task-specific grasp datasets that cannot cover all possible grasp-task pairs. To that end, we present TGrasp6D, a vision-language based approach for generating task-oriented 6DoF grasps in a zero-shot manner. Given a task and a single RGB-D image of an associated object, TGrasp6D reasons about the appropriate semantic affordance for the given task and object geometry to produce an initial task- informed prior. This generated prior is further refined via 6DoF pose optimization for collision avoidance while preserving task consistency. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art success rate of 94% on the TaskGrasp dataset, with the best baseline at 74%. We also deploy our approach on a Franka Research 3 robot to demonstrate zero-shot generalization to real world objects

</details>


### [147] [EgoJEPA: Beyond Retargeting for Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20904)
*Meng Cao,Can Zhao,Jinjie Wei,Haonan Zhao,Yulong Zhang,Duo Han,Xiaofan Li,Ziwu Song,Rongtao Xu,Ruyi Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang,Daolin Ma,Ian Reid,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoJEPA 提出三阶段框架，用 JEPA 式视觉预测从第一视角人类视频中学习具身无关的潜在动作与世界模型，再通过轻量动作适配器迁移到不同机器人，避开手工重定向，在5个真实任务、3种构型上取得74%成功率并减少47.2%跨具身总耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角人类视频是可扩展的机器人示教替代品，但主流重定向方法依赖手工几何映射，需针对不同构型反复标定，且会把手部跟踪误差传播到下游控制。作者希望学习与具身无关的表征，摆脱规则式人到机器人重定向。

Method: EgoJEPA 三阶段：(1) 人类动作预训练——以手部运动差为条件，用 JEPA 式视觉预测学习"效果感知"的潜在动作；(2) 潜在动作世界模型——联合预测未来视觉动态与潜在动作，将人类先验预训练与机器人特定的控制参数化解耦；(3) 具身特定微调——通过轻量动作适配器把预训练模型适配到多种机器人。全程不含规则式重定向。

Result: 在3种机器人具身、5个真实世界操作任务上验证：域外评测平均成功率74%，重定向基线为65%；跨具身累计墙钟成本从743.2小时降至392.7小时，减少47.2%。

Conclusion: 以 JEPA 式潜在动作与世界模型取代几何重定向，可在无需手工映射的前提下复用第一视角人类操作先验，并以轻量适配低成本覆盖多具身，兼具效果与效率优势。

Abstract: Egocentric human videos offer a scalable alternative to costly robot demonstra- tions, yet transferring their manipulation priors to robot control remains chal- lenging. The commonly adopted retargeting-based approaches convert human wrist and finger poses into embodiment-specific action commands through hand- crafted geometric mappings. However, such mappings require meticulous recal- ibration across different embodiments and risk propagating hand-tracking errors into downstream robot control pipelines. To this end, we propose EgoJEPA, a three-stage framework that learns embodiment-agnostic representations from ego- centric human videos without rule-based human-to-robot retargeting: 1) Human- action pre-training learns effect-aware latent actions through JEPA-style visual prediction conditioned on hand-motion differences; 2) Latent-action-based world modeling jointly predicts future visual dynamics and latent actions, which decou- ples human prior pre-training from robot-specific control parameterizations; 3) Embodiment-specific tuning adapts the pretrained model to diverse robots through lightweight action adapters. Experiments on five real-world manipulation tasks across three robot embodiments demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of EgoJEPA. It achieves a 74% average success rate under the out-of-domain evalu- ation, compared with 65% for the retargeting baseline, while reducing cumulative cross-embodiment wall-clock cost by 47.2%, from 743.2 to 392.7 hours.

</details>


### [148] [Planning to Learn: Real-Time Robust Adaptive Control on the GPU with Guaranteed Future Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20925)
*Anutam Srinivasan,Siddhant Panse,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A-SLS：基于系统级合成的学习感知鲁棒自适应MPC框架，预测未来参数不确定性并保留估计更新相关性以构造更紧的可达集，配GPU加速求解器，实现四足全身体控制与Crazyflie在线参数学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统鲁棒自适应控制在陌生环境中规划时未考虑未来可获得的参数学习信息，导致保守性过高；同时难以扩展到高维系统，限制其在真实机器人上的实时应用。

Method: 提出A-SLS学习感知RAMPC框架，基于system-level synthesis (SLS)构建。核心是预测未来参数不确定性，并保留过程扰动、参数误差与估计更新之间的相关性，从而构造考虑未来信息增益的紧致保证可达集；另开发GPU加速求解器实现实时性能。

Result: 在非线性机器人系统上降低可达管保守性，保持实时性能；扩展到61状态、8个不确定参数、12控制输入的四足全身体控制；在硬件上让实体Crazyflie无人机在线学习未知动力学参数并实时稳控。

Conclusion: 将学习感知与鲁棒自适应MPC结合并GPU加速，可在保证安全的同时提升学习效率与可扩展性，具备真实硬件可行性。

Abstract: Robust adaptive control is vital for keeping robots safe while actively reducing uncertainty in unfamiliar settings. However, traditional methods are often conservative since they fail to account for guaranteed future parameter learning during planning and struggle to scale to high-dimensional systems. In this work, we present A-SLS, a learning-aware robust adaptive model predictive control (RAMPC) framework based on system-level synthesis. A-SLS forecasts future parameter uncertainty and preserves correlations between process disturbances, parameter error, and estimate updates to construct tight, guaranteed reachable sets that account for future information gain. We also develop a GPU-accelerated A-SLS solver to enable real-time performance. Across nonlinear robotic systems, A-SLS reduces reachable-tube conservativeness, maintains real-time performance, and scales to whole-body control for a quadruped with 61 states, 8 uncertain parameters, and 12 control inputs. We further demonstrate the approach on hardware, where a physical Crazyflie drone learns an uncertain dynamics parameter online to stabilize on the fly.

</details>


### [149] [LEAP: Gradient Flow Augmented Lagrangian Trajectory Optimization for Contact Constrained Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20936)
*César E. Ramos Chuquiure,Ryan Tsai,Yutong Zhou,Zihang Huang,Vansh Thakur,Akshunn Trivedi,Zachary Lin,Pearl Lin,Ruikai Yang,Challen Enninful Adu,Ram Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LEAP：用伪谱配点+增广拉格朗日原始-对偶梯度流的线性隐式积分器，求解含摩擦锥约束的腿足/操作机器人全身最优控制，在五个平台上全部求解成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人生成动力学可行运动需在整段轨迹上满足摩擦锥约束的非凸非线性OCP，现有求解器对初值敏感、随关节数规模扩展性差。

Method: 用伪谱配点离散OCP；不对其做Newton/SQP，而是对Powell–Hestenes–Rockafellar增广拉格朗日做原始-对偶梯度流；针对流的刚性，设计带Gauss–Newton度量的线性隐式积分器，并加入动量与基于merit函数的步长控制保证增广拉格朗日单调下降。

Result: 在五个平台（平面跳跃机器人到32自由度人形）的运动基准上，LEAP是唯一全部求解成功的方法；相对Crocoddyl与Aligator显著加速，并在更小算力下达到可跟踪参考，轨迹经高保真仿真与硬件跟踪验证。

Conclusion: 将增广拉格朗日的原始-对偶梯度流与结构化的线性隐式积分相结合，可作为接触丰富OCP的鲁棒高效求解范式，代码将开源。

Abstract: Generating dynamically feasible motions for legged and manipulating robots requires solving a large, nonlinear, non-convex optimal control problem (OCP) in which contact wrenches must satisfy friction-cone constraints throughout the motion, not only at isolated instants. Existing solvers are sensitive to the initial guess and scale poorly with the number of joints. This paper introduces LEAP, which transcribes the OCP with pseudospectral collocation and, instead of a Newton or SQP solve, integrates a primal--dual gradient flow of a Powell--Hestenes--Rockafellar augmented Lagrangian to compute a solution. Because the flow is stiff, this paper designs a linearly implicit integrator with a Gauss--Newton metric, momentum, and a merit-based step controller that keeps the augmented Lagrangian monotone. Across locomotion benchmarks on five robot platforms, from planar hoppers to 32-joint humanoids, LEAP is the only method to solve every tested problem, achieves substantial speedups over Crocoddyl and Aligator on several benchmarks, and reaches trackable references at smaller compute budgets. The resulting trajectories are validated by tracking them in a high-fidelity simulator and on hardware. We will make the code public upon final acceptance of this paper.

</details>


### [150] [Adaptive Prescribed Performance Trajectory Tracking for Uncertain Autonomous Underwater Vehicles With Input Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20957)
*Athanasios K. Gkesoulis,Christos Vlachos,George K. Fourlas,Charalampos P. Bechlioulis,George C. Karras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出低复杂度自适应预设性能控制器，通过名义与实际饱和输入失配在线放大性能漏斗，实现输入受限与耦合动态下的水下机器人轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下机器人水动力不确定性大、推进器权限有限，传统预设性能控制在输入饱和时漏斗约束不可行，导致性能保证失效。

Method: 以请求控制量与饱和后控制量之差驱动性能函数自适应调节，仅在执行能力不足时放宽漏斗；结合耦合不变集分析证明闭环信号有界、滤波跟踪误差满足预设界，并给出显式可行性条件。

Result: BlueROV2对比仿真显示，八字机动中自适应方案保持预设性能，而常规预设性能控制因输入限幅丧失可行性；实机水池实验验证了稳定轨迹跟踪与工程可实施性。

Conclusion: 输入失配驱动的自适应漏斗调节是解决饱和约束下预设性能可行性问题的简洁有效途径，具备低成本实机部署价值。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory tracking is essential for autonomous underwater vehicles operating with uncertain hydrodynamics and limited thruster authority. This paper develops a low-complexity adaptive prescribed performance controller for uncertain underwater vehicle dynamics with coupled and saturated inputs. The proposed method adapts the performance functions directly through the mismatch between the requested and saturated control inputs, enlarging the performance funnels only when the available actuation cannot meet the nominal specifications. A coupled invariant-set analysis establishes boundedness of all closed-loop signals, prescribed bounds on the filtered tracking errors, and explicit feasibility conditions. Comparative simulations on a BlueROV2 underwater vehicle show that the adaptive design maintains prescribed performance during a figure-eight maneuver, whereas conventional prescribed performance control loses feasibility under input limits. A real-time pool experiment further demonstrates stable trajectory tracking and the practical implementation of the proposed method on an underwater robotic platform.

</details>


### [151] [Line-Augmented cuVSLAM: Robust Visual Odometry in Low-Texture Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20958)
*Dara Molloy,Terry Wu,Wonju Lee,Abhilash Nand Kumar,David Adley,Conor Ferguson,Hesam Rabeti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: cuVSLAM 的点特征视觉 SLAM 在无纹理仓库环境中失效；作者将线特征（Plücker + Bartoli-Sturm 最小 4-DOF 表示）以鲁棒点-线残差与自适应信息权重的方式直接嵌入 cuVSLAM 的逐帧高斯-牛顿位姿求解和滑动窗口 BA，在 TUM RGB-D 与 Replica 上分别降低 38.5% 与 30.1% 的轨迹误差，零跟踪失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库与生产设施中的自主移动机器人恰好运行在点特征视觉里程计最易失效的场景：无纹理通道、单色墙面、重复货架与高光地面。但这类环境富含稳定、高对比度的直线边缘结构（货架立柱、门框、墙角、柜体轮廓），且该信号在宽视角变化下保持稳定。因此需要把线特征引入现有 SLAM 管线以弥补点特征稀缺时的估计退化。

Method: 作为 cuVSLAM（CUDA 加速视觉 SLAM）的线特征扩展：线用 Plücker 坐标做几何传输、用 Bartoli-Sturm 最小 4-DOF 正交表示做优化；采用 Cauchy 鲁棒点-线残差并推导解析雅可比，直接折叠进 cuVSLAM 的逐帧高斯-牛顿位姿求解，同一线状态在 Schur 补滑动窗口 BA 中联合优化。引入自适应信息权重，使点特征稀疏时线特征承担更多估计负担；实现上向现有求解器添加残差块而非新增第二估计器，禁用时精确退化为原点管线。评测为 RGB-D 模式、SE(3) Umeyama 对齐、不做尺度校正。

Result: 在 TUM RGB-D Freiburg3 的 22 条序列上平均绝对轨迹误差降低 38.5%；在 Replica 的 8 条房间与办公室序列上降低 30.1%，其中中位平移误差降低 38.9%、中位旋转误差降低 66.4%；所有测试配置中零跟踪失败。

Conclusion: 线特征可作为点特征管线的即插式补充，在结构化室内环境中显著提升 SLAM 精度与鲁棒性，且不改变原有估计器架构，禁用时完全退化为纯点管线，便于工程部署。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots in warehouses and production facilities operate in precisely the environments that defeat point-feature visual odometry: featureless aisles, uniform walls, repetitive racking and specular floors. Such environments are, however, rich in straight-edge structure: rack uprights, door frames, wall junctions and cabinet outlines present high-contrast linear edges stable across wide viewpoint changes. A line-feature extension to cuVSLAM, a CUDA-accelerated visual-SLAM system, is presented that recovers this signal. Lines are parameterised as Plücker coordinates for geometric transport and as the minimal 4-DOF orthonormal representation of Bartoli and Sturm for optimisation; a Cauchy-robust point-to-line residual with analytic Jacobians is folded directly into cuVSLAM's per-frame Gauss-Newton pose solve, and the same line state is jointly refined in the Schur-complement sliding-window bundle adjustment. An adaptive information weight causes lines to assume more of the estimation burden exactly as point features become scarce, and the extension adds residual blocks to existing solvers rather than introducing a second estimator, reducing exactly to the unmodified point pipeline when disabled. Evaluated in RGB-D mode under SE(3) Umeyama alignment with no scale correction, line features reduce mean absolute trajectory error by 38.5% over the 22 sequences of the TUM RGB-D Freiburg3 collection and by 30.1% over the 8 Replica room and office sequences, where median translation error falls by 38.9% and median rotation error by 66.4%, with zero tracking failures in any configuration tested.

</details>


### [152] [SOMA: Stack-of-Objects Manipulation using a Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20968)
*Stergios E. Bachoumas,Panagiotis Artemiadis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOMA：分层强化学习框架，实现四足机器人对多物体堆垛的全身体非抓取式三维推操作，并完成零样本仿真到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人缺乏专用操作器，难以在不可预测环境中执行重载物理操作；传统方法局限于二维平面推动。作者希望借助显式机身高度控制，解锁三维推动策略，从而将多物体堆垛作为统一系统进行操控，避免上层物体相对滑动或倾覆。

Method: 分层强化学习架构：高层操作策略使用简化结构化状态空间序列（S5）编码器，对长时程历史进行推理，并通过Beta分布预测有界的目标跟踪指令；低层运动策略动态调整腿部站立构型。整体为全身体非抓取式操作，并加入显式机身高度控制。

Result: 仿真基准中单物体重定位成功率达90.3%，动态双箱堆垛最高达90.1%，误差约束严格（位置误差0.05 m、姿态误差1°）。在Unitree Go1四足机器人上完成零样本sim-to-real迁移，跨越多种负载质量与几何形状，展示出主动机身高度调整以维持重载推动中的牵引力与平衡。

Conclusion: 显式机身高度控制与分层策略相结合，可将四足机器人从二维推动扩展到三维全身体非抓取式操作，并在真实机器人上零样本部署，为无臂四足机器人的重载操作提供可行路径。

Abstract: Executing heavy-duty physical manipulation in unpredictable environments remains a major challenge for quadrupedal robots lacking dedicated manipulators. This paper presents SOMA, a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for whole-body non-prehensile manipulation of multi-object stacks using a quadruped robot. Unlike traditional methods restricted to two-dimensional planar pushing, SOMA integrates explicit robot base-height control, unlocking full three-dimensional pushing strategies. This capability enables the quadruped to manipulate complex payload stacks as a unified system while preventing relative sliding or tipping of upper layers. The architecture factorizes control into a high-level manipulation policy using a Simplified Structured State Space Sequence (S5) encoder to reason over long-horizon temporal history and predict bounded target tracking commands via Beta distributions, and a low-level locomotion policy that dynamically adapts leg stance configurations. In extensive simulation benchmarks, SOMA achieves a $90.3\%$ success rate for single-object reposing and up to $90.1\%$ for dynamic two-box stacks under stringent error margins ($0.05\text{ m}$ position error, $1^\circ$ orientation error). Finally, we validate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a Unitree Go1 quadruped robot across diverse payload masses and geometries, demonstrating active base-height adjustments to maintain traction and balance during heavy pushing tasks.

</details>


### [153] [When a robot can trust reversing wind to find an odour source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20970)
*Junlong Shen,Shunsuke Shigaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究揭示风向推导的朝向（wind-derived heading）可用于引导四足机器人逆风搜寻气味源，甚至在风向反转后仍然有效，但其可靠性取决于羽流的输运历史；作者给出朝向正确性与净气味输运之间的精确几何条件，并用仿真区分感知误差与偏离名义流场的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统化学趋向/风趋性导航依赖当前风向指向气味源这一假设，但在风向反转或湍流中该假设失效。作者想回答：仅凭历史风信息推导的朝向在何时可信、何时会指向错误一侧，以及如何用间歇性的气味接触来修正朝向。

Method: 在一台初始朝向气味源的四足机器人上做风向反转实验；提出一个精确的几何条件，将"由风向推导的朝向是否正确"与羽流的净气味输运方向联系起来；通过仿真分离感知误差与对名义流场的偏离（均匀输运 vs. 随机/随机流场），并测试在新气味接触时重新获取朝向对间歇性接触搜索的改善。

Result: 保持初始朝向的控制器在每一次风向反转试验中都找到了气味源，而对照控制器从未稳定收敛到该位置；当交替风向夹角过小、单靠反转无法产生错误侧朝向时，错误侧朝向率从均匀输运下的 1.0% 上升到随机流场中的 4.7%；在气味接触间歇出现时，从新的气味接触重新获取朝向可改善仿真搜索表现。

Conclusion: 风向引导搜索需要两个不同条件：(1) 朝向必须得到输运几何的支持；(2) 需要足够频繁的间歇性气味接触来替换过时朝向。该几何条件可作为判断 wind-derived heading 何时可信的判据。

Abstract: A wind-derived heading can guide a robot towards an odour source even after the wind reverses, but its reliability depends on the plume's transport history. On a quadruped initially facing the source, holding the initial heading reached it in every reversing-wind trial, whereas comparison controllers never settled there. An exact geometric condition links heading correctness to net odour transport, and simulations separate sensing error from departures from nominal flow. Where the alternating wind directions are too close for reversal alone to produce wrong-side headings, the wrong-side rate rises from 1.0% under uniform transport to 4.7% in a stochastic flow. Re-acquiring headings from new odour contact improves simulated search when contact is intermittent. These results identify distinct requirements for wind-guided search: a heading supported by the transport geometry and sufficient intermittent contact to replace it.

</details>


### [154] [MoCaP: Mobile Code-as-Policies for Zero-Shot Mobile Manipulation from Passive Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20998)
*Yiyang Cai,Tatsuya Kamijo,Ninghao Lu,Zijin Hu,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoCaP 是一个 agentic 框架，将被动人类视频转化为空间记忆与交互先验，通过闭环 code-as-policy 协调导航与工具使用，使移动机械臂能完成难以示范的移动操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动人类视频蕴含大量人类经验，但存在两大障碍：一是从无结构视频中恢复可用知识，二是将知识适配到形态与运动学约束完全不同的机器人本体上。现有方法无法在真实移动操作任务中利用这类经验取得成功。

Method: MoCaP 从被动人类视频中抽取空间记忆（spatial memory）与人类接近/预备交互的行为线索（human approach prior），用于指导导航与"可操作位姿"的搜索；再以闭环 code-as-policy 方式生成并执行代码，协调规划与工具使用原语（tool-use primitives），在机器人几何与运动学约束下求解动作，形成端到端移动操作策略。

Result: 在真实移动机械臂上，两个室内移动操作任务各取得 80% 成功率，而现有方法均无法成功。消融显示：视频导出的空间记忆将物体导航成功率从 70% 提升到 100%；人类接近先验在不利起始位置把找到可触及基座位姿的比率从 0% 提升到 100%。

Conclusion: 被动人类视频可作为一种可迁移的经验来源，经由"空间记忆 + 交互先验 + 代码即策略"的智能体化管线，在无任务示范条件下显著提升移动操作的导航与操作成功率，为跨本体经验复用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Passive human videos of everyday activity offer robots access to a massive amount of human experience without requiring a demonstration for each task. However, using this experience requires recovering knowledge from unstructured video recordings and adapting it to a different body (i.e., the robot). To overcome these challenges, we present mobile code-as-policy (MoCaP), an agentic framework that turns passive human video recordings into guidance for an end-to-end mobile manipulation policy. MoCaP extracts spatial memory and cues of how people prepare to interact with objects, which inform navigation and the search for a manipulation-ready configuration. Guided by passive human experience and current observations, MoCaP uses a closed-loop code-as-policy to coordinate planning and tool use primitives to solve motions under the robot's geometry and kinematic constraints. On a real mobile manipulator, MoCaP achieves 80% success on each of two mobile manipulation tasks in an indoor environment whereas existing approaches cannot succeed at the task. Video-derived spatial memory improves object-navigation success from 70% to 100%, and human approach prior raises the rate of finding a reachable base pose from 0% to 100% at an unfavorable start location. Videos and code are available on the project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [155] [Slip-Induced Fall Suppression During Cornering for a Remotely Operated Motorcycle with a Rear-Wheel-Swing Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21089)
*Tadashi Sumioka,Kazushi Akimoto,Takuya Tsujimura,Sho Takayanagi,Kineo Tomura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将摩托车侧滑摔倒抑制建模为优化问题的方法，按车速与路面状况分配辅助控制输入（前后轮转向），并通过远程操控摩托车在低摩擦沥青与合成冰面实测验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧滑摔倒即使在熟练骑手下也难避免，是单车辆事故的主要成因，且此前尚无实用性抑制方案。

Method: 在作者早先提出的可同时控制前轮与后轮转向的摩托车模型基础上进行扩展，将摔倒抑制形式化为优化问题，依据车速与路面条件分配辅助控制输入；搭建远程操控摩托车平台进行实测。

Result: 两类场景测试均成功：5.5 m/s 低摩擦路面绕桩，以及合成冰面上模拟雨天井盖的转向机动；挑战性条件下均未摔倒。

Conclusion: 所提优化型辅助控制可有效抑制侧滑引起的摩托车摔倒，具备实际可行性。

Abstract: Slip-induced falls when riding motorcycles are difficult to avoid even for expert riders and represent a major cause of single-vehicle accidents. Despite their prevalence, no practical solution has yet been proposed to suppress this phenomenon. We previously introduced a motorcycle model applicable to systems with both front- and rear- wheel steering. In this paper, we extend that model and formulate the suppression of slip-induced falls as an optimization problem that distributes assistive control inputs according to vehicle speed and road surface conditions. To evaluate the proposed method, we developed a remotely operated motorcycle and conducted experiments under two scenarios: slalom driving on a low-friction surface at 5.5 m/s, and turning maneuvers on synthetic ice to simulate driving over manholes on rainy days. The results demonstrate that our approach successfully suppresses falls under these challenging conditions.

</details>


### [156] [Manifold Generative Annealing for Contact Constraint-aware Impedance Control with Reinforcement Learning Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21140)
*Zhilin He,Guoquan Qi,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Manifold Generative Annealing (MGA)框架，联合model-free RL策略提议与model-based rollout细化，在流形上对齐任务约束与真实接触响应，实现运动-阻抗优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的机器人任务在不确定交互动力学和物理约束下需要协调运动与阻抗。已有学习策略在接触条件偏离训练分布时易失败；model-based生成方法虽能做滚动时域轨迹优化，但缺乏结构化的行为引导，也无法同时兼顾约束几何与真实接触响应。

Method: MGA为无模型与模型结合的生成式运动-阻抗优化框架：(1) 将预训练RL策略组织为结构化序列提议，保留任务交互策略但不直接执行；(2) 通过model-based rollout对提议与探索样本进行评估，用于细化或单独执行选择；(3) 提出realization-aware manifold refinement，将细化银行的更新与任务约束及局部可达的物理响应对齐，使经验在线引导适应变化的接触条件。

Result: 在表面扫描、轴孔插入与人形机器人推压三类任务上验证，在标称与传感偏移条件下提升了阻抗控制的安全成功率。

Conclusion: 结构化策略提议与可达性感知流形细化相结合，可在接触条件变化下实现更安全、更鲁棒的运动-阻抗在线自适应。

Abstract: Contact-rich robotic tasks require coordinated motion and impedance under uncertain interaction dynamics and physical constraints. While recent methods on learned policies provide useful interaction strategies, they may fail when contact conditions differ from those encountered during training. Model-based generative methods show promising receding-horizon trajectories optimization results, but often lack structured behavioral guidance and refinement that jointly accounts for constraint geometry and actual contact responses. To this end, we propose Manifold Generative Annealing (MGA), a joint model-free and model-based generative framework for motion--impedance optimization. MGA first organizes pretrained reinforcement learning policies into structured sequence proposals, retaining task-specific interaction strategies without committing to their execution. It then evaluates these proposals alongside exploratory samples through model-based rollouts for refinement or separate execution selection. Crucially, realization-aware manifold refinement aligns updates from the refinement bank with both task constraints and locally achievable physical responses, allowing learned experience to guide online adaptation under changing contact conditions. Experiments cover surface scanning, peg insertion, and humanoid pushing, with improved safety success rate of impedance control under nominal and sensing-shift conditions.

</details>


### [157] [KODA: Kinematics--Observation Data Alignment for Humanoid Learning from Human Egocentric Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21152)
*Zibo Zhou,Yueru Chen,Junsong Wu,Weiji Xie,Qingyao Xu,Sheng Yin,Shiqing Wang,Jiyuan Shi,Siheng Chen,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KODA 将第一视角人类演示转换为全身 VLA 训练数据：先用稀疏跟踪器+采样式指令优化生成经仿真校验的全身轨迹并据此合成机器人视角观测，形成可复用的"规范记录"；再针对不同全身控制器优化指令、生成状态-动作监督，实现跨控制器复用。在 Unitree G1 四任务、SONIC/HEFT 两控制器上，人类演示覆盖但机器人数据缺失条件下宏平均成功率提升约 45–46 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练人形全身 VLA 策略需要昂贵的机器人演示（机器人视角观测+控制器兼容的全身状态-动作标签）。第一视角人类演示可规模化，但存在视角不对齐、运动线索稀疏问题；现有方法要么预训练后仍需机器人数据微调，要么生成与特定控制器绑定的指令而难以复用；且转换过程还需要与生成运动对齐的机器人视角观测。

Method: 以"可复用规范轨迹"为核心：(1) 用稀疏跟踪器配合基于采样的指令优化，生成经仿真校验的全身轨迹；(2) 由该轨迹直接确定机载相机位姿，合成对应的机器人视角观测；(3) 运动、观测与任务指令构成规范记录；(4) 对每个部署控制器，优化指令以跟踪存储运动，并记录相应动作标签与本体状态；(5) 切换控制器时保留规范记录，仅重新生成状态-动作监督，与匹配的机器人演示协同训练。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 上四个 loco-manipulation 任务、两种全身控制器（SONIC、HEFT）下评测。固定机器人数据不变时，对于人类演示覆盖但机器人数据未覆盖的条件，宏平均成功率分别提升 45.0 与 46.25 个百分点；两种控制器下该指标随人类数据预算增加呈上升趋势，显示可进一步扩展的潜力。

Conclusion: KODA 通过解耦"规范轨迹/观测"与"控制器相关监督"，实现第一视角人类演示向全身 VLA 数据的高效转换与跨控制器复用，显著缓解机器人演示依赖，并表现出数据规模扩展潜力。局限在于仍需仿真校验轨迹且验证集中于单一本体与两类控制器。

Abstract: Training humanoid whole-body vision-language-action (VLA) policies requires costly robot demonstrations with aligned robot-view observations and controller-compatible whole-body state-action labels. Egocentric human demonstrations offer scalable supervision but have misaligned views and sparse motion cues. Existing approaches either pretrain policies that still require robot data for adaptation or generate controller-specific commands that limit reuse. Conversion also requires robot-view observations aligned with the generated whole-body motion. We present KODA (Kinematics-Observation Data Alignment), which converts egocentric RGB demonstrations into whole-body VLA training data using a reusable canonical trajectory to guide robot-view synthesis and generate controller-specific whole-body state-action supervision. KODA first generates a simulator-checked whole-body trajectory using a sparse tracker with sampling-based command optimization. This generated trajectory directly determines the onboard camera poses used to synthesize corresponding robot-view observations. The motion, observations, and task instruction form a canonical record. For each deployment controller, KODA optimizes commands to track the stored motion and records the corresponding action labels and proprioceptive states. When switching controllers, KODA retains this canonical record and regenerates whole-body state-action supervision for co-training with matching robot demonstrations. We evaluate KODA on four Unitree G1 loco-manipulation tasks using two whole-body controllers, SONIC and HEFT. With robot data held fixed, macro-average success on conditions covered by human demonstrations but absent from robot data improves by 45.0 and 46.25 percentage points, respectively. Under both controllers, this metric shows an upward trend across the evaluated human-data budgets, suggesting potential for further scaling.

</details>


### [158] [When to Ask for Help: A Decision-Theoretic Account and Measurement Standard for Slow-Module Scheduling in Dual-System Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21160)
*Shilin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将双系统VLA中"慢模块调用"形式化为一个校准决策问题：统一固定比例、固定预算和历史门控三类调度为单一量——干预价值VoI；证明状态无关崩塌下最优调度为周期性的，增益受成本比ρ与崩塌离散度支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双系统VLA（快策略+慢高成本模块）的触发机制缺乏共享目标、缺乏实测代价，也没有将"调度质量"与"模块质量"分离的标准，导致慢模块调用停留在设计经验层面。

Method: 把调用决策形式化为校准决策问题，用VoI统一退化调度族；用更新过程（renewal）基线推导周期性最优调度；实测三类系统跨越三个数量级的ρ（FiS-VLA≈0.95，RoboICL≈10^3）；在LIBERO上做14组闭环对照（ρ=1.53）检验自适应触发、语言重写通道与回滚执行器；提出四测度无泄漏评估协议（929条部署轨迹）；给出检测受限前沿定理将检测质量转为可调度性判据；并用编译循环把实测价值函数转成每任务刷新周期。

Result: 低ρ预测获直接验证：同质崩塌下校准增益消失、异质崩塌下增益增大；自适应触发的查询分配与阈值规则预测完全一致，但语言重写通道在LIBERO-Spatial与LIBERO-Goal上均无法转化为成功率，仅"回滚重写"干预在两套件上零净损失，重写缺陷由13点扩大到42点；四测度协议把表观早期预警AUC从0.93降到0.62；编译循环所得每任务刷新周期与闭环oracle差距在2.4%以内。

Conclusion: 该工作为慢模块调用提供了统一的目标函数、可测代价与可分离的评估协议，把调度问题从设计观点转为可度量的决策问题；同时指出语言重写通道的收益瓶颈，而回滚式干预是稳健可用的选择。

Abstract: Dual-system vision-language-action (VLA) models pair a high-frequency fast policy with a slow, expensive module, and published trigger mechanisms for invoking it lack a shared objective, measured costs, and any standard separating schedule quality from module quality. We provide the missing account: invocation is formalized as a calibrated decision problem whose fixed-ratio, fixed-budget, and history-gated schedules are degenerate cases of one quantity, the Value of Intervention (VoI); a renewal baseline proves the optimal schedule periodic under state-independent collapse, with adaptive gains governed by the cost ratio $\rho$ and the dispersion of collapse. Measured systems span three orders of magnitude in $\rho$ (FiS-VLA $0.95$; RoboICL about $10^3$), and a direct test confirms the low-$\rho$ prediction: calibrated gains vanish under homogeneous collapse and grow with heterogeneity. Fourteen closed-loop arms on LIBERO test the contested middle of the continuum ($\rho=1.53$): the adaptive trigger allocates its queries exactly as the threshold rule predicts, yet the language-rewrite channel cannot convert them into success on either suite, and a revert actuator that undoes a failed rewrite is the only intervention with zero net downside on both LIBERO-Spatial and LIBERO-Goal, where the rewrite deficit deepens from thirteen to forty-two points. A four-measure leak-free protocol cuts apparent early-warning AUC from $0.93$ to $0.62$ on $929$ deployment traces, a detection-limited frontier theorem converts detection quality into a schedulability statement, and a compilation loop turns measured value functions into per-task refresh periods within $2.4\%$ of the closed-loop oracle. Together these results turn slow-module invocation from a design opinion into a measured decision.

</details>


### [159] [Evo-DexBench: Benchmarking Generalization across Related Tasks for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21180)
*Bing Cheng,Yiran Mao,Zewei Ye,Yifan Jin,Xinyi Chen,Ruifeng Tu,Shuhuai Wang,Tao Lin,Zhimu Zhou,Yuxin Du,Fangwei Zhong,Junchi Yan,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Evo-DexBench 灵巧操作基准，基于 Core-Gen 原则控制源任务相关性与目标任务示范数量，包含 30 个任务、6 个功能族、1,248 条多模态示范，最佳方法平均成功率仅 38.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作基准提供过多目标任务示范，且源任务与目标任务差异不受控，无法区分"知识迁移"与"任务记忆"。作者希望构建能真正检验策略复用跨任务功能交互知识能力的评测平台。

Method: 提出 Core-Gen 原则：Core 任务为共享功能交互的相关源任务，Gen 任务为同功能族内仅提供少量示范的新目标任务。据此构建 Evo-DexBench，包含 6 个功能族共 30 个任务，配套 VR 遥操作流水线采集示范，数据集覆盖每任务 3 个日常场景，共 1,248 条多模态示范，使用带 123 个触觉单元（taxels）的仿真 Shadow Hand 采集。

Result: 最佳方法平均成功率仅 38.5%。两个任务上，Core+Gen 联合训练较"先 Core 训练再 Gen 微调"分别提升 12% 与 38%。功能匹配的 Core 数据带来的成功率增益大于不匹配数据。

Conclusion: Evo-DexBench 通过受控的源任务相关性与目标任务暴露量，有效暴露现有方法在功能交互知识迁移上的不足；联合训练与功能匹配的源数据有助于提升泛化。数据集与基准已公开。

Abstract: General-purpose dexterous manipulation requires policies to transfer knowledge from related tasks to new tasks with only a few target demonstrations. Existing benchmarks often provide too many target demonstrations. They also leave the differences between source and target tasks uncontrolled. As a result, they cannot clearly distinguish knowledge transfer from task memorization. Our key insight is the Core-Gen principle. Core tasks are related tasks that share a functional interaction and provide source knowledge, while a Gen task is a novel target within the same functional family that has only a few demonstrations. This setup tests whether a policy can reuse functional interaction knowledge. Based on this principle, we propose Evo-DexBench, which controls both source-task relatedness and target-task exposure. It comprises 30 tasks in six functional families and a VR teleoperation pipeline. The resulting dataset covers three everyday scenes per task. It contains 1,248 multimodal demonstrations collected with simulated Shadow Hands equipped with 123 taxels. Experiments show that even the best-performing method achieves an average success rate of only 38.5%, highlighting the challenges existing methods face in transferring functional interaction knowledge. Further analysis shows that, across the two tasks, joint Core+Gen training improves performance by 12% and 38%, respectively, over training on Core followed by fine-tuning on Gen. This indicates that joint training makes better use of Core knowledge and that functionally matched Core data yields greater gains in success rate than mismatched data. We release the Evo-DexBench dataset and benchmark to support the evaluation of dexterous manipulation policies' generalization across related tasks.

</details>


### [160] [LIN-SLAM: Lightweight LiDAR--Inertial Neural SLAM for Real-Time Onboard Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21199)
*Shiqi Che,Yijie Liu,Shuo Wang,Wenxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LIN-SLAM，一种将高频率几何LiDAR-惯性估计与神经隐式跟踪、持续建图和全局优化耦合的混合SLAM方法，在保持高精度定位的同时实现移动平台上的实时神经隐式后端运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何LiDAR-惯性SLAM效率高但缺乏连续地图表示与全局优化能力；神经隐式SLAM具备这些能力但计算开销大，难以在嵌入式移动平台上实时运行。作者希望结合两者优势，实现兼具精度与实时性的机载SLAM。

Method: 提出混合式架构LIN-SLAM：以前端高频率LiDAR-惯性几何估计提供高效状态估计，后端引入神经隐式跟踪、持续建图与全局优化模块，二者耦合以兼顾效率与连续地图表示。

Result: 在3个数据集、12个代表性序列（32/64/128线LiDAR配置）上，LIN-SLAM在所评估的几何与神经隐式SOTA方法中取得最低总体ATE均值，同时相比现有神经隐式SLAM显著降低运行时间。在Unitree Go2（NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX + 16线LiDAR）上完成1.47 km室内重复走廊轨迹的实时定位与神经建图，终点闭合误差仅0.141 m。

Conclusion: LIN-SLAM据称是首个在移动机器人平台上实现实时在线运行、同时保持SOTA定位精度的神经隐式后端LiDAR-惯性SLAM系统。属于增量式、面向特定平台的SLAM/建图工作，未展示跨场景泛化能力，代码待接受后开源。

Abstract: Geometric light detection and ranging (LiDAR)--inertial simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) provides efficient state estimation but lacks the continuous map representation and global refinement capabilities of neural implicit SLAM, while neural implicit methods remain computationally expensive. We propose Lightweight LiDAR--Inertial Neural SLAM (LIN-SLAM), a hybrid method that couples high-rate geometric LiDAR--inertial estimation with neural implicit tracking, continual mapping, and global refinement for accurate real-time onboard SLAM. Experiments on twelve representative sequences from three datasets spanning 32-, 64-, and 128-channel LiDAR configurations show that LIN-SLAM achieves the lowest overall mean absolute trajectory error (ATE) among the evaluated state-of-the-art geometric and neural implicit methods, while substantially reducing runtime compared with existing neural implicit SLAM methods. On a Unitree Go2 equipped with an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX and a 16-channel LiDAR, LIN-SLAM sustains real-time localization and neural mapping over a 1.47-km indoor trajectory through challenging repetitive corridor environments, with an endpoint closure error of only 0.141~m. To the best of our knowledge, LIN-SLAM is the first LiDAR--inertial SLAM system with a neural implicit backend to achieve real-time online operation on a mobile robotic platform while maintaining state-of-the-art localization accuracy. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [161] [An Agentic Framework for Proactive Robots with Procedural Assistance Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21211)
*Dohyun Lee,Minho Park,Jeonghoon Park,Dongyoon Hwang,Junha Hyung,Sungwon Hwang,Byungkun Lee,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于记忆的VLM+VLA主动辅助框架：用程序性辅助记忆（PAM，有限状态机+辅助规则+事件转移）驱动任务预测，并由反思VLM每日更新记忆（无需权重更新），在5天桌面辅助任务中随经验积累持续提升精度/召回/F1并将首次触发延迟从约5秒降至1秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动机器人需在用户明确请求前预判需求。仅靠常识推断意图存在歧义：同一行为在不同用户处可能对应不同需求，导致误援助。作者观察到真实日常routine具有重复模式，学习这些模式可降低主动决策的歧义性。

Method: 记忆式agentic框架：VLM任务预测器读取PAM与当前观测，决定何时辅助及执行何种任务；VLA执行器完成预测任务。PAM建模为活动阶段上的有限状态机，含每状态辅助规则与事件驱动转移；反思VLM根据每日交互轨迹修订PAM，不更新权重、不重训练。

Result: 5天桌面辅助评测中，无记忆基线偏保守、主动任务召回有限；本系统随经验累积一致提升，取得最高precision、recall与F1，首次触发延迟由Day1约5秒降至Day5约1秒；并通过持续记忆反思适应用户routine变化，在单次记忆更新后即从记忆-行为错位中恢复。

Conclusion: 程序性记忆+反思机制可有效缓解主动辅助的意图歧义，实现免训练、可在线适应的持续改进，为长期个性化主动机器人提供可行范式。

Abstract: Proactive robots should anticipate user needs and provide assistance before being explicitly asked. A straightforward approach is to infer human intentions from commonsense knowledge, but the same observed behavior can imply different needs across users, potentially leading to unwanted assistance. We observe that everyday routines in the real world exhibit recurring patterns, so learning these patterns can reduce ambiguity in proactive decisions. Based on this, we propose a memory-based agentic framework in which a VLM task predictor reads a Procedural Assistance Memory (PAM) alongside the current observation to decide when to assist and what task to perform, and a VLA executor performs the predicted task. PAM is structured as a finite-state machine over activity phases, per-state assistance rules, and event-driven transitions; a reflection VLM revises PAM from each day's interaction trace without weight updates or retraining. In our 5-day desk-assistance evaluation, memory-free baselines remain conservative, with limited recall on proactive tasks. Our system improves consistently with accumulated experience, achieving the highest precision, recall, and F1 and reducing first-fire delay from approximately 5 seconds on Day 1 to 1 second by Day 5. Our approach also adapts to changes in the user's routine through continued memory reflection, recovering from memory--routine misalignment after a single memory update. The project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [162] [Vision-Guided Closed-Loop Autonomous Navigation of Light-Driven Janus Microbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21222)
*Kanhu Charan Andia,Harsh Prashant Katole,Parth Nitinkumar Bhosale,Hemant Kumar,Dhruv Pratap Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作为约3 μm的光驱动立方状赤铁矿/TiO2 Janus 微机器人构建了视觉引导的闭环导航框架：以自训练 YOLOv11 实现实时视觉定位，结合离散航点磁转向完成精确路径跟踪（>90% 轨迹偏差 <6 μm），并具备自由手绘轨迹、迷宫自主导航、A* 动态重规划避障以及捕获并运输多枚货物的任务执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微米尺度下的自主导航高度困难：定位与驱动分辨率受限，且环境与障碍物动态变化，传统开环磁场操控难以沿复杂轨迹精确行进，也难以在动态环境中实时重规划。作者希望把可编程轨迹控制、自主路径规划、环境实时适应和任务执行统一到一个光驱动 Janus 微机器人平台上。

Method: 硬件为约 3 μm 的光驱动立方状 hematite/TiO2 Janus 微机器人；感知端用自定义训练的 YOLOv11 检测模型做实时视觉定位；控制端采用离散化航点（waypoint）磁转向构成闭环；规划端引入 A* 进行实时路径重规划以规避多枚独立游动的 5 μm 球形 Janus 微马达；任务层实现多货物颗粒的选择性捕获、自主运输与定点投放。

Result: 在测试的多种路径几何下，超过 90% 的实测轨迹偏差保持在 6 μm 以内；框架支持任意用户手绘自由轨迹；可在物理受限迷宫中自主导航；在动态环境中通过 A* 实时重规划避开多枚自主游动的 5 μm 微马达；最终实现选择性捕获多枚货物颗粒并自主运输、投放到预定位置，同时规避动态障碍。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一个单一光驱动 Janus 微机器人系统内的集成平台，同时涵盖可编程轨迹控制、自主路径规划、实时环境适应与任务执行，为微尺度自主运输与操作提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Artificial microbots capable of precise and autonomous navigation could provide versatile platforms for microscale transport, manipulation, and task execution in complex environments. However, reliable navigation becomes increasingly challenging at the micrometre scale, particularly when the surrounding environment changes dynamically. Here, we develop a vision-guided closed-loop navigation framework for light-driven cube-shaped hematite/TiO2 Janus microbots with a characteristic size of approximately 3 {\mu}m. Real-time visual localization using a custom-trained YOLOv11 object-detection model is integrated with discretized waypoint-based magnetic steering to enable precise navigation along complex prescribed trajectories, with more than 90% of the measured trajectory deviations remaining below 6 {\mu}m across the tested path geometries. The framework further enables arbitrary user-defined free-hand trajectories and autonomous navigation through a physically confined maze. In dynamically changing environments, A* path planning enables real-time path replanning while avoiding multiple independently swimming 5 {\mu}m spherical Janus micromotors. Finally, the microbot selectively captures multiple cargo particles and autonomously transports and delivers them to a predefined location while simultaneously avoiding dynamic obstacles. These results establish an integrated platform combining programmable trajectory control, autonomous path planning, real-time environmental adaptation, and task execution within a single light-driven Janus microbot system.

</details>


### [163] [CLIS-MPPI: Closed-Loop Model Predictive Path Integral Control With Initial-State Sampling Under Execution Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21239)
*Zidong Liu,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CLIS-MPPI，通过将规划延迟建模为执行时刻初始状态分布并采样rollout，同时在执行阶段施加基于rollout的局部反馈修正，缓解采样类MPPI规划中的规划-执行状态错配问题，并在UR5e实机上验证安全性与一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI等采样规划依赖并行rollout实现实时非线性控制，但真实系统中控制输入往往在不可忽略的规划延迟后才执行，导致前向仿真所用状态与执行时刻实际状态不一致，进而降低规划精度甚至引发碰撞等不安全行为。

Method: 将规划延迟显式建模为执行时刻可行初始状态上的概率分布，从采样初始状态出发进行rollout优化（初始状态采样），并在控制执行过程中加入基于rollout的局部反馈修正，构成闭环MPPI（CLIS-MPPI）。

Result: 在6-DoF UR5e机械臂窄通道避障+末端目标到达任务中，5次实机运行：标准MPPI有2次碰撞，CLIS-MPPI全部5次无碰撞。在各自无碰撞运行上平均（MPPI 3次、CLIS-MPPI 5次）：任务完成时间25.4s→24.4s，末端路径长度0.456m→0.425m，关节位置与关节速度RMSE由0.100rad、0.108rad/s降至0.017rad、0.011rad/s。

Conclusion: CLIS-MPPI能有效缓解执行时刻状态错配，在真实机械臂任务中同时提升安全性、规划-执行一致性与运动平滑度。

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planning such as Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control has become attractive for robotics due to its ability to handle nonlinear dynamics and non-smooth objectives without gradient information, while leveraging parallel rollout computation for real-time performance. However, in real-world robotic systems, the optimized control input is often applied after a non-negligible planning delay, causing a mismatch between the state used in forward simulation and the actual state at control execution, which can significantly degrade planning accuracy and may even lead to unsafe behavior such as collisions. This paper proposes Closed-Loop MPPI with Initial-State Sampling (CLIS-MPPI), which explicitly addresses execution-time state mismatch by modeling planning delay as a distribution over plausible execution-time initial states, performing rollout optimization from sampled initial states, and applying a rollout-based local feedback correction during control execution. The proposed method is validated through real-world experiments on a 6-DoF UR5e manipulator in a narrow-passage task involving obstacle avoidance and end-effector target reaching. Across 5 real-world runs of the same task, standard MPPI collided in 2 runs, whereas CLIS-MPPI remained collision-free in all 5 runs. Averaged over all collision-free runs for each method (3 for MPPI and 5 for CLIS-MPPI), CLIS-MPPI reduced the average task completion time from 25.4s to 24.4s and the average end-effector path length from 0.456 m to 0.425 m. It also reduced the overall joint-position and joint-velocity root mean square errors (RMSEs) from 0.100 rad and 0.108 rad/s to 0.017 rad and 0.011 rad/s, respectively. These results demonstrate improved safety, planning-execution consistency, and motion smoothness.

</details>


### [164] [LICOA: Learning Instruction-Conditioned Optical Actions for Mobile Spatial Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21282)
*Yuchen Deng,Shunkai Zhou,Ruixuan Zhu,Haibin Ling,Bingyao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LICOA将投影光视为具身动作：给定语言指令与视觉观测，系统自主决定底盘移动、2自由度反射镜指向与投影尺度，构建匹配仿真具身与150任务/7500片段语料，在未见真实环境中评测，最佳迁移动作模型取得57.50%成功率与40.64% SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间增强现实（SAR）系统通常假设投影位姿、目标区域或运动路径已固定或预先指定，缺乏"语言指令驱动、自主决策投影位置与尺度"的具身能力；同时该方向缺乏专用训练数据与统一评测协议。

Method: 构建LICOA平台：差速驱动底盘负责房间尺度接近与重定位，2自由度可控反射镜加投影缩放实现光束的快速局部控制；设计共享接口，将同步的外部与机载RGB相机、机器人控制状态映射为物理光学动作，并适配预训练动作模型。配套搭建匹配仿真具身与150任务、7500渲染片段的语料，仅用该语料训练后在真实环境评测。

Result: 12任务、480次完整闭环真实执行中，最佳适配动作模型成功率57.50%、SPL 40.64%，而相同架构从零训练仅8.33%与5.15%；额外150次真实消融实验表明双相机视角与底盘-反射镜协同控制均不可缺。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了把投影光作为具身动作的物理平台、共享感知与光学动作接口、训练语料及真实世界评测协议，验证了指令条件化光学动作策略的可行性。

Abstract: Spatial augmented reality (SAR) augments physical environments with projected content, but most systems assume that the projector pose, target region, or motion path has been fixed or specified. We instead make projected light an embodied action: given a language instruction and current visual observations, a system must decide how to move and where and at what scale to place the projection. We introduce LICOA to realize this capability. Its differential-drive chassis provides room-scale approach and repositioning, while a steerable mirror with two degrees of freedom (2DoF) and projection scale provide rapid local control of the beam. A shared interface maps synchronized external and onboard RGB cameras with robot control state to physical optical actions across pretrained action models. Because instruction-conditioned optical action lacks dedicated training data and a common evaluation protocol, we construct a matched simulation embodiment and a corpus with 150 tasks and 7,500 rendered episodes, then evaluate models trained only on this corpus in previously unseen real-world environments. Across 480 complete closed-loop real-world execution trials (rollouts) on 12 tasks, the best adapted action model achieves a 57.50% success rate and 40.64% success weighted by path length, compared with 8.33% and 5.15% for the same architecture trained from scratch. A further 150 real-world ablation rollouts support the need for both camera views and coordinated control of the chassis and mirror. These results establish a physical platform, shared perception and optical action interface, corpus, and real-world evaluation protocol for treating projected light as an embodied action.

</details>


### [165] [MR-MPCC: Model Predictive Contouring Control for Robotic Manipulator Tracking under Moving Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21292)
*Chonghao Cheng,Tiancheng Li,Jonathan Woolfrey,Liang Zhao,Shoudong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MR-MPCC：面向速度控制机械臂在移动参考系扰动下的分层MPCC框架，上层优化路径进度并惩罚轮廓/姿态误差，下层用约束RMRC映射为关节速度，在UR5e与ROS2-MuJoCo上验证优于PI与时序跟踪MPC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在加工、手术自动化等紧公差操作中，参考系运动会破坏传统时序索引控制器（如跟踪MPC）与参考轨迹的固定时间对应关系，导致路径相对误差累积。

Method: 分层控制：上层MPCC在6-DoF末端位姿调节下，同时优化路径进度并惩罚轮廓误差与姿态误差；下层约束RMRC将笛卡尔twist映射为满足关节与安全约束的可行关节速度；实现于ROS2–MuJoCo流水线，路径系由电磁传感器扰动并跟踪。

Result: 在UR5e上于匹配扰动条件下，相比PI控制与时序索引跟踪MPC，MR-MPCC取得更低的几何路径跟踪误差与更小的移动路径系偏差。

Conclusion: 通过以轮廓误差为核心的MPCC加约束RMRC分层结构，可在移动参考系扰动下改善机械臂路径跟踪性能，为高公差操作任务提供可行方案。

Abstract: Maintaining small path-tracking errors under moving reference frames is essential for robotic manipulation tasks with tight execution tolerances, such as machining and surgical automation. Conventional time-indexed controllers, including tracking Model Predictive Control (MPC), rely on a fixed temporal correspondence with the reference trajectory and may therefore accumulate path-relative error under abrupt reference-frame motion or timing mismatch. This paper presents MR-MPCC, a Model Predictive Contouring Control (MPCC) framework for velocity-controlled robotic manipulators with 6-DoF end-effector pose regulation under Moving-Reference disturbances. A hierarchical control architecture is adopted, in which an upper-level MPCC optimizes path progress while penalizing contouring and orientation errors, and a constrained resolved motion rate control (RMRC) layer maps the commanded Cartesian twist to feasible joint velocities under joint-level and safety constraints. MR-MPCC is implemented in a ROS2--MuJoCo pipeline and validated on a UR5e platform under moving-reference disturbances, where the path frame is perturbed and tracked by electromagnetic sensors. Compared with PI control and time-indexed tracking MPC under matched disturbance, MR-MPCC achieves lower geometric path-following error and smaller deviations in the moving path frame, demonstrating improved path-following performance under reference-frame motion.

</details>


### [166] [CQDC-Warp: GPU-Accelerated Differentiable Quasi-Dynamic Simulation for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21349)
*Yanchen Ding,Jiayi Chen,Jianfeng Xu,Wei Zhao,Li Yi,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CQDC-Warp 是一个 GPU 加速的可微准动力学仿真器，用于批量 MPC 下的接触引导轨迹优化，在 17 物体 Allegro 手内旋转基准上取得 79.8% 成功率，并将轨迹生成时间缩短 27–50 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作的轨迹合成对下游机器人学习至关重要。基于梯度的规划虽有前景，但联合优化运动与接触决策仍困难，且多数现有规划器在 CPU 上运行、吞吐量有限。

Method: 提出 CQDC-Warp：GPU 加速的可微准动力学仿真器，支持批量 MPC。采用接触引导轨迹优化——规划前指定期望的机器人-物体接触，通过软代价鼓励，而接触几何与摩擦响应由仿真动力学决定，无需预设接触模式时序。

Result: 在 17 物体 Allegro 手内旋转基准的 17,408 次试验中成功率达 79.8%，优于 CFree (73.7%) 与 IMPACT (65.2%)，平均轨迹生成时间分别降低 27.0 倍与 50.2 倍。单张 RTX 4090 上 2,048 个并行世界的前向/反向吞吐达 2.06×10^5 与 2.25×10^5 world-steps/s。额外实验涵盖 LEAP、Shadow 手操作、环境接触利用、双臂操作、全身控制及 Sharpa 手上的真机轨迹回放。

Conclusion: 可微、GPU 批量的准动力学仿真结合接触引导的软代价优化，可在无接触模式时序的前提下高效生成接触丰富操作轨迹，并具备跨平台与真机迁移潜力。

Abstract: Efficient synthesis of contact-rich manipulation trajectories is essential for downstream robot learning. Gradient-based planning offers a promising approach, but jointly optimizing motion and contact decisions remains challenging. Meanwhile, many existing planners run on CPUs with limited throughput. We present CQDC-Warp, a GPU-accelerated differentiable quasi-dynamic simulator for batched model predictive control (MPC). We use it to study contact-guided trajectory optimization, in which intended robot--object contacts are specified before planning and encouraged through soft costs, while the simulated dynamics determine contact geometry and frictional response without a prescribed contact-mode schedule. Across $17{,}408$ trials on the $17$-object Allegro in-hand rotation benchmark, our framework achieves $79.8\%$ success, compared with $73.7\%$ for CFree and $65.2\%$ for IMPACT, and reduces mean trajectory-generation time by factors of $27.0$ and $50.2$, respectively. On a single NVIDIA RTX 4090 with $2{,}048$ parallel worlds, forward and backward throughput reach $2.06\times10^5$ and $2.25\times10^5$ world-steps per second, respectively. Further experiments cover manipulation with LEAP and Shadow hands, environmental contact exploitation, bimanual manipulation, whole-body control, and real-world trajectory replay on a Sharpa hand.

</details>


### [167] [A Black-Box Positioning Solution for Effortless Integration in Wearable Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21353)
*Anh-Van Vu,Changmin Sung,Dongsoo Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PDRS，一个即插即用、黑盒化的行人航位推算（PDR）模块，将微处理器与 IMU 集成于紧凑低功耗单元，片上实时完成传感器融合并通过 I2C/SPI/UART 输出位置，实现软硬件协同设计以降低集成门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 GPS 退化环境（室内、遮挡、地下）中准确定位仍很困难；基于惯性传感器的 PDR 是有前景的替代方案，但传统实现需要大量算法设计与系统集成工作，导致开发周期长、成本高。作者希望提供一种封装好的硬件模块，使开发者无需深厚专业知识即可快速接入可穿戴与 IoT 平台。

Method: 采用软硬件协同设计：在紧凑低功耗模块内集成微处理器与 IMU，片上实时运行传感器融合算法（步态检测与航向/位移推算，抑制漂移），通过标准接口（I2C、SPI、UART）以黑盒方式对外提供位置更新，无需外部主机参与计算。

Result: 在多样化条件下完成实验评估：1250 步中步态检测误差仅 0.16%，行走距离误差 1.87%；系统延迟低至 2.5 ms，功耗约 52.35 mA，表现出较强鲁棒性与低延迟、低功耗特性。

Conclusion: PDRS 通过整体式软硬件协同设计在性能与集成便利性上均优于传统纯算法研究，降低了技术门槛，可加速可穿戴与 IoT 场景的原型开发。未来工作聚焦于提升精度、降低功耗与体积，并引入 AI 驱动方法以更好抑制漂移。

Abstract: Accurate localization is critical for a wide range of applications, including navigation, augmented reality, safety management, and healthcare. However, achieving accurate positioning remains challenging, especially in GPS-degraded environments. Pedestrian Dead Reckoning (PDR), which estimates position based on inertial sensor data, offers a promising alternative but typically demands substantial effort in algorithm design and system integration. This complexity often leads to prolonged development timelines and elevated costs. To address these challenges, we present the PDR Sensor (PDRS), a plug-and-play, black-box PDR module optimized for seamless integration into wearable and IoT platforms. PDRS integrates a microprocessor and an inertial measurement unit (IMU) within a compact, low-power module. It executes real-time sensor fusion onboard, providing position updates via standard interfaces (I2C, SPI, UART). Experimental evaluations demonstrate its high robustness, achieving a step detection error of just 0.16% over 1250 steps and a traveled distance error of 1.87% under diverse conditions. The system further exhibits low latency (2.5 ms) and modest power consumption (52.35 mA). Unlike conventional studies, the PDRS leverages a holistic co-design of hardware and software to enhance performance and integration aspects, simplifying adoption without requiring deep expertise. By lowering technical barriers, PDRS enables developers and researchers to prioritize innovation in IoT and wearable technologies, accelerating prototyping, reducing development time, and advancing industry progress. Future work aims to enhance accuracy, reduce power usage and module size, and integrate AI-driven techniques to better mitigate drift.

</details>


### [168] [Hierarchical Building-Floo-Room Framework for Privacy-Preserving Autonomous Delivery in Large-Scale Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21361)
*Xu Xiaoji,Luo Rui,Chen Jinhua,Xiang Changying,Zhang Leihan,Liao Zhongyuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Building-Floor-Room 三层递送框架，将大尺度楼宇自主配送建模为建筑感知的 CVRP 扩展，并配套非交互式隐私保护硬件、可达性距离与闭环建图/定位/导航系统，在真实多传感器机器人平台上完成多层配送验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有楼宇自主配送研究偏重算法本身，缺少与真实机器人平台的集成与实机验证；同时多数方法忽略建筑物的层级结构以及房间级隐私约束，导致实际可用性受限。此外，建筑内部多层、多房间的连通性使全局任务分配与路径规划复杂度极高，需要层级化分解来降低优化规模。

Method: 1) 以 Building-Floor-Room 抽象对问题分层分解，降低优化复杂度；2) 提出 building-aware 的 CVRP 扩展，显式建模建筑间/楼层间连通性、房间级准入约束与隐私敏感区域，实现全局可行且隐私合规的任务分配与路径规划；3) 基于该模型评估不同楼宇布局下的配送效率并优化特定配置的仓库选址；4) 执行层设计非交互式隐私保护交付硬件，配合可达性感知距离度量与闭环建图-定位-导航系统，保证规划路径与物理导航一致。

Result: 在真实多传感器机器人平台上完成验证，实现可靠的多楼层配送，路径合理性与整体效率均有提升；框架可用于比较不同楼宇布局的配送效率并给出仓库选址优化。

Conclusion: 层级化建模加实机闭环执行是楼宇级自主配送落地的可行路径；将隐私约束与建筑拓扑直接纳入组合优化，可在保证合规的同时维持调度效率。

Abstract: Autonomous delivery in large scale buildings requires coordinated planning across multiple spatial levels, including building, floor, and room, while ensuring scheduling efficiency. Existing methods often focus solely on algorithmic development, neglecting integration with physical robotic platforms, and rarely validate performance in real world scenarios. Many approaches also overlook hierarchical building structures and room level privacy constraints, limiting practical applicability. To address these challenges, this paper presents a hierarchical delivery framework based on a Building-Floor-Room abstraction, which decomposes the problem across multiple levels to significantly reduce optimization complexity. We formulate a building aware extension of the capacitated vehicle routing problem (CVRP) that explicitly models inter building and inter floor connectivity, room level access constraints, and privacy sensitive areas, enabling globally feasible and privacy compliant task assignment and routing. Based on this hierarchical model, the framework can evaluate delivery efficiency across different building layouts and optimize depot placement for specific configurations. At the execution level, we introduce a novel hardware design for non interactive, privacy preserving delivery, supported by reachability aware distances and a closed loop mapping, localization, and navigation system, ensuring consistency between planned routes and physical navigation. The proposed framework is validated on a real multi sensor robotic platform, demonstrating reliable multi floor delivery with improved path rationality and overall efficiency.

</details>


### [169] [LTM: Latent Transition Based In-Context Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21362)
*Qixin Sun,WenLong Gao,Xiansheng Chen,Zhongyuan Wang,Si Liu,Pengwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Transition Memory (LTM)，将历史建模为相邻观测间的紧凑潜变量转移，在 π0.5 上实例化为 LTM-π，无需记忆专用语义标注或外部推理模型即可实现长时程机器人操作记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作常需回忆当前不可见的交互历史，但现有 VLA 策略受限于有限上下文窗口，且依赖记忆专用语义标注或外部推理模型进行显式记忆推理，代价高、难部署。因此需要一种可在策略内部增量运行、无需额外标注的历史记忆机制。

Method: LTM 将历史表示为连续观测之间的潜变量转移（latent transition），而非直接存储原始帧或语义记忆条目。实例化 LTM-π：使用冻结的潜动作编码器（frozen latent action encoder）、轻量适配器（lightweight adapter）与因果 KV 缓存（causal KV caching）实现增量式推理；采用延迟记忆准入（delayed memory admission）策略；配合 DreamDojo 提供的转移数据训练。整个流程不依赖记忆专用语义标注，也不调用外部推理模型。

Result: RoboMME 成功率 58.54%，LIBERO-Mem 80.5%，在无外部推理模型/无记忆专用标注的基线中最优。消融显示转移内容优于纯时序线索，延迟记忆准入具效率优势。RoboMME 示范数据扩至 10 倍时成功率从 57.67% 升至 87.50%；Cup Swap 诊断表明过程监督对时序组合起作用。200 token 记忆预算下成功率仍为 84.00%（无压缩为 87.50%）。真机计数任务成功率从 11.25% 提升到 82.50%。

Conclusion: 潜变量转移记忆在效果、可扩展性与效率上均得到验证，能够在有界上下文下承载复杂时序信息，并有效支撑真实机器人控制，为长时程 VLA 记忆提供了一种无需标注与外部推理的轻量方案。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires remembering interactions that are no longer visible. We introduce Latent Transition Memory (LTM), which stores history as compact latent transitions between consecutive observations. Instantiated on $\pi_{0.5}$ as LTM-$\pi$, our method uses a frozen latent action encoder, a lightweight adapter, and causal KV caching for incremental inference without memory-specific semantic annotations or external reasoning-model calls. With DreamDojo transitions, LTM-$\pi$ achieves 58.54% success on RoboMME and 80.5% on LIBERO-Mem, performing best among baselines without external reasoning models or memory-specific annotations. Ablations support the contribution of transition content beyond temporal cues and the efficiency of delayed memory admission. Scaling RoboMME demonstrations tenfold improves success from 57.67% to 87.50%, while a Cup Swap diagnostic highlights the role of process supervision in temporal composition. Under a 200-token memory budget, success remains 84.00%, versus 87.50% without compression. Finally, real-robot counting success improves from 11.25% to 82.50%. Together, these results demonstrate the effectiveness, scalability, and efficiency of latent transition memory, highlighting its capacity for complex temporal information, bounded-context operation, and real-world robotic control.

</details>


### [170] [From Optical Flow to Safety: Visual Safety Certification via Scale-Free Relative Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21374)
*Bazeela Banday,Jishnu Keshavan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于单目光流的无尺度相对运动表示与鲁棒视觉碰撞锥CBF（RV-C³BF），通过在线Concurrent Learning观测器估计归一化相对速度并传播不确定性，实现在不重建障碍物几何/世界模型前提下的安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视觉自主导航中障碍物的显式状态不可直接观测；传统光流导航仅用图像运动塑造避障行为，缺乏安全性保证。作者希望在无需重建障碍物位置、速度、几何或度量世界模型的前提下，直接在该相对运动表示中编码碰撞锥几何与安全约束，从而获得可证明的安全认证能力。

Method: 以尺度无关、深度归一化的单目光流相对运动作为碰撞风险认证表示；用在线Concurrent Learning观测器估计归一化相对速度并给出可计算的估计误差界；将不确定性经角度膨胀和单目机器人足迹裕度传播进碰撞锥，构造鲁棒视觉碰撞锥控制障碍函数RV-C³BF；用最优衰减CBF二次规划（optimal-decay CBF-QP）以最小改动修正名义控制输入，在给定假设下强制满足鲁棒安全约束。

Result: 仿真与TurtleBot3真实世界杂乱环境（静态与动态障碍）实验表明：仅用单目视觉和机载本体感受即可避障；估计误差界在98.3%的仿真样本中包容实测误差；100次真实试验中93次实现无碰撞导航。

Conclusion: 该工作将安全性认证从度量世界模型重建转移到尺度无关的相对运动表示，配合在线学习观测器与鲁棒CBF-QP，在单目感知条件下提供可计算、可验证的避障安全保障，并在真实机器人上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Safe autonomous navigation using monocular vision remains challenging because explicit obstacle states are not directly observable. This letter introduces a visual safety-certification paradigm that uses scale-free, depth-normalized relative motion encoded by monocular optical flow as the representation for collision-risk certification, without reconstructing obstacle position, velocity, geometry, or a metric world model. Unlike conventional optical-flow-based navigation approaches that rely on image motion to shape avoidance behavior, the proposed framework directly encodes collision-cone geometry and safety constraints in this relative-motion representation. An online Concurrent Learning observer estimates the normalized relative velocity and provides a computable estimation-error bound. This uncertainty is propagated into the collision cone through angular inflation and a monocular robot-footprint margin, yielding a Robust Vision-Based Collision Cone Control Barrier Function (RV-C$^3$BF). An optimal-decay CBF quadratic program minimally modifies the nominal control input to enforce the resulting robust safety constraint under the stated assumptions. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments on a TurtleBot3 in cluttered environments with static and dynamic obstacles demonstrate collision avoidance using only monocular vision and onboard proprioception, with the estimation-error bound containing the measured error in $98.3\%$ of simulation samples and the robot achieving collision-free navigation in $93$ of $100$ real-world trials.

</details>


### [171] [Fast-VLA: You Only Think Once for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21389)
*Kehong Gong,Guanli Hou,Xiaoxu Zheng,Qi Wang,Mingxi Xu,Anbang Zhai,Xiaoyu He,Mingyuan Zhang,Michael Bi Mi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fast-VLA 提出只在每回合调用一次冻结的 4B VLM，将指令与初始观测编码为固定多层 KV 任务记忆，执行时用轻量在线 actor 融合时间/臂索引查询与实时视觉、本体感受反馈，从固定记忆中检索任务上下文并预测动作块，无需重复运行基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略把数十亿参数模型放进闭环反馈中，每次预测动作块都要重算任务上下文，推理开销大；但任务理解的变化远慢于机器人状态变化，因此重复计算高度冗余。

Method: 每回合仅调用一次冻结的 4B VLM，对指令与初始视图生成固定、多层的 key-value 任务记忆；在线轻量 actor 用工学的时间索引与臂索引查询，融合当前视觉与本体感受构成动作查询，检索固定记忆并直接预测动作块。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Randomized 成功率 92.46%/92.40%，LIBERO 与 LIBERO-Plus 达 95.95%/79.11%；同硬件下相较三个持续条件化基线，RoboTwin 摊销策略推理时间降低 10.9–17.1 倍，prefill 后在线常驻内存 1.22 GiB；任务记忆替换实验显示 actor 依赖与回合匹配的任务记忆。

Conclusion: 任务上下文可一次性建立，闭环控制在整个执行过程中仍保持响应性；以一次性任务记忆解耦慢变任务理解与快变机器人状态，是降低 VLA 推理成本的有效范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies commonly place a multi-billion-parameter model inside the feedback loop, repeatedly recomputing task context whenever they predict a new action chunk. Yet task understanding changes far more slowly than the robot state. We introduce Fast-VLA, which invokes a frozen 4B VLM once per episode to contextualize the instruction and initial views as fixed, multi-layer key-value task memory. During execution, a lightweight online actor fuses learned time- and arm-indexed queries with current visual and proprioceptive feedback to form action queries. These queries retrieve task context from the fixed memory and predict action chunks without re-running the foundation model. Fast-VLA achieves 92.46% and 92.40% success on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean and Randomized, and 95.95% and 79.11% on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus evaluation. Against three continually conditioned baselines on the same hardware, it reduces RoboTwin amortized policy inference time by 10.9-17.1x and requires 1.22 GiB of post-prefill online resident memory. Task-memory swaps further show that the actor relies on episode-matched task memory. These results show that task context can be established once while closed-loop control remains responsive throughout execution. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [172] [Modular Inverse Dynamics and Wrench Recovery for Closed-Chain Mechanisms Using Path Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21400)
*Mohammad Dastranj,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模块化逆动力学工作流，结合路径式运动重建与唯一可解的执行器/关节力旋量恢复，用于刚性闭链机构中不确定内力的判定与求解，并以7自由度重型混联机械臂对照Simscape验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
