Motivation: 刚性闭链机构即使运动完全给定，其内部反力仍可能不唯一（indeterminate），传统逆动力学难以区分唯一可解量与不确定负荷族，需要一套能同时重建被动运动并甄别唯一输出的方法。

Method: 对装配变换路径做解析二阶微分，在规则闭合分支上得到被动关节速度与加速度；在物理刚体-关节图上组装空间Newton-Euler平衡方程；利用零空间与对偶判据识别并恢复唯一确定的执行器力与约束力旋量分量，并以平衡、理想关节可行性及仿射族诊断评估非唯一参考反力。

Result: 在7自由度重型混联机械臂的给定运动下，与Simscape Multibody对照：被动位置、执行器出力及唯一可恢复的约束力旋量分量吻合良好，反力诊断结果与可行数值载荷族一致。

Conclusion: 该工作流能以模块化方式处理闭链逆动力学中的不确定性，稳健分离唯一可解输出与不确定反力族，适用于混联/闭链机构的负载与内力分析，但验证限于理想刚体-理想关节假设与仿真对照。

Abstract: Rigid closed chains can admit indeterminate internal reactions even when their motion is fully specified. This paper develops a modular inverse-dynamics workflow that combines path-based motion reconstruction with recovery of uniquely determined actuator and joint-wrench outputs. Analytical second-order differentiation of assembled transformation paths supplies passive velocities and accelerations on a regular closure branch. Spatial Newton--Euler balances are then assembled over the physical body-and-joint graph. Established nullspace and dual criteria identify and recover unique outputs, while balance, ideal-joint admissibility and affine-family diagnostics assess nonunique reference reactions. Evaluation against Simscape Multibody uses prescribed motion of a seven-degree-of-freedom heavy-duty hybrid serial--parallel manipulator under common rigid-body, ideal-joint assumptions. The results show close agreement with the reference for passive positions, actuator efforts and uniquely recoverable constraint-wrench components. Reaction diagnostics support compatibility with the admissible numerical load family.

</details>


### [173] [MuSTMM: Learning Sound-Triggered Mobile Manipulation across Multiple Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21429)
*Hao Ju,Feng Yan,Zhihao Zhong,Heng Yang,Shaofei Huang,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MuSTMM 数据集与带显式空间记忆的导航策略，用于跨 31 个未见过场景的声音触发移动操作评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声音触发移动操作（STMM）评测主要在有限建筑结构内变换物体布局，未充分探索未见过场景下的挑战。

Method: 构建 MuSTMM：结合空间音频渲染与物理交互，覆盖 31 个不同建筑结构与物体布局的场景，含 1436 万条预计算双耳房间脉冲响应（RIR），并提供场景不相交划分以评测单事件与多事件设置；在导航策略模型中引入显式空间记忆：在共享坐标系中累积位姿对齐的深度观测，投影为自我中心鸟瞰图（BEV）占据地图，将地图特征与声音上下文融合生成目标描述符，提供目标位置与场景结构先验以引导导航。

Result: 相比现有方法成功率提升 2.60%，组件消融支持空间记忆的有效性。

Conclusion: MuSTMM 与导航策略为未见过场景下声音触发移动操作研究提供了测试平台，代码与数据集将在录用后发布。

Abstract: Sound-Triggered Mobile Manipulation (STMM) requires robots to respond to sound events by navigating to the associated objects and performing appropriate physical interactions. Existing STMM evaluations primarily vary object layouts within limited architectural structures, leaving challenges to unseen scenes insufficiently explored. We introduce Multi-scene Sound-Triggered Mobile Manipulation (MuSTMM), a dataset that combines spatial audio rendering and physical interaction across 31 scenes with diverse architectures and object layouts. MuSTMM contains 14.36 million precomputed binaural Room Impulse Responses (RIRs) and a scene-disjoint split for evaluating single-event and multi-event settings in unseen scenes. We further augment the Navigation Policy Model with explicit spatial memory that complements acoustic cues with observed geometry. Specifically, we accumulate pose-aligned depth observations in a shared coordinate frame and project this memory into an egocentric Bird's-Eye View (BEV) occupancy map. Fusing map features with sound context produces a goal descriptor that provides both target-location and scene-structure priors, guiding navigation to the target region required for subsequent physical interaction. This policy achieves a 2.60% success rate boost over existing methods, while component ablations further support the effectiveness of spatial memory. MuSTMM and the navigation policy provide a testbed for the new challenge of studying sound-triggered mobile manipulation in unseen scenes. The code and dataset will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [174] [Policy-Aligned Predictive Verification for Adaptive VLA Action-Chunk Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21434)
*Minkyu Kim,Junhoo Lee,Seungyeon Kim,Suhyun Jeon,Baekseung Kim,Donghoon Han,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出策略对齐预测验证器（PAV），在VLA内部任务条件表征空间中预测候选动作前缀的后果，以有效性分数指导执行视野选择，在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真机上提升成功率-查询次数权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略生成动作序列以减少重复推理，但"执行多长前缀"的决策至关重要。现有自适应方法依赖生成稳定性或预测一致性，这些信号无法判断执行某前缀后是否仍能完成任务，缺乏任务完成性保证。

Method: PAV不生成动作，而是在VLA内部任务条件表征空间中对已有候选动作进行评估。训练上用轨迹级存活监督（survival supervision）从终端任务结果反推局部判断，并引入定位先验（localization prior）保留失败轨迹中的有用行为；输出的有效性分数用于选择执行视野。

Result: 在pi_0与pi_0.5两个VLA模型、LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0及真实世界操作任务上评测，成功率-查询次数权衡均有改善。

Conclusion: 未来预测在机器人中可扮演新角色：不是生成动作，而是评估策略所提动作值得信任到什么程度，从而决定执行距离。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies generate action sequences that reduce repeated inference, but deciding how much of each sequence to execute is critical to task success. Existing adaptive methods make this decision using generation stability or prediction agreement, yet these signals do not establish whether executing a candidate prefix preserves the ability to complete the task. We introduce a policy-aligned predictive verifier (PAV) that anticipates the consequences of candidate action prefixes and evaluates their compatibility with successful continuation before execution. Rather than using future prediction to generate actions, our method evaluates existing proposals in the VLA's internal task-conditioned representation space. To learn local judgments from terminal task outcomes, we combine trajectory-level survival supervision with a localization prior that preserves useful behavior within failed rollouts. The resulting validity scores guide execution-horizon selection. Evaluations with pi_0 and pi_0.5 across LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world manipulation show improved success-query trade-offs. These findings point toward a different role for future prediction in robotics, using it to assess how far to trust a policy's proposed actions rather than to generate new ones.

</details>


### [175] [X-Fly: Learning to Reach and Stop in Full-Chunk Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21450)
*Ruibin Ju,Ruifeng Cai,Min Xu,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-Fly 用分阶段训练（Recovery DAgger + Flow-GRPO）解决全动作块空中视觉语言导航中因长块执行导致的偏离专家分布与不停机问题，在 OpenFly test-seen 达 74.18% SR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中 VLN 依赖大视觉语言模型，逐步查询代价高；执行完整动作块再重规划可将推理从 195ms 降到 42ms/路点。但全块执行使策略查询状态偏离专家示范，导致既无恢复监督也无学习者自身到达处的终止监督，OpenFly test-seen 上不停机失败占全块 BC 结果的 47.78%。

Method: 提出 X-Fly 分阶段训练框架：1) Recovery DAgger，对学习者访问的决策状态打标签，用可重连至未来专家段的平滑动作块，并在首个区域内状态给 STOP；2) Flow-GRPO，基于同任务闭环结果联合优化分块 flow 控制器与 Bernoulli STOP 策略，辅以结果验证的停止监督。每决策 8 个控制。

Result: test-seen 上 SR 74.18%，test-unseen 51.73%；对比全块 BC 的 16.45%/18.43% 与 DreamFly 的 32.04%/29.46%。

Conclusion: 全块执行需配套分布纠正与终止学习：恢复式模仿加闭环强化可显著提升空中 VLN 成功率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (VLN) agents built on large vision-language models are expensive to query. Executing a complete predicted action chunk before replanning amortizes this cost, reducing model inference from 195 to 42 ms per executed waypoint for our policy. It also moves the states at which the policy is queried away from expert demonstrations, which supervise neither recovery from these states nor termination at the learner's own arrival. On OpenFly test-seen, no-stop failures account for 47.78% of full-chunk behavior cloning (BC) outcomes. We present X-Fly, a staged training framework for full-chunk aerial VLN. Recovery DAgger labels learner-visited decision states with smooth chunks that reconnect to future expert segments and with STOP at the first in-region state. Flow-GRPO then jointly refines the chunked flow controller and a Bernoulli STOP policy from same-task closed-loop outcomes, supplemented by outcome-verified stopping supervision. With eight controls per decision, X-Fly reaches 74.18% SR on test-seen and 51.73% on test-unseen, compared with 16.45% and 18.43% for full-chunk BC and 32.04% and 29.46% for DreamFly.

</details>


### [176] [CarryOn: A Dual-Loop Harness for Personalized and Reliable Robot Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21453)
*Zixuan Wang,Yuqi Liu,Yuxin Chen,Sengqiao Yang,Jinhui Ye,Pengguang Chen,Changsheng Lu,Shu Liu,Bei Yu,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CarryOn：双环（规划环+执行环）框架包裹冻结VLA策略，实现个性化子任务分解与可解释安全监督及失败恢复，真机+仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLA模型虽能开放世界操作，但缺少两项关键能力：一是缺乏基于持久用户偏好的个性化任务分解（如"给我做个三明治"需按用户口味拆解子任务），二是缺乏可解释的安全保障机制，无法主动预防危险并从物理失败中优雅恢复。现有工作依赖单一通用解，且安全性与可恢复性不足。

Method: 提出双环harness增强冻结的VLA策略：(1)规划环维护用户级与场景级持久上下文，持续与新观测对齐、通过交互修订，跨对话与场景变化实现个性化任务选择与执行grounding；(2)执行环主动监督策略rollout，屏蔽预测为危险的接触动作；执行失败时调用可复用的基于控制的恢复技能，恢复任务进度并通过反思先前失败迭代精炼技能。

Result: 真机与仿真实验：跨用户、跨场景准确派发符合偏好的子任务；将已安全微调VLA的违规率从52.6%降至6.1%；复用精炼技能后恢复成功率从13%提升至80%。

Conclusion: 在不重训VLA的前提下，通过外置双环harness可同时注入个性化与安全/恢复能力，说明模块化框架是提升现成VLA实际部署可靠性的有效路径，但对VLA本体能力无改进，个性化依赖上下文质量。

Abstract: While generalist vision-language-action (VLA) models have enabled diverse open-world manipulation, real-world robotic assistance requires two critical capabilities they currently lack: personalization and reliable execution. First, executing an instruction like "make me a sandwich" requires grounding subtask decomposition in persistent user preferences rather than relying on a single generic solution. Second, reliable deployment demands explainable safety assurance that proactively prevents hazards and gracefully recovers from occasional physical failures. To address these gaps, we present CarryOn, a dual-loop harness designed to augment a frozen VLA policy. The planning loop maintains persistent user-level and scene-level context that is continually grounded against new observations and revised through interaction, enabling personalized task selection and execution grounding across conversations and changing scenes. The execution loop actively supervises policy rollouts, shielding actions from predicted hazardous contact. When execution fails, the system invokes reusable control-based recovery skills that restore task progress and are refined through reflection on earlier failures. On a real robot and in simulation, CarryOn dispatches preference-consistent subtasks across users and scenes, reduces the violation rate of an already safety-finetuned VLA from 52.6% to 6.1%, and increases recovery success from 13% to 80% when refined skills are reused.

</details>


### [177] [A 2D Image Feedback-Based Autonomous Endovascular Navigation Method: A Perception and Control Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21481)
*Sheng Cao,Shuxiang Guo,Yonggan Yan,Jian Guo,Chunying Li,Bin Wang,Mingchao Ding,Yongxin Zhang,Yongwei Zhang,Pengfei Yang,Jianmin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于二维DSA/X光图像的中心线图与器械尖端投影，用规则控制器实现血管内介入的自主导航，并在实体双器械平台上完成65次闭环实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管内介入中医生依赖二维DSA与透视图像判读血管解剖与器械运动，将这类二维图像转化为可供自主导航使用的反馈仍是难点。

Method: 将血管掩膜转为带标签的中心线图，把器械尖端估计投影到规划路径上，得到推进度、偏差与方向；规则控制器据此执行递送、分支选择、试探、回撤与恢复动作。在实体双器械机器人平台上用仿真渲染的平面图像，跨三条路径进行65次闭环运行评估。

Result: 所有运行的最大观测导丝推进度均不低于0.95；仅2次（3.1%）触发预设失败条件（≥3次自主停止事件）。

Conclusion: 结果验证了该平台上路径级导航性能，但临床图像序列与更广泛解剖结构仍待评估；方法为规则驱动、未见学习式泛化证据。

Abstract: In endovascular interventions, physicians rely on planar DSA and X-ray fluoroscopy to interpret vessel anatomy and device motion, yet converting these images into feedback for autonomous navigation remains difficult. A perception and control method is formulated for two-dimensional image feedback. Vessel masks are converted into a labeled centerline graph, and device-tip estimates are projected onto a planned path to obtain progress, deviation, and direction. A rule-based controller uses these quantities for delivery, branch selection, probing, rollback, and recovery. The method was evaluated on a physical dual-device robotic platform in 65 closed-loop runs using simulator-rendered planar images across three paths. All runs reached at least 0.95 maximum observed guidewire progress; two runs (3.1%) met the prespecified failure rule of three or more autonomous stop events. The results establish route-level performance on the tested platform; clinical image sequences and broader anatomy remain to be evaluated.

</details>


### [178] [Location-Based Safety Management with Wearable Seamless Indoor-Outdoor Positioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21503)
*Anh-Van Vu,Seungwoo Chae,Changmin Sung,Van-Tuan Nguyen,Dongsoo Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向工业安全管理的室外-室内无缝定位系统：资源受限可穿戴标签（GNSS+IMU+气压+LTE Cat-M，边缘轻量融合）+云端地图感知融合，配套数据库、地图工具与看板；14人约1400 m路线的实测平均误差为室内1.32 m、室外3.30 m，延迟低且功耗低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时工业安全管理需要可靠的工人定位，但室外到室内的连续定位仍具挑战：GNSS在室外可用、靠近建筑退化、室内失效；许多室内系统依赖昂贵基础设施或大量标定。因此希望以低成本可穿戴设备实现可扩展的无缝定位。

Method: 采用设备-云分离架构：标签端集成GNSS、IMU、气压计与LTE Cat-M通信，执行轻量级传感器融合；云端执行地图感知融合以实现连续跟踪。系统作为可运营平台还包含数据库、地图工具与监控看板，支持实时监控、基于地理围栏的进入告警以及安全与效率分析。

Result: 多名参与者各沿约1400 m的室外-室内路线行走，使用LTE Cat-M通信，室内平均误差1.32 m、室外平均误差3.30 m，同时保持较低的定位延迟与高效功耗。

Conclusion: 结果验证了基于可穿戴设备的无缝定位用于可扩展实时安全管理的可行性，并为设备-云协同融合在资源受限场景下的工程落地提供了实证参考。

Abstract: Reliable worker localization is essential for real-time industrial safety management, but seamless outdoor-indoor positioning remains challenging. GNSS performs well outdoors yet degrades near buildings and fails indoors, while many indoor systems require costly infrastructure or extensive calibration. This paper presents a practical safety-oriented positioning system that uses resource-constrained wearable tags and a device-cloud split architecture for continuous outdoor-indoor worker tracking. The tag integrates GNSS, IMU, barometric sensing, LTE Cat-M communication, and performs lightweight sensor fusion, while the cloud server executes map-aware fusion for continuous tracking. Developed as an operational platform, the system also includes a database, a map tool, and a dashboard that provide real-time monitoring, geofencing-based access alerts, and safety and productivity analysis. Experiments with multiple participants, each walking an approximately 1400 m outdoor-indoor route, using LTE Cat-M communication, achieve mean errors of 1.32 m indoors and 3.30 m outdoors, while maintaining low positioning latency and efficient power consumption. These results demonstrate the feasibility of wearable-based seamless positioning for scalable real-time safety management.

</details>


### [179] [TRACE: Typed Reference-Aware Conversational Editing for Humanoid Motion Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21515)
*Zejian Kang,Yifu Luo,Boyang Shen,Jingxuan Li,Zekai Chen,Zekun Shi,Yongxue Xu,Zhenyu Hou,Bingqian Wu,Shaolei Zhang,Wentao Yang,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TRACE，面向 Unitree G1 人形机器人的语言条件运动生成与编辑框架，以「类型化可测运动学量」为核心，用运动数据监督事件预测并驱动扩散生成与参考感知编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件人形运动生成的核心难点在于：用户请求通常欠指定，难以映射到具体运动结构，且后续修改需相对于已接受的参考运动进行，生成与编辑割裂。作者希望找到生成与编辑共享的统一表征基础。

Method: 以类型化、可度量的运动学量作为公共基底。生成侧：语言模型从运动数据派生的监督中预测支撑、根、手部事件，事件的时间对齐编码与原始文本共同输入扩散生成器；引入重力参考状态与轨迹-接触目标提升运动学一致性。编辑侧：类型化请求定义身体-时间作用域与源相对目标，由独立的参考条件扩散编辑器结合修定后的事件实现修改，采样时投影保持受保护状态坐标。

Result: 定量评测与人工评估显示语义对齐和运动质量优于对比方法；受控消融验证了运动接地条件与运动学一致性的价值；多轮编辑实验表明在共享保持约束下能更可靠地执行连续相对请求。

Conclusion: 结果支持「类型化运动学结构」可作为运动生成与参考感知修订的共同基础，为语言驱动的人形机器人运动生成与迭代编辑提供一体化范式。

Abstract: Language-conditioned humanoid motion generation enables users to create and refine robot movements through natural language. The central challenge is to connect an underspecified request to concrete motion structure that can subsequently be revised relative to an accepted reference. We present TRACE, a generation and editing framework for the Unitree G1 humanoid robot built around typed, measurable kinematic quantities. Our insight is to ground generation conditions in paired training motions and editing goals in the current motion. A language model learns to predict support, root, and hand events from motion-derived supervision; their time-aligned encodings complement the original text in a diffusion generator. Gravity-referenced states and trajectory--contact objectives improve kinematic consistency. For editing, typed requests define body--time scopes and source-relative goals. A separate reference-conditioned diffusion editor uses these goals and revised events to realize changes, while sampling-time projection preserves protected state coordinates. Quantitative evaluation and human assessment show improved semantic alignment and motion quality over the compared methods. Controlled ablations support the value of motion-grounded conditioning and kinematic consistency, while multi-turn editing evaluations demonstrate more reliable execution of successive relative requests under shared preservation constraints. Together, these results support typed kinematic structure as a common basis for motion generation and reference-aware revision.

</details>


### [180] [Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21522)
*Lichao Ni,Yuhan Liu,Pengfei Li,Guanghuan Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两级 on-policy 蒸馏框架，将多个领域专用 flow-based VLA 专家整合为单一学生策略：在环境观测层面由学生驱动交互采样，在动作生成层面沿学生生成的动作流轨迹进行教师速度场监督；相对 Action Loss OPD 与 Teacher-rollout SFT 在 LIBERO-40、CALVIN、Meta-World MT50 上取得显著绝对成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用 VLA 策略在各自领域能力强，但难以合并为一个共享策略；on-policy 蒸馏可缓解部署时的分布错配，而 flow-based VLA 的错配同时出现在环境观测与动作生成中间状态两个层面，现有方法未同时处理。

Method: 冻结多个领域专家联合监督单一学生。从联合 SFT 初始化开始，每轮更新后由学生重新采集环境经验并生成新的动作流轨迹；一级在学生诱导的环境观测上蒸馏，二级在学生生成的动作流轨迹上施加教师速度场监督（Velocity Loss OPD），与 Action Loss OPD、Teacher-rollout SFT 对比。

Result: 在联合 SFT 初始化基础上，Velocity Loss OPD 绝对成功率提升：LIBERO-40 +22.55%，CALVIN 五指令序列 +33.4%，Meta-World MT50 +22.6%；相对 Action Loss OPD 与 Teacher-rollout SFT 提升 6.4–9.5%（LIBERO-40）、9.7–18.3%（CALVIN）、3.2–4.2%（MT50）；CALVIN 上序列越长增益越大。

Conclusion: 两级 on-policy 监督可有效实现多域能力整合，单一学生策略可跨域部署；速度场层面的监督优于动作层面的蒸馏与教师 rollout 模仿。

Abstract: Specialized vision-language-action (VLA) policies acquire strong domain-specific skills, yet consolidating these skills into one shared policy remains challenging. On-policy distillation (OPD) transfers teacher knowledge on inputs induced by the student, addressing the distribution mismatch encountered during deployment. For flow-based VLAs, this mismatch arises at two levels: environment observations and intermediate action-generation states. We propose a two-level on-policy distillation framework that couples student-driven environment interaction with teacher velocity supervision along student-generated action flow trajectories. Frozen domain specialists jointly supervise one student. After each update, the student collects new environment experience and generates fresh action-flow trajectories. Starting from a joint supervised fine-tuning (SFT) initialization, Velocity Loss OPD yields absolute success-rate gains of 22.55% on LIBERO-40, 33.4% on CALVIN five-instruction sequences, and 22.6% on Meta-World MT50. Compared with Action Loss OPD and Teacher-rollout SFT, it achieves absolute success-rate gains of 6.4-9.5% on LIBERO-40, 9.7-18.3% on CALVIN, and 3.2-4.2% on Meta-World MT50. Analysis by sequence length further shows larger success-rate gains on longer CALVIN sequences. These results demonstrate effective multi-domain capability consolidation through two-level on-policy supervision, with a single student deployed across domains.

</details>


### [181] [Attribution-Gated Joint Estimation of Intention and Capability for Assist-as-Needed Upper-Limb Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21534)
*Pengcheng Li,Shuxiang Guo,Hanze Wang,Jun Leng,Bin Wang,Mingchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双测量量多模态MHE-MPC框架与归因门，将AAN控制中"意图轨迹变化"与"有效自主力矩能力变化"解耦，在9名健康被试的阶乘负载任务中取得最低跟踪误差，并在疲劳前后验证了辅助自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Assist-as-needed控制中，意图运动变化与有效自主力矩能力变化会产生相似的动力学与交互响应，传统方法难以区分两者，导致辅助策略错误归因、参考轨迹误更新。需要在线分离并分别适配。

Method: 构建双测量量（dual-measurand）多模态MHE-MPC基础架构，将意图轨迹与任务相关有效自主力矩能力作为两个独立在线估计量。将两通道间的歧义建模为连续信用分配问题，用归因门（attribution gate）求解：轨迹变化指标来自持续性纠正性交互，能力变化指标来自归一化肌电激活与动力学重构自主力矩之间的残差。9名健康被试完成目标高度×负载阶乘试验，以及疲劳前后杯子搬运任务。

Result: 无目标高度输入时，门控方法在各负载下均产生正确排序的低-中-高参考；目标变化优先归因于轨迹通道，附加负载则将自适应偏向能力通道。9个条件下RMSE最低（0.017 rad，对比无门控双估计0.041 rad、固定参考误差型AAN 0.127 rad）。疲劳后能力估计下降、各高度辅助量增加。

Conclusion: 归因门可有效消解轨迹与能力双通道的信用分配歧义，实现可解释、可分解的AAN自适应，并在疲劳诱导的能力退化场景下保持功能性可行，为物理人机交互中的个性化辅助控制提供方法基础。

Abstract: Assist-as-needed (AAN) control must distinguish between changes in intended motion and changes in effective voluntary torque capability, even though both can produce similar kinematic and interaction responses. A dual-measurand multimodal MHE-MPC architecture is developed as the base framework, in which the intended trajectory and the task-dependent effective voluntary torque capability are estimated online as distinct quantities, and both estimates are used to adapt robotic assistance. Building on this architecture, the ambiguity between the two update channels is formulated as a continuous credit-assignment problem and resolved by an attribution gate. A trajectory-change indicator is obtained from persistent corrective interaction, whereas a capability-change indicator is derived from the residual between normalized muscle activation and dynamics-reconstructed voluntary torque. Nine healthy participants completed factorial target-height-load trials and a pre-post fatigue cup-transport task. Without target-height input, the gated method generated correctly ordered Low-Middle-High references under every load: target changes were preferentially assigned to trajectory adaptation, whereas added load shifted adaptation toward the capability channel. It achieved the lowest RMSE in all nine conditions (0.017 rad versus 0.041 rad for ungated dual estimation and 0.127 rad for fixed-reference error-based AAN). Post-fatigue capability estimates decreased and assistance increased at all heights, supporting functional feasibility.

</details>


### [182] [AVALON: Zero-Shot Indoor Navigation with Human-Seeded Topological Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21586)
*Jing Xia,Changyan Li,Yuduo Wang,Guiyin Tian,Bo Wang,Yifan Liao,Mingjun Li,Shiyan Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AVALON：面向"人熟悉、机器人未见"室内场景的拓扑导航框架，以人先验分层空间记忆、节点验证拓扑执行与任务触发式人机记忆协同更新为核心，无需建图或现场训练，在Unitree Go2上完成任务并显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内服务机器人常进入从未探索但对用户而言布局与任务约束已知的建筑。现有视觉点目标导航策略缺乏全局结构先验与任务约束，而传统SLAM建图对该"人熟悉、机器人未见"的设定既冗余又低效。作者希望利用人的先验知识（楼层平面图、地点线索、任务约束、离线环境变更报告）与机器人现场证据（可通行性、物理状态）互补，实现零现场遍历、零站点训练的部署。

Method: 提出AVALON拓扑导航框架，三个组成部分：(1) 人播种的分层空间记忆，部署仅需平面图、地点线索和初始拓扑-里程计对齐；(2) 节点验证的拓扑执行，视觉深度点目标导航在有验证节点到达的情况下执行平移重锚定，无需机器人自建全局SLAM地图；(3) 任务触发、证据门控的人机记忆协同更新——人类提供视觉不可观测的任务约束与离线环境变更，机器人通过主动现场验证提供物理状态证据；任务相关性决定人类报告的环境变动是否在任务中接受机器人验证，现场感知证据门控记忆更新，显式区分"人类许可"与"机器人观测可通行性"。搭配X-NavDP策略。

Result: Unitree Go2实机实验中，AVALON+X-NavDP完成12.81 m的Intersection-to-Dining任务用时46.4 s（成功率5/5），在无建图条件下比DimOS-Go2（78.5 s，4/5）减少40.9%机器人时间；仅使用策略的X-NavDP在5次运行中全部超时。

Conclusion: 结果表明该拓扑导航框架在人类熟悉但机器人未知的室内场景中，无需SLAM建图与站点训练即可高效完成任务，且有实机验证与相对基线的明显时间与成功率优势，验证了人机记忆协同更新与节点验证拓扑执行的有效性。局限在于实验规模较小（单一平台、单任务路线、5次运行），记忆协同的长期演化与多任务泛化仍待验证。

Abstract: Indoor service robots often enter unmapped buildings whose layouts and task constraints are already familiar to users. We address this human-familiar, robot-unseen setting with AVALON, a topological navigation framework organized around human-seeded layered spatial memory, node-verified topological execution, and task-triggered, evidence-gated human--robot memory co-updating. The robot has no prior on-site traversal or site-specific training. Deployment requires only a floor plan, place clues, and an initial topology-odometry alignment. Visual depth point-goal navigation leverages verified node arrivals to perform translational re-anchoring, without requiring a robot-constructed global SLAM map. Human--robot memory co-updating fuses complementary sources of information. Human operators specify task constraints that are unobservable via vision and report offline environmental modifications, whereas the robot provides physical-state evidence through active in-situ validation. Task relevance governs whether human-reported environmental changes undergo robot validation during the active mission. Field-collected perceptual evidence gates memory updates, maintaining an explicit separation between human-specified permissions and robot-observed traversability. Our experiments on a Unitree Go2 show that AVALON with X-NavDP completes a 12.81 m Intersection-to-Dining mission in 46.4 s (5/5) with no mapping, 40.9% less robot time than DimOS-Go2 (78.5 s, 4/5), while policy-only X-NavDP times out in all of the 5 runs. These demonstrate the superiority of the proposed topological navigation framework.

</details>


### [183] [A Flow-Theoretic Multimodal Control Framework Fusing Mental and Physical Engagement for Upper-Limb Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21590)
*Jun Leng,Pengcheng Li,Hanze Wang,Shuxiang Guo,Junlin Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于"流理论"的多模态按需辅助(AAN)框架，融合EEG心理投入与sEMG生理投入，通过四象限模糊决策引擎生成自适应阻抗控制策略，使上肢外骨骼在意图-执行失配时注入前馈辅助、在注意力涣散时提升导引刚度。实验显示机器人能耗降低71.9%，流状态比例从4.6%升至42.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有按需辅助(AAN)控制仅依赖物理指标(如sEMG、力矩)，无法区分"肌肉无力"与"心理不投入"两种本质不同的低参与状态，导致辅助策略误判、用户被动化，削弱运动学习效果。因此需要引入心理维度信息以提升辅助的选择性与针对性。

Method: 构建流理论(Flow)驱动的多模态AAN框架：以EEG估计心理投入度、以sEMG估计生理投入度，双通道生理指标输入四象限模糊决策引擎，映射为连续自适应阻抗控制策略；外骨骼在检测到"意图-执行失配"时注入前馈辅助，在检测到心理走神时提高导引刚度作为惩罚性反馈。与仅用sEMG的现有AAN基线在健康受试者上对比验证。

Result: 相比sEMG-only基线：机器人能量消耗降低71.9%，同时保持精确轨迹跟踪；神经可塑性区间时间占比(流状态比)由4.6%提升至42.8%；表明用户的自主努力显著增加，被动性得到缓解。

Conclusion: 解耦并协同调节心理与生理状态可显著提升机器人辅助的选择性，有效抑制用户被动、维持对运动学习至关重要的参与度。该框架为康复外骨骼的人机协同控制提供了多模态生理闭环的新范式。

Abstract: While Assist-as-Needed (AAN) control aims to maximize user participation in robot-assisted training, existing methods relying solely on physical metrics cannot distinguish muscular inability from mental disengagement, leading to suboptimal interventions. To address this ambiguity, a Flow-theoretic multimodal AAN framework is proposed, fusing EEG-based mental engagement and sEMG-based physical engagement into an adaptive impedance control strategy. A four-quadrant fuzzy decision engine translates these dual physiological metrics into continuous control policies. This enables the upper-limb exoskeleton to inject feedforward assistance during intent-execution mismatch (e.g., motor output attenuation despite mental focus) and escalate guiding stiffness to penalize mental distraction. The framework was validated with healthy participants against a state-of-practice sEMG-only AAN baseline. Kinematically and mechanically, the proposed framework reduced robotic energy expenditure by 71.9\% while maintaining precise trajectory tracking, confirming it elicited greater voluntary human effort. Physiologically, the system increased the time spent in the neuroplasticity zone (Flow State Ratio) from 4.6% to 42.8%. These results validate that decoupling and jointly regulating mental and physical states enhances the selectivity of robotic assistance, mitigating user passivity and sustaining the engagement crucial for motor learning.

</details>


### [184] [Training-Free Target-Aware Refinement of 3D Gaussian Language Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21619)
*Sibaek Lee,Kyeongsu Kang,Seongbo Ha,Yewon Jeon,Joonyeol Choi,Daegeol Ko,Jaehyuk Hur,Taemin Choi,Minsu Kim,Hyungjoo Ahn,Wonsok Yoo,Hyeonwoo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练、目标感知的3D语言场精修方法：利用可文本提示的分割器检测目标区域，将目标文本嵌入与区域内视觉嵌入做凸组合，并以闭式解求取每个目标的最小注入系数；同时发现并修正文本嵌入公共分量导致余弦读出与查询无关的失效模式。在三个基准上提升开放词汇分割（Replica上mIoU约翻倍），并实现四足机器人自然语言导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉-语言特征蒸馏进3D高斯泼溅得到的语言图虽可用任意文本查询，但在实际部署中常无法可靠定位用户指定的少量目标；同时又不能牺牲对其他词汇的开放词汇查询能力。因此需要在已有蒸馏地图上做目标感知的精修。

Method: 免训练精修：由可文本提示的分割器引导，对每个检出目标区域，将区域视觉嵌入与目标文本嵌入做凸组合（系数控制注入先验强度），非目标区域不变；对每个目标求解仍能获胜的最小系数，并给出全部系数的闭式解，无需调参。此外引入读出阶段的中心化步骤，消除文本嵌入公共分量导致的查询无关性。

Result: 在三个基准上一致提升开放词汇分割，Replica基准mIoU约提升至基线的两倍；语言目标定位比基础地图更可靠；可作为插件迁移到现成的场式与物体级语言地图；支持四足机器人在真实世界中按自然语言目标导航。

Conclusion: 该方法以零训练、零超参的方式闭环解决了蒸馏语言图的部署短板，兼顾目标可靠定位与开放词汇泛化，并具跨地图可插拔性与真实机器人导航可行性。

Abstract: Distilling vision-language features into 3D Gaussian Splatting yields a language map that can be queried with arbitrary text. In deployment, however, a small set of user-specified targets must be reliably located, which current distilled maps often miss, while open-vocabulary queryability for other words must remain intact. We propose a training-free, target-aware refinement of an already-distilled language map. Guided by a text-promptable segmenter, the visual embedding inside each detected target region is blended with the target text embedding, a convex combination whose coefficient sets the amount of injected prior, while non-target regions are left untouched. Injecting too much overwrites appearance cues and general queryability, so we seek, for each target, the smallest coefficient that still lets it win, and we obtain every coefficient in closed form, leaving no hyperparameters to tune. We further identify a failure mode of injection in language-goal localization. Text embeddings share a common component, and injecting them makes the standard cosine read-out query-agnostic, which a centering step in the read-out removes. Across three benchmarks, the refinement consistently improves open-vocabulary segmentation, roughly doubling the base mIoU on Replica, and localizes language goals more reliably than the base map. It also transfers as a plug-in to off-the-shelf field-based and object-level language maps and supports a real-world demonstration in which a legged robot navigates to natural-language goals. Project page and code: this https URL

</details>


### [185] [SPEAR: Selective Preference Elicitation with Adaptive Rewards for VLM-Guided Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21621)
*Zewen Wu,Ruizhe Liu,Pei Zhou,Feng Chen,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPEAR 通过可区分性感知查询选择与自适应奖励过渡，把 VLM 变成高效偏好预言机，在真机 ALOHA 任务上实现 88.9% 平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 可用于机器人操作的自动偏好标注，但每次查询推理成本高，且面对视觉上困难的比较时常弃答（abstain），导致部署不实用。核心矛盾是查询效率与探索效率双重瓶颈。

Method: 两个机制：(1) distinguishability-aware query selection，只挑选有信息量且可区分的比较对以降低弃答率；(2) adaptive reward transition，先用 VLM 合成引导进行探索自举，再过渡到基于偏好的细化。属于 VLM 偏好奖励 + 在线 RL 的机器人学习框架。

Result: 操作基准上平均成功率 88.9%，比基线高出 23.3 个百分点，并显著降低弃答率与墙钟训练时间；在真实硬件上完全在线学习三个 Mobile ALOHA 操作任务，验证端到端真机可用性。

Conclusion: 通过同时缓解查询效率与探索难题，SPEAR 使 VLM 成为可扩展机器人学习的实用偏好预言机，免去人工奖励工程。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable automated preference labeling for robotic manipulation, yet their deployment remains impractical since each query incurs substantial inference cost, while VLMs frequently abstain on visually challenging comparisons. We propose SPEAR, a framework that transforms VLMs into efficient preference oracles via two synergistic mechanisms. First, we design distinguishability-aware query selection to reduce abstention rates by targeting only informative and distinguishable comparisons. Second, we deploy an adaptive reward transition that bootstraps exploration with VLM-synthesized guidance before preference-based refinement. Experiments on the manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that SPEAR achieves 88.9% average success, outperforming baselines by 23.3 percentage points, while significantly reducing abstention and wall-clock training time on contact-rich tasks. SPEAR learns three Mobile ALOHA manipulation tasks fully online on real hardware, demonstrating end-to-end real-world applicability. By addressing query efficiency and exploration challenges, SPEAR establishes VLMs as practical preference oracles for scalable robot learning without manual reward engineering.

</details>


### [186] [Diffeomorphic Hidden-State Steering: Improving Imitation Learning Policy Robustness through Expert Hidden-State Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21666)
*KwangBin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Diffeomorphic Hidden-State Steering (DHS)，利用专家演示在冻结策略中重放得到的隐藏状态学习可逆非线性坐标映射，按专家方差对不同坐标进行差异化缩放以在测试时引导隐藏状态，在不额外示范与重训练的前提下提升模仿学习策略在观测噪声下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环执行中小误差会累积并破坏已训练模仿学习策略；作者希望仅利用原始专家示范，在测试时通过隐藏状态引导提升鲁棒性，无需额外示范或策略重训练。观察到专家隐藏状态变异集中于少数维度，且捕捉该变异的方向随区域而变，线性手段难以直接刻画这种几何结构。

Method: 在冻结策略上重放专家示范收集隐藏状态，学习从隐藏状态到新坐标的可逆非线性（微分同胚式）映射；在变换后的坐标系中对低专家方差坐标施加更强衰减、高方差坐标更弱衰减，再经逆映射回隐藏状态空间，从而得到随当前隐藏状态变化的引导方向，同时保留全部坐标（区别于硬投影降维）。在四个仿真操作任务中与未修改的Transformer策略对照，并与线性坐标、硬投影、满秩重缩放及动作平滑做受控比较。

Result: 在四个仿真操作任务、观测噪声条件下，DHS相对未修改的Transformer策略提升平均成功率。受控比较显示：学习到的非线性坐标在硬投影与满秩重缩放两种设定下均优于线性坐标的平均成功率。在部分任务中，DHS结合动作平滑比单独动作平滑取得更高的平均成功率。

Conclusion: 测试时隐藏状态引导可作为无需额外示范与重训练的鲁棒性提升手段，隐藏状态空间的可逆非线性重参数化与方差感知缩放优于线性投影或重缩放；效果随任务而异，与动作平滑的叠加亦仅在某些任务中带来增益，尚缺乏真实机器人验证。

Abstract: Small observation or action errors can compound during closed-loop execution and disrupt a trained imitation learning policy. We study whether the original expert demonstrations can improve robustness through test-time hidden-state steering without additional demonstrations or policy retraining. Our analysis of a trained policy finds that expert hidden-state variation is concentrated in relatively few dimensions, while the directions capturing this variation differ across regions. Motivated by this geometry, we propose Diffeomorphic Hidden-State Steering (DHS), which learns an invertible nonlinear coordinate map from expert hidden states obtained by replaying the demonstrations through the frozen policy. DHS attenuates coordinates with low expert variance more strongly than those with high variance, then maps the modified coordinates back to hidden-state space. This allows steering directions to vary with the current hidden state while retaining every coordinate. Across four simulated manipulation tasks, DHS improves mean success under observation noise over the unmodified Transformer policies. Controlled comparisons yield higher mean success with learned nonlinear coordinates than with linear coordinates under both hard projection and full-rank rescaling. Combining DHS with action smoothing improves mean success rate under observation noise in certain tasks compared with action smoothing alone.

</details>


### [187] [MAIA: Anticipating Interaction Anomalies in Contact-Rich Manipulation via Multimodal World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21686)
*Yi Qiao,Maolin Zheng,Haoran Sun,Lujia Dong,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAIA是一个多模态异常预判框架，通过融合视觉-动作-力的世界模型学习标称交互动力学并预测计划动作的视觉与力响应，配合轻量异常头提前预判交互异常；同时发布CRIA真实世界接触丰富操作数据集（2,852条轨迹、18个任务、帧级异常标注）。实验显示其在已见/新任务上bACC为0.765/0.710，中位预警提前28.0/25.1步，并将端到端任务成功率从52.6%提升至61.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作中，执行偏差需要尽早发现才能采取纠正措施，但现有监控方法多为反应式的，仅在异常可观测之后才识别。预判异常困难的原因在于：视觉观测只能部分揭示物理交互，而计划动作描述的是意图运动而非其实际物理后果。因此需要建模未来物理后果以实现主动异常预判。

Method: 提出MAIA多模态异常预判框架：其视觉-动作-力世界模型学习标称交互动力学，预测计划动作对应的视觉与力响应；一个轻量级异常头在此基础上预测即将发生的交互异常。为系统评估，构建CRIA真实世界多模态数据集，包含2,852条轨迹、18个接触丰富操作任务，并提供异常起始与演化的帧级标注。

Result: 在已见与新颖任务上，MAIA的异常判别精度优于代表性基线，且预警更早：bACC分别为0.765和0.710，中位预判提前时间为28.0步和25.1步。此外，将预测用于异常感知的动作选择后，无需重训练底层操作策略即可将端到端任务成功率从52.6%提升至61.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，建模未来物理后果对主动异常预判具有重要价值，融合视觉-动作-力的世界模型能够提前预判接触丰富操作中的异常并改善下游任务表现。

Abstract: Reliable contact-rich manipulation requires detecting execution deviations early enough for corrective action. Yet most monitoring methods are reactive, identifying anomalies only after they become observable. Anticipating such anomalies is challenging because visual observations only partially reveal physical interaction, while planned actions describe intended motion rather than their actual physical consequences. We address this problem with MAIA, a multimodal framework for anomaly anticipation. Its vision-action-force world model learns nominal interaction dynamics and predicts the visual and force responses of planned actions, while a lightweight anomaly head predicts impending interaction anomalies. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce CRIA, a real-world multimodal dataset of 2,852 trajectories spanning 18 contact-rich manipulation tasks, with frame-level annotations of anomaly onset and evolution. Experiments on both seen and novel tasks show that MAIA achieves more accurate anomaly discrimination than representative baselines while providing earlier warnings, with bACC scores of 0.765 and 0.710 and median anticipation lead times of 28.0 and 25.1 steps, respectively. Moreover, using these predictions for anomaly-aware action selection improves end-to-end task success from 52.6% to 61.5% without retraining the underlying manipulation policy. These results highlight the value of modeling future physical consequences for proactive anomaly anticipation.

</details>


### [188] [Uni-PNP: Unified Prehensile and Non-prehensile Policy Learning for Generalizable Object Pose Reaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21735)
*Wenxuan Zhu,Yuze Wang,Jiangran Lyu,Jiayi Chen,Yixin Zheng,Mi Yan,Li Yi,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-PNP：统一策略联合学习抓取与非抓取操作，通过接触动力学数据集预训练几何编码器并在两阶段RL课程中训练，实现跨物体与夹爪的位姿到达，显著超越基线并展示真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将抓取（prehensile）与非抓取（non-prehensile）操作分开开发，再通过序列化预设中间目标的原语拼接整合，限制了接触运动与抓取的协同；同时对物体/夹爪几何与环境支撑敏感，难以部署。作者希望用单一统一策略同时学得两类接触操作能力。

Method: 1) 构建仿真交互生成的接触动力学数据集（多样物体×多样夹爪），预训练几何编码器以预测接触诱导的物体运动；2) 用两阶段强化学习课程训练策略，从可抓取物体过渡到不可抓取物体，促使策略自发发现非抓取交互并与抓取整合。

Result: 在数百个已见物体（配夹爪）上达到76.39%成功率，未见物体57.91%，较最强位姿到达基线分别高出20.52和17.79个百分点。观察到多种涌现行为：非抓取与抓取式重定位、需先倾倒后抓取的抓取获得、抓取状态下的桌面辅助再定向，并成功迁移到真实场景。

Conclusion: 统一的预训练+课程RL框架可有效实现抓取与非抓取操作的协调整合，并对新物体、新夹爪及真实环境具有较好泛化与迁移能力，为接触丰富操作提供可行路径。

Abstract: Object manipulation often requires combining non-prehensile actions with grasping. However, existing approaches typically develop these capabilities separately and integrate them by sequencing distinct primitives through prescribed intermediate objectives, limiting the coordination of contact motions and grasping. Their integration also remains sensitive to object and gripper geometry and depends on particular environmental supports, complicating real-world deployment. We present Uni-PNP, a unified policy that jointly learns prehensile and non-prehensile manipulation for object pose reaching across diverse objects and grippers. To achieve this, we first pretrain a geometry encoder to predict contact-induced object motion using a contact-dynamics dataset constructed from simulated interactions between diverse objects and grippers. We then train the policy in a two-stage reinforcement learning curriculum from graspable objects to ungraspable peers, encouraging the discovery of non-prehensile interactions and their integration with grasping. Experiments show 76.39% success on hundreds of seen objects with grippers, and 57.91% on unseen peers, outperforming the strongest pose-reaching baseline by 20.52 and 17.79 percentage points, respectively. We also analyze emergent behaviors, including nonprehensile and prehensile repositioning, grasp acquisition that requires grasping after tipping, and table-assisted reorientation while grasping, and transfer to the real-world scenario. Project page available at this http URL .

</details>


### [189] [Comfortably Stuck: Evaluation Pitfalls in Bench2Drive's Closed-Loop Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21741)
*Thomas Schwarzfischer,Emad Aghajanzadeh,Moritz Thoma,Nael Fasfous,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Pierpaolo Mori,Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 指出 Bench2Drive 闭环评测协议存在缺陷：舒适度指标实现有误且奖励静止（与原指标相关性 r=0.97），作者提出基于距离的修正指标（相关性降至 0.06），并揭示导航信号供给中控制器替换模型转向、评测目标点与训练不一致两个陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环评测被认为比开环更能反映端到端驾驶模型的真实能力，Bench2Drive 已成为该领域主流基准，但其评测协议本身可能误判模型表现，需要审查并修正。

Method: 对 Bench2Drive 的舒适度指标（源自 nuPlan）进行实现层面审计，指出实现错误与分段方案奖励静止的问题；提出基于距离的修正舒适度指标；在八个模型上做相关性分析，并在匹配行驶速度条件下重新比较驾驶分数—舒适度权衡；以 UniAD 和 VAD 为对象，通过默认控制器覆盖模型转向角、评测目标点与训练分布不一致两个消融实验验证导航信号陷阱。

Result: 原舒适度指标与静止时长相关性高达 r=0.97，修正指标降至 r=0.06；在匹配速度后，原指标所报告的强“驾驶分数—舒适度”权衡不再可检测，说明舒适度实际与行驶速度而非驾驶质量冲突；两个导航信号陷阱在 UniAD 与 VAD 上均被证实，且影响差异显著。

Conclusion: Bench2Drive 现有闭环评测协议会系统性误导模型排名，未来闭环评测应显式检查这些陷阱；作者将把修正代码独立发布，使修正后舒适度分数可直接由日志轨迹计算，无需重跑闭环仿真。

Abstract: Closed-loop evaluation is widely regarded as a more faithful way to assess end-to-end (E2E) driving models than open-loop evaluation, and Bench2Drive, built on the CARLA simulator, has become the dominant benchmark for it. We show that its evaluation protocol itself contains pitfalls that can misrepresent the models scored on it. Bench2Drive's comfort metric, adapted from nuPlan, has several implementation errors and a segmentation scheme that rewards standing still, so it mostly measures how long a vehicle stays stationary, correlating with stationary time at r = 0.97 across eight models. We propose a corrected, distance-based metric that cuts that correlation to r = 0.06. Under it, the strong driving-score/comfort tradeoff the original metric reports is no longer detectable once models are compared at matched driving speed, which suggests that comfort trades against how fast a model drives rather than against how well it drives. We also uncover two pitfalls in how the harness supplies navigation signals: the default controller can replace a model's own steering with an angle toward the route target point, and the target point supplied at evaluation time can differ substantially from what a model saw in training. We demonstrate both on UniAD and VAD, where their effects differ sharply, and argue that future closed-loop evaluations should check for them. We will release all fixes as standalone code, so corrected comfort scores can be obtained from logged trajectories without re-running closed-loop simulation.

</details>


### [190] [Trajectory-based Diffusion from One-shot Human Video for Generalizable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21792)
*Guoping Pan,Xinmiao Yu,Wannan Cao,Jincheng Li,Baolin Jiang,Yizhe Li,Yi Cheng,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TDV 是一个基于轨迹的数据生成框架，从人类视频中提取物体轨迹作为跨模态、跨本体的中间表示，从而无需遥操作即可实现可泛化的机器人操作策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习依赖昂贵的人工遥操作数据采集，扩展性差；人类视频虽隐含操作知识，但存在视觉域差与缺乏显式动作标签两大迁移障碍。

Method: 利用即插即用的 6D 位姿估计从人类视频中提取任务相关物体的轨迹，抑制背景与视角干扰；以轨迹作为模态无关的中间表示（独立于视觉域与本体），据此进行高效的数据生成，用于训练可泛化的操作策略。

Result: 在 RLBench 仿真七项任务上接近 100% 成功率，优于先前方法；真实世界实验中实现从单次人类演示出发的高效轨迹生成，并达成跨本体泛化操作。

Conclusion: 轨迹这一模态无关中间表示可有效桥接人类视频与机器人动作，缓解视觉域差与动作标签缺失问题，实现单样本演示下的跨本体泛化操作，为可扩展的模仿学习数据来源提供思路。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to perform complex tasks by leveraging human-collected demonstrations. However, most existing approaches require labor-intensive data collection with additional teleoperation systems, which hinders scalability. Human videos represent a natural source of data that implicitly contain knowledge of manipulation behaviors. However, transferring them to robots remains challenging due to the visual domain gap and the absence of explicit action labels. In this work, we introduce TDV, a trajectory-based framework that leverages data generation to enable generalizable manipulation. TDV focuses on task-relevant objects by leveraging plug-and-play 6D pose estimation techniques to extract object trajectories from human videos, thereby mitigating the impact of irrelevant backgrounds and varying viewpoints. As a modality-agnostic intermediate representation, trajectories are inherently independent of the visual domains and different embodiments, enabling efficient data generation. Our method outperforms previous approaches on RLBench simulation tasks, achieving near 100\% success rates across seven tasks. In real-world experiments, TDV enables cross-embodiment generalization manipulation from one-shot human demonstrations by leveraging efficient trajectory generation techniques.

</details>


### [191] [Cross-Embodiment Causal Transfer from Human Videos to Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21798)
*Xingyu Zhang,Yingchao Li,Peizheng Guo,Jie Hu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CBST，用 causal beam 的视角分析人类视频到机器人操作的可迁移子任务知识，仅保留能维持局部机制输出并对输入保持非平凡响应的上下文变量，用机器人示范将其对齐到任务级语义变量，再以世界-动作模型生成动作，在操作基准上超过对应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规模化机器人操作模型依赖大量交互数据，而真实机器人数据昂贵；人类操作视频丰富多样，却因形态、动作空间与控制机制差异无法直接作为机器人动作监督。因此需要回答：人类视频中究竟哪些知识应跨具身迁移到机器人操作。

Method: 以 causal beam 为分析工具研究子任务迁移，明确应保留的上下文条件与交互依赖；提出 Causal-Beam-Guided Subtask Transfer：从人类视频学习操作相关表示，并用机器人示范将其 grounding 到任务级语义变量；在预定义操作模型内，用 causal-beam 约束选择上下文信息（保留的赋值需维持局部机制输出，同时对保留输入保持非平凡响应）；所选语义信息条件化一个 world-action model，使机器人可直接从自身观测生成动作，无需人-机运动对应。

Result: 在机器人操作基准上，任务成功率相对相应的 world-action model 基线有所提升。

Conclusion: 因果视角下的子任务迁移可有效筛选人类视频中可跨具身迁移的语义知识，避免对动作层面的直接监督依赖，为利用人类视频提升机器人操作提供了一条可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Scaling robot manipulation models requires large amounts of interaction data, yet collecting diverse real-robot experience remains costly. Human manipulation videos, in contrast, provide abundant and diverse task experience that could potentially benefit robot learning. However, differences in morphology, action spaces, and control mechanisms make human motions unsuitable as direct supervision for robot actions, raising a scientific question: what knowledge in human videos should be transferred across embodiments to robot manipulation? To address this question, we analyze subtask transfer through the lens of causal beams, focusing on which contextual conditions and interaction dependencies should be retained to support local task effects. Based on this perspective, we propose Causal-Beam-Guided Subtask Transfer (CBST). CBST learns manipulation-related representations from human videos and grounds them in task-level semantic variables using robot demonstrations. Within a predefined manipulation model, causal-beam constraints guide contextual information selection by requiring retained assignments to sustain local mechanism outputs while preserving nontrivial responsiveness to retained inputs. The selected semantic information conditions a world-action model, allowing the robot to generate actions from its own observations without requiring human-robot motion correspondence. Experiments on robot manipulation benchmarks show improvements in task success over the corresponding world-action model baselines.

</details>


### [192] [Time-Optimal Ergodic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21823)
*Christian Sulana,Christian Hughes,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出时间最优遍历检索（TOER）公式：将检测建模为泊松过程，在目标函数中纳入返回阶段，最小化总任务时间，实验较遍历基线最多缩短19%任务时长，并验证2D/3D非凸环境下的仿真与硬件可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遍历控制（ergodic control）规划能实现按目标分布比例访问的时间最优搜索路径，但其优化目标是完整确定性搜索的时长；而实际检索任务在轨迹中未知时刻完成检测，还必须返回基地，因此原有公式与检索任务的时间最优目标不匹配。

Method: 将目标检测建模为泊松过程，重新构造时间最优遍历搜索问题，把返回基地阶段纳入优化目标以最小化总任务时间，得到TOER方法；在2D与3D、含非凸几何的环境中进行仿真与硬件验证。

Result: TOER相比遍历基线最多缩短19%的任务时长，且返回阶段代价越高优势越明显；非凸几何环境及2D/3D搜索域中仿真与硬件实验均验证了方法兼容性。

Conclusion: 将检测随机性与返回阶段统一纳入时间最优遍历规划，可显著提升检索任务的整体效率，且对复杂几何与不同维度任务具有泛化性。

Abstract: Retrieval tasks are a form of search task where a robot must explore, find an object, and return with it as quickly as possible. Recent work in ergodic control has demonstrated time-optimal path planning for search tasks where visitation is proportionally matched, within some tolerance, to a given target distribution. However, these planners minimize the duration of a full deterministic search, while retrieval missions end their search at an unknown point in the trajectory and must return to a given base. In this work, we propose a new formulation of the time-optimal ergodic search problem that models detection as a Poisson process and minimizes total mission time by including the return phase in the objective. Experimental results show that time-optimal ergodic retrieval (TOER) achieves up to $19\%$ shorter mission durations relative to ergodic baselines and that this advantage grows as the return phase becomes more costly. Simulation and hardware implementations validate TOER's compatibility with environments of non-convex geometry as well as both 2D and 3D search domains.

</details>


### [193] [MAGMA: Multiplier-Augmented Geometric Motion Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21852)
*César E. Ramos Chuquiure,Vansh Thakur,Challen Enninful Adu,Ram Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAGMA，一种基于仿射几何热流(AGHF)并融合增广拉格朗日与原始-对偶流的轨迹优化器，用于求解高维受约束机械系统的最优控制问题，并在含障碍、状态与执行器限制的轨迹生成任务上以更少的调参取得高质量解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维机械系统的最优控制因非线性动力学与非凸可行域而困难；现有求解器在约束处理与调参负担上存在明显痛点。

Method: 在AGHF框架中引入增广拉格朗日形式，通过原始-对偶流进行求解；理论上刻画原始-对偶AGHF，证明MAGMA等价于在适当定义能量上的梯度流。

Result: 在含障碍、状态限制与执行器限制的多类挑战性轨迹生成问题上验证；与当前最优求解器对比，在最困难问题上以显著更低的调参代价获得高质量解。

Conclusion: MAGMA为受约束高维运动规划提供有理论保证的梯度流视角与实用求解器，兼具解质量与调参效率优势。

Abstract: Solving optimal control problems for high-dimensional mechanical systems subject to constraints remains challenging due to nonlinear dynamics and non-convex feasible sets. This paper introduces MAGMA(Multiplier-Augmented Geometric Motion Algorithm), an Affine Geometric Heat Flow (AGHF)-based trajectory optimizer that incorporates an augmented Lagrangian framework solved via a primal-dual flow. A theoretical characterization of the primal-dual AGHF is provided, establishing that MAGMA is a gradient flow on an appropriately defined energy. MAGMA is validated across a variety of challenging trajectory generation problems with obstacles, state limits, and actuation limits. Comparative evaluations against state-of-the-art solvers demonstrate that MAGMA achieves high quality solutions with significantly reduced tuning effort on the hardest problems.

</details>


### [194] [Sub-Millisecond Reflexes for Continuous Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21913)
*Jon Škerlj,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于增强型 ROS 2 兼容多模态控制器的开源亚毫秒级反射引擎，通过无锁指针操作实现控制模态的确定性实时切换，并在 Franka 机械臂上验证了低于 0.1 ms 的切换延迟与连续安全运行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续物理人机交互要求机器人在不中断任务执行的前提下快速调整控制行为，但现有 ROS 2 控制架构依赖"停止-切换"（stop-to-switch）机制，引入显著延迟并打断执行流程，无法满足反射级实时性与安全性需求。

Method: 在 ROS 2 兼容的增强型多模态控制器基础上构建开源反射引擎：将控制器转换实现为无锁（lock-free）指针操作，消除运行时中间件开销，实现控制模态间的确定性切换，并集成用于缓解接触危害与运动学极限违规的反射行为。

Result: Franka 机械臂实验显示切换延迟低于 0.1 ms，比传统 ros2_control 快四个数量级以上，且安全关键事件期间仍保持连续运行；在多阶段操作任务中执行时间减少 3.5%，峰值轨迹加加速度（jerk）降低三倍以上。

Conclusion: 通过消除运行时中间件开销并以无锁指针操作实现控制器转换，可在开源机器人框架内实现反射级响应能力，为连续物理人机交互提供低延迟、确定性、可安全运行的控制基础。

Abstract: Continuous physical human-robot interaction requires robots to rapidly adapt their control behaviour without interrupting task execution. However, existing ROS 2 control architectures rely on stop-to-switch mechanisms that introduce substantial delays and interrupt execution. We present an open-source, sub-millisecond reflex engine based on an enhanced ROS 2-compatible multimode controller that enables deterministic switching between control modalities and integrates reflex behaviours for mitigating contact hazards and kinematic limit violations. Experiments on a Franka Robotics manipulator demonstrate switching delays below 0.1 ms, more than four orders of magnitude faster than conventional ros2_control, while maintaining continuous operation during safety-critical events. In a multi-stage manipulation task, the proposed framework reduced execution time by 3.5% and peak trajectory jerk by more than threefold. By eliminating runtime middleware overhead, controller transitions are implemented as lock-free pointer operations, enabling reflex-level responsiveness within an open-source robotics framework.

</details>


### [195] [What Should Robot Policies Learn to Predict? Rethinking Supervision Targets in Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21915)
*Haitao Shen,Hanyu Liu,Yihan Lin,Huiping Liu,Junbo Cui,Daoheng Li,Yang Li,Jing Zhang,Wenxin Li,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出统一的VLA训练框架（双流MMDiT + 样本级路由器），将每个训练样本显式分配给动作预测或辅助目标（潜动作LA/未来视觉FV），在固定预算下证明辅助监督可超越动作独占训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通常以动作预测为唯一主监督，辅助目标（潜动作、未来视觉预测）虽被证明有益，但与动作预测争夺同一训练预算，二者如何权衡缺乏系统研究。作者希望回答：固定预算下，把大量样本分给辅助目标而非动作预测，是否能提升策略学习。

Method: 构建统一VLA框架，将双流多模态扩散Transformer（MMDiT）与样本级路由器耦合，路由器为每个训练样本分配动作预测或某一辅助目标，使监督分配显式化、可控。在此框架内实例化三种LA目标（基于LAPA及其DINO特征变体）与三种FV目标（基于DINOv3、V-JEPA 2、Wan2.2表征），在同等预算下对比不同分配方案。

Result: 在LIBERO、CALVIN及四个真实机器人任务上，合适的LA与FV监督在相同预算下优于纯动作训练，即使动作预测仅获得不超过一半训练样本；FV总体优于LA，但最强LA目标在CALVIN上仍具竞争力。诊断分析表明：基于DINO的LA目标侧重相对运动，FV目标侧重绝对位置与任务身份。

Conclusion: 机器人策略"学习预测什么"以及"训练预算如何在各目标间分配"应被视为显式设计选择，而非默认采用动作主导训练。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are typically trained with action prediction as the primary supervision signal, since robots ultimately execute actions. Auxiliary objectives such as latent-action (LA) and future-vision (FV) prediction are often added to improve policy learning, yet they compete with action prediction for the same training budget. We ask whether, under a fixed budget, allocating a substantial share of training samples to such objectives, rather than to further action prediction, can improve policy learning. We build a unified VLA framework that couples a two-stream multimodal diffusion transformer (MMDiT) with a sample-level router, which assigns each training sample to either action prediction or an auxiliary objective and thus makes supervision allocation explicit. Within this framework, we instantiate three LA targets based on LAPA and its DINO-feature variants, and three FV targets based on DINOv3, V-JEPA 2, and Wan2.2 representations. Across LIBERO, CALVIN, and four real-robot tasks, suitable LA and FV supervision outperforms action-only training under the same budget, even when action prediction receives no more than half of the training samples. FV generally outperforms LA, although the strongest LA target remains competitive on CALVIN. Diagnostic analyses suggest that DINO-based LA targets emphasize relative motion, whereas FV targets emphasize absolute position and task identity. We therefore argue that what robot policies learn to predict, and how training is allocated among these targets, should be treated as explicit design choices rather than defaulting to action-dominant training.

</details>


### [196] [TS-WAM: Temporal Latent Actions and World-Action Coupling for Vision-Language Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21922)
*Menghao Yin,Jiaju Yin,Xiyue Dong,Lixin Xu,Zhenhui Zhang,Ke Zhang,Yating Feng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TS-WAM：轻量级师生世界-动作模型，通过 vlm-jepa 自监督时序学习与 delta-lwm 潜动作/未来视觉预测，从无动作视频与机器人演示中联合学习，实现强泛化的操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略依赖带动作标注的机器人演示，且缺乏显式的未来视觉状态预测机制，导致无法利用无动作视频、易过拟合演示条件、泛化与鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出 TS-WAM 师生世界-动作模型：(1) vlm-jepa 框架用 EMA 目标分支做自监督时序表征学习，可混合机器人演示与无动作人类视频，无需外部预训练教师；(2) delta-lwm 从当前观测与指令预测潜动作，并据此预测未来视觉特征，在推理时引导动作生成；(3) 端到端统一训练，联合优化时序表征、视觉动力学与机器人控制，无需分离的潜动作/世界模型预训练；推理时移除 EMA 分支以保持轻量。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、SimplerEnv 仿真基准及 Franka Research 3、RevoTron 真机操作实验上，均取得较强的操作性能、泛化性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将自监督时序表征学习、显式未来视觉预测与动作生成统一在一个端到端、可轻量化部署的师生框架中，是提升 VLA 策略数据利用率与泛化鲁棒性的有效路径。

Abstract: Many vision-language-action policies rely on action-labeled robot demonstrations and lack an explicit mechanism for predicting future visual states. These limitations hinder learning from action-free videos and can encourage overfitting to demonstration conditions, weakening generalization and robustness. We present TS-WAM, a lightweight Teacher-Student World-Action Model that addresses these challenges through self-supervised temporal learning and explicit future prediction. Its vlm-jepa framework learns from heterogeneous data, including robot demonstrations and action-free human videos, using an EMA target branch without requiring a separately trained external teacher. By coupling vlm-jepa with delta-lwm, TS-WAM learns to predict latent actions from the current observation and instruction and uses them to predict future visual features that guide action generation at inference. A unified end-to-end training architecture jointly optimizes temporal representation learning, visual dynamics, and robot control without a separate latent-action or world-model pretraining stage. The EMA target branch is removed at inference, keeping the deployed policy lightweight. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and SimplerEnv, together with real-world manipulation experiments on Franka Research 3 and RevoTron, demonstrate that TS-WAM achieves strong manipulation performance, generalization, and robustness.

</details>


### [197] [De-Risking Robotic Welding for In-Space Metal Joining Through Physics-Grounded Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21939)
*Zhou Daniel Hao,Umer Masood Chaudry,Robert Shaw,Andrew Cheney,Nick Ludford,Mark Sims,Hongbiao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ISPARK项目提出面向在轨机器人焊接的物理基础数字孪生原型：有限体积熔池模型 + GP/神经网络代理模型 + 六轴机械臂仿真，用于焊接工艺与闭环控制策略的离线筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务、制造与装配需要可靠的金属连接与在轨维修手段，但微重力与低压条件下的机器人焊接特性表征不足、飞行级实验机会有限，亟需先在地面用可信的仿真框架降低工艺与控制器开发风险。

Method: 构建模型驱动原型：有限体积热-熔池模型（含固液相变、等效热毛细输运、局域压力参数），并以高斯过程与神经网络代理模型替代昂贵求解；重力效应通过静水压-毛细压比值单独评估，不进入热能量方程；结合模拟六轴机械臂（UR5）与比例控制器动态调节协同电源设定。

Result: 五折交叉验证中GP在全部8个输出上的归一化平均绝对误差均低于神经网络；10个留出边界算例中80个求解响应有74个落入名义90% GP预测区间；三输出GP查询耗时249微秒（含缩放与预测方差）。L形焊缝仿真中，闭环12个工作点全部、开环12个中9个落在示例性1.0–3.0 mm根侧熔宽带内。

Conclusion: 仿真结果支持将该框架用于实验前的焊接条件与控制策略筛选，但模型与控制器有效性仍需热真空、定向减重力及机器人硬件在环实验验证。

Abstract: Growing interest in in-space servicing, manufacturing, and assembly has renewed attention on reliable metal joining for modular construction and on-orbit repair. Robotic welding is promising, but reduced-gravity and low-pressure operation remains poorly characterised and flight-like tests are limited. The UK Space Agency-supported ISPARK project is developing a physics-grounded digital twin for structured process de-risking. This paper evaluates a model-based prototype combining a finite-volume thermal weld-pool model, Gaussian-process (GP) and neural-network surrogates, and a simulated six-axis manipulator. The reduced model includes solid-liquid phase change, effective thermocapillary transport, and a local-pressure parameter. Gravity is assessed separately through a hydrostatic-to-capillary pressure ratio and does not enter the thermal energy equation. Across repeated five-fold cross-validation, the GP has lower normalised mean absolute error than the neural network for all eight outputs. In 10 held-out boundary cases, 74 of 80 solver responses fall within nominal 90% GP predictive intervals. A three-output GP query takes 249 microseconds, including scaling and predictive variance. In a simulated UR5 L-shaped-seam study, a proportional controller adjusts the synergic power-source setting as travel speed falls near the corner. Finite-volume re-evaluation finds all 12 closed-loop operating points and 9 of 12 open-loop points within an illustrative 1.0-3.0 mm root-side-width band. These simulation results support using the framework to screen welding conditions and control strategies before experimental tests. Thermal-vacuum, targeted reduced-gravity, and robotic hardware-in-the-loop studies are needed to assess the model and controller.

</details>


### [198] [Perceptual Comparisons of Viscoelastic Renderings: Fractional- vs Integer-Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21965)
*Gorkem Gemalmaz,Harun Tolasa,Volkan Patoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 比较分数阶(FO-SLS)与整数阶粘弹性模型(SLS、广义Maxwell)在触觉渲染中的感知真实感，用物理辨识和人在回路感知优化两套参数选择方法，人类实验表明FO-SLS被感知显著更真实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性材料的真实触觉渲染需再现蠕变与应力松弛等时变行为，分数阶粘弹性模型能紧凑、准确地刻画这类记忆效应，但其更高保真度是否能转化为触觉交互中的感知收益尚不清楚。

Method: 对比FO-SLS与3参数SLS、5参数广义Maxwell三种模型；两种参数选择途径：(1)基于最小二乘的系统辨识拟合物理参考材料，建立客观基线；(2)基于定性反馈的人在回路优化与聚合，获得群体级感知最优参数；开展人类被试实验评估感知真实感。

Result: 在两种参数选择方法下，人类被试实验均表明FO-SLS模型被感知为显著比两种整数阶模型更真实。

Conclusion: 分数阶粘弹性模型不仅在理论上合理，在触觉渲染蠕变与松弛主导的粘弹性行为时也具有感知相关性，为真实触觉渲染提供实验证据。

Abstract: Realistic haptic rendering of viscoelastic materials requires reproducing time-dependent behaviors such as creep and stress relaxation, which arise from the material's inherent memory effects. Fractional-order viscoelastic models have been shown to capture these dynamics accurately and compactly, but it remains unclear whether their increased fidelity translates into perceptual benefits during haptic interaction. In this work, we investigate whether users perceive fractional-order models as more realistic than conventional integer-order models while rendering viscoelastic materials. We compare the fractional-order standard linear solid (FO-SLS) model with two integer-order alternatives: the 3-parameter SLS model and the 5-parameter generalized Maxwell model. We consider two complementary approaches to optimal parameter selection to evaluate rendering realism from both objective and perceptual perspectives: In the first approach, we determine the model parameters via least-squares based system identification of physical viscoelastic reference materials to establish evaluation baselines. In the second approach, we determine population-level perceptually optimal parameters using a qualitative-feedback-based human-in-the-loop optimization and aggregation method. This method establishes model parameters that maximize perceived realism of viscoelastic behaviors through qualitative evaluations. Human-subject experiments indicate that the FO-SLS model is perceived as significantly more realistic than both integer-order alternatives under both parameter selection approaches. Overall, this study provides experimental evidence that fractional-order viscoelastic models are not only theoretically well-motivated, but also perceptually relevant for realistic haptic rendering of creep- and relaxation-dominated behaviors of viscoelastic materials.

</details>


### [199] [Towards bio-inspired visual navigation: a neuromorphic navigation paradigm for MAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21978)
*Junjie Jiang,Ankang Zhou,Zheng Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个端到端事件驱动机器人视觉导航范式，融合神经形态视觉、脉冲神经网络与神经形态处理器，实现与ANN方法相当的MAV导航性能，同时显著降低功耗（感知模块19.57%、Loihi上导航模块12.37%的ANN功耗）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物视觉导航在极低功耗下具备卓越鲁棒性，而依赖人工神经网络的帧式机器人系统在鲁棒性与能效两方面均受限，尤其对复杂环境中能量受限的微型飞行器。神经形态感知与计算受生物视觉系统事件驱动、稀疏、异步原则启发，有望成为鲁棒且高能效导航的替代方案。

Method: 提出首个端到端事件驱动神经形态范式，整合神经形态视觉、脉冲神经网络与神经形态处理器。先用脉冲感知表征模块从事件流提取环境表征，再输入脉冲导航模块，通过引导学习与群体编码在少时间步内高效学习策略，最终输出航点并转换为可执行轨迹点。

Result: 导航性能与基于ANN的方法相当，同时大幅降低功耗：脉冲感知模块功耗仅为其ANN对应部分的19.57%；部署于Intel Loihi的脉冲导航模块功耗仅为运行于NVIDIA Orin NX的ANN对应部分的12.37%。在仿真与真实部署中实现了完整神经形态视觉导航系统。

Conclusion: 研究确立了神经形态范式用于自主导航的可行性，为鲁棒、低功耗的MAV导航提供了仿生路径；相较以往仅关注较简单导航子任务的神经形态系统，本文展示了覆盖仿真与真实部署的完整系统。

Abstract: Biological visual navigation achieves remarkable robustness at extremely low power, whereas frame-based robotic systems relying on artificial neural networks remain limited in both respects, particularly for energy-constrained micro aerial vehicles (MAVs) operating in complex environments. Inspired by the event-driven, sparse, and asynchronous principles of biological visual systems, neuromorphic sensing and computation provide a promising alternative for robust and energy-efficient navigation. In this paper, we propose the first end-to-end event-driven neuromorphic paradigm for MAV visual navigation in complex environments by integrating neuromorphic vision, spiking neural networks, and neuromorphic processors. The proposed method first extracts the environmental representation from event streams using a spiking perceptual representation module. This representation is fed into a spiking navigation module, where guidance learning and population coding enable efficient policy learning with few time steps. The navigation module then outputs a waypoint converted into executable trajectory points. Experiments show that the proposed neuromorphic method achieves navigation performance comparable to ANN-based methods while markedly reducing power consumption. The spiking perceptual module consumes only 19.57% of the power of its ANN counterpart, while the spiking navigation module deployed on Intel Loihi consumes only 12.37% of that required by its ANN counterpart running on NVIDIA Orin NX. Beyond prior neuromorphic systems focused mainly on relatively simple navigation subtasks, we demonstrate a complete neuromorphic visual navigation system spanning simulation and real-world deployment. Our results establish the feasibility of the neuromorphic paradigm for autonomous navigation and provide a bio-inspired pathway toward robust and low-power MAV navigation.

</details>


### [200] [GRID-Planner: Graph-Structured Relational Reward Shaping for Diffusion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21984)
*Zhao Yang,Zezhong Qian,Yinan Shi,Longjun Liu,Yawei Jueluo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRID-Planner 提出图-奖励融合的扩散规划框架，将图感知重要性加权、时间索引关系奖励代理与优势加权扩散目标结合，形成无需显式似然的 rollout-return-advantage-update 闭环，在 nuPlan 与 interPlan 上相对纯扩散基线降低碰撞率 35.8%、车道偏离 60.8%、抖动 27.4%，规划分数提升 3.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规划需在复杂多智能体交互下选出安全、合规、舒适的轨迹，但规划关键场景中的闭环决策仍具挑战。现有扩散与强化学习规划器多依赖通用奖励，关系结构只用于表征，未让规划关键轨迹真正参与策略改进。

Method: GRID-Planner 将图结构信息融入奖励与策略优化：采用图感知重要性加权，构建时间索引的关系奖励代理，并使用优势加权扩散目标配合在线自蒸馏，从而在无显式似然条件下形成 rollout—回报—优势—更新的闭环。

Result: 在 nuPlan 与 interPlan 上，相较纯扩散基线，碰撞率下降 35.8%，车道偏离下降 60.8%，抖动下降 27.4%，规划分数提升 3.4%。

Conclusion: 结果表明将关系结构信号直接注入扩散规划的奖励与更新回路，可显著提升闭环规划的安全性与舒适性，为结构化-生成式规划结合提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous driving planning requires selecting trajectories that remain safe, rule-compliant, and comfortable under complex multi-agent interactions, yet robust closed-loop decision making in planning-critical scenes remains challenging. Existing diffusion- and RL-based planners often rely on generic rewards and use relational structure mainly for representation, limiting how planning-critical rollouts shape policy improvement. We present GRID-Planner, a graph--reward integrated diffusion planning framework that aligns generative planning with structured relational signals. Specifically, GRID-Planner combines graph-aware importance weighting, a time-indexed relational reward surrogate, and an advantage-weighted diffusion objective with on-policy self-distillation, forming a rollout-return-advantage-update loop without explicit likelihoods. Experiments on nuPlan and interPlan show that, compared with the diffusion-only baseline, GRID-Planner reduces collision rate, lane deviation, and jerk by 35.8\%, 60.8\%, and 27.4\%, respectively, and improves the planning score by 3.4\%. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [201] [VeriVLA: Adaptive Test-Time Verification for Flow-Based VLAs with Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21985)
*Xueli Sun,Yuxiang Lu,Yantong Liu,Xianzhe Fan,Xi Chen,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的两轴触发机制与JEPA潜空间世界模型验证器，对flow-based VLA策略的多候选动作块进行质量排序，提升真实与仿真操作任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow-based VLA策略本身具有随机性，同一观测下不同噪声种子可产生质量差异较大的动作块，但测试时通常只采样一次且缺乏质量评估，细粒度操作中执行次优动作会造成不可逆的任务破坏。因此需要利用策略随机性，在关键状态处对候选动作块进行显式验证与排序。

Method: 方法完全免训练：用两个初始采样动作块测量块内时序变化与跨样本方差，构成两轴触发信号以检测"关键状态"；触发后，基于JEPA的潜空间世界模型预测动作条件下的未来潜状态及其任务进展变化，据此对候选动作块排序。全部在潜空间完成，避免像素重建，从而提高效率与排序准确性。仅在被触发时引入额外开销。

Result: 真实世界三个任务（笔插入、移液、苹果放置）平均完成分数从50.8%提升至65.5%，单张RTX 4090上每次触发决策额外增加326 ms（采样+验证）。仿真中RoboTwin 2.0干净设置成功率66.4%→73.6%，随机化设置62.8%→69.6%，LIBERO 96.9%→97.6%。

Conclusion: 通过触发式潜空间世界模型验证，可低成本地挖掘flow-based VLA策略的随机性红利，在真实细粒度操作与仿真基准上均取得一致提升，且不依赖额外训练。

Abstract: Flow-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are inherently stochastic: different noise seeds can produce action chunks of varying quality from the same observation. At test time, however, the policy typically commits to a single sample without an explicit estimate of its quality. In fine-grained manipulation, executing a suboptimal action can irreversibly disrupt task progress and lead to failure. To exploit policy stochasticity, we introduce a training-free two-axis trigger that uses two initial samples to measure within-chunk temporal variation and cross-sample variance, detecting the critical states. Once triggered, a JEPA-based latent world model ranks candidate action chunks by predicting action-conditioned future latent states and the corresponding changes in task progress. By operating in latent space, the verifier avoids pixel reconstruction and enables efficient and more accurate ranking. Across three real-world tasks, $\textit{i.e.,}$ pen insertion, pipetting, and apple placement), our method raises the average completion score from 50.8% to 65.5%, while adding 326 ms for sampling and verification per triggered decision on a single RTX 4090. Consistent gains are also observed in simulation, where the average success rate rises from 66.4% to 73.6% in the clean setting of RoboTwin 2.0, from 62.8% to 69.6% in its randomized setting, and from 96.9% to 97.6% on LIBERO.

</details>


### [202] [VP-EVT: Active Visibility Preservation for Embodied Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21999)
*Yanci Wen,Tianle Zeng,Jianwei Peng,Weixi Situ,Hanjing Ye,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VP-EVT将目标可见性作为运动控制的显式目标，用语言引导感知分离目标与行人观测，在遮挡时结合视觉历史与自运动估计相对位姿，并预测持续跟随是否会维持遮挡，进而用循环控制器协调视角调整与跟随；同时提出闭环评测基准OccEVT-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景中行人反复遮挡导致现有具身视觉跟踪（EVT）方法频繁长时间丢失目标：被动式跟踪缺乏主动保持可见性的机制，遮挡下目标丢失时间过长。作者因此把可见性保持从"跟随的副产品"提升为运动控制的显式优化目标。

Method: VP-EVT框架：(1) 语言引导感知，分别提取目标与行人的独立观测；(2) 当遮挡使当前观测不完整时，融合视觉历史与自运动（ego-motion）维持二者相对位置与运动的估计；(3) 结合估计结果与当前跟航点路点，预测继续跟随是否会持续造成遮挡；(4) 风险低则保留原跟随运动，风险高则由循环控制器结合更新估计与前序决策的机动上下文，协调视角调整与回归稳定跟随。另构建OccEVT-Bench闭环评测持续遮挡，并采集专家演示用于训练。

Result: 在OccEVT-Bench的Risk划分上，相对有竞争力的基线，成功率提升121.4%，可见性赤字与最长目标丢失时长分别降低78.8%和74.4%。

Conclusion: 把目标可见性显式建模为控制目标、并预测遮挡持续性来主动调整视角，是缓解具身视觉跟踪中持续性遮挡问题的有效途径；配套的闭环遮挡基准为该问题提供了可比较的评测手段。

Abstract: Embodied Visual Tracking (EVT) is an essential capability for intelligent robots in human-robot collaboration, requiring an agent to follow a designated target person in dynamic environments relying solely on egocentric visual perception. However, persistent pedestrian occlusions in dynamic scenes pose critical challenges to existing EVT methods. Lacking proactive visibility preservation, reactive tracking methods frequently experience prolonged target loss under severe occlusions. Therefore, we propose VP-EVT, a framework that treats target visibility as an explicit objective of motion control rather than a by-product of following. Acting on this objective requires understanding both what obstructs the target and how robot motion can change that obstruction. VP-EVT therefore uses language-guided perception to extract separate target and pedestrian observations. When occlusion makes current observations incomplete, VP-EVT combines visual history with ego-motion to maintain estimates of their relative positions and motion. Using these estimates and the current following waypoints, VP-EVT predicts whether continued following will sustain the obstruction. When the predicted risk is low, the original following motion is retained; otherwise, a recurrent controller combines updated estimates with maneuver context from preceding decisions to coordinate viewpoint adjustment and the return to stable following. Moreover, we introduce OccEVT-Bench for closed-loop evaluation of persistent occlusion and collect expert demonstrations for training. On its Risk split, VP-EVT improves success rate by 121.4% relative to a competitive baseline and reduces visibility deficit and longest target-loss duration by 78.8% and 74.4%, respectively.

</details>


### [203] [DemoStream: Batched Task-and-Motion-Plan Optimization for Automated Learning from Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22020)
*Caelan Garrett,Neel Jawale,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DemoStream：基于GPU加速TAMP的批量采样与约束求解系统，通过优化演示质量提升行为克隆成功率，并可在真实世界以约66条/小时采集演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作能力依赖大量演示数据，但现有采集方式要么需持续人工遥操作，要么仅适用于单一任务；TAMP虽可自主生成通用任务演示，但其解的多模态性使行为克隆学习困难。

Method: 在GPU加速TAMP基础上提出DemoStream：引入批量TAMP采样操作与新的TAMP算法，并行进行批量约束满足以探索大量解；通过仿真实验消融不同动作空间、代价函数与批量大小对下游策略成功率的影响；并利用TAMP的安全性保证直接采集真实世界演示。

Result: 消融实验揭示了动作空间、代价函数与批量大小对下游策略成功率的影响规律；真实世界演示采集平均速率达66条/小时。

Conclusion: 优化演示质量是提升从TAMP演示中学习行为克隆效果的有效途径，批量并行TAMP采样可实现高效、安全的真实世界演示采集。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration is an effective strategy for endowing robots with manipulation capabilities but requires training on datasets comprised of massive amounts of demonstrations. Existing demonstration collection approaches either require constant human involvement, for example, via teleoperation or are specialized for a single task. Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) systems are able to autonomously solve and therefore generate demonstrations for general-purpose tasks; however, learning from their demonstrations has proven difficult due to multi-modality in their solutions. In this work, we build on recent work leveraging GPU acceleration for TAMP and propose DemoStream, a TAMP system that optimizes demonstration quality in order to improve behavior cloning success rates. First, we introduce batched TAMP sampling operations along with a new TAMP algorithm that correspondingly performs batched constraint satisfaction in order to explore many solutions in parallel. We conduct simulation experiments ablating different action spaces, cost functions, and batch sizes to analyze the effect of these choices on downstream policy success rates. Finally, we show that we can leverage the safety guarantees of TAMP to directly collect real-world demonstrations at an average rate of 66 demos per hour. See our website for policy rollout videos: this http URL .

</details>


### [204] [Plans Are Not Executions: Adapting Frozen World-Model Planners to Controller-Induced Execution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22041)
*Danqi Zhao,Keyu Chen,Wenchao Sun,Minglei Huang,Jianhe Zhang,Yan Zhu,Hao Cheng,Zifan Cao,Sifa Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出ExecutionBridge，通过计划-执行轨迹对推断控制器特有执行特性，并据此适配世界模型中的自车潜在rollout，从而缩小规划-执行差距，在NAVSIM上跨未见控制器配置稳定提升规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型规划器基于训练期学到的"计划-运动"关系预测候选轨迹后果，但部署时下游控制器可能以不同方式执行同一规划轨迹，形成"规划-执行差距"（planning-execution gap），使想象的未来与实际执行不符。作者进一步发现：执行保真度越高并不必然带来更好决策——直接替换为预测执行轨迹仅带来0.048 PDMS提升，说明规划轨迹（作为下游控制的参考）与执行轨迹（已描述控制器后运动结果）之间存在语义错配。

Method: 提出ExecutionBridge：从规划-执行轨迹对中推断控制器特有的执行特性，用其适配原生自车潜在rollout（latent rollout），同时保持原始规划轨迹不变。规划器、世界模型与奖励头均保持冻结（frozen），因此是一种轻量适配方法而非重训练。

Result: 在10个未见控制器配置下，直接替换预测执行轨迹仅带来0.048 PDMS提升，远小于所提适配方法的增益。在NAVSIM上，ExecutionBridge在多种未见控制器配置及多个冻结世界模型规划器上均一致提升规划表现，且在执行错配更强烈影响候选评估的困难驾驶场景中增益更大。

Conclusion: 提高执行保真度不等于提升决策质量，关键在于弥合计划轨迹与执行轨迹之间的语义错配；通过在潜在空间中适配执行特性而非替换轨迹，可在控制器变化和困难场景下稳健提升世界模型规划性能。

Abstract: World-model planners evaluate candidate trajectories by predicting their future consequences, implicitly relying on the plan-to-motion relationship learned during training. At deployment, a different downstream controller may execute the same planned trajectory differently, creating a planning-execution gap between the frozen model's imagined future and actual execution. A natural remedy is to replace the planned trajectory with its predicted or observed execution. However, we find that better execution fidelity does not necessarily yield better decisions: directly substituting predicted execution trajectories yields only a 0.048 PDMS improvement across 10 unseen controller configurations, far smaller than the gain from our proposed adaptation. This suggests a semantic mismatch between a planned trajectory, which serves as a reference for downstream control, and an executed trajectory, which already describes the resulting post-controller motion. We introduce ExecutionBridge, which infers controller-specific execution characteristics from planned-executed trajectory pairs and uses them to adapt the native ego latent rollout while keeping the original planned trajectory unchanged. The planner, world model, and reward head remain frozen. On NAVSIM, ExecutionBridge consistently improves planning across unseen controller configurations and multiple frozen world-model planners, with substantially larger gains in challenging driving scenes where execution mismatch more strongly affects candidate evaluation.

</details>


### [205] [CBF-Based Control Allocation for Omnidirectional Tiltrotor Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22057)
*Seobeom Lee,Hyondong Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于控制屏障函数(CBF)的力矩分配器，在有界伺服-构型失配下保持六维力旋量可行性；通过等效力旋量恒等式、椭球储备与十二个推力裕度约束，构建六变量伺服速率QP，并用条件前向不变性证明实际构型可行性；MuJoCo仿真中位置误差低于基线，受扰侧翻机动1751个采样点全可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动倾斜式空中机器人可通过倾斜实现六维力旋量输出，但伺服响应慢，重构过程中期望力旋量可能瞬时不可行。已有分配方法多假设构型与指令一致，缺乏对伺服-构型失配下力旋量可行性的鲁棒保证，因此需要一种能在重构过渡期保持可行性的分配控制器。

Method: 1) 建立标称构型与实际构型下所需推力的精确等效力旋量恒等式；2) 对非奇异六通道分配映射，用逆分配将鲁棒力旋量包含问题约化为十二个推力裕度约束，并由椭球储备收紧；3) 对裕度求全导数，形成六变量伺服速率二次规划，随后用微分推力分配跟踪；4) 给出条件前向不变性结果，证明在储备覆盖、可行初始化与容许CBF输入下实际构型可行性成立；5) MuJoCo中对比实测角度实现与几何分配、增广微分分配、归一化微分分配。

Result: 在圆周与侧翻(cartwheel)机动的四个记录场景中，所提实现位置误差均低于三类基线，但圆周跟踪存在姿态折衷。受扰侧翻机动中，1751个连续旋转采样点的期望力旋量全部可行；基线违反率介于60.8%至100%。作者指出这些对比仅评估采样点可行性，标称轨迹证书的验证仍需标称状态与储备覆盖记录。

Conclusion: CBF分配器能在有界伺服-构型失配下保持六维力旋量可行性，且实际构型可行性有前向不变性支撑，仿真中位置精度优于现有分配方案。局限在于证据为MuJoCo仿真、缺实机验证，且采样可行性不等价于完整证书验证。

Abstract: Actively tilting aerial robots can generate six-dimensional wrenches, but slow servo response can make a desired wrench infeasible during reconfiguration. This paper develops a control barrier function (CBF) allocator that preserves wrench feasibility under bounded servo-configuration mismatch. An exact equivalent-wrench identity connects the thrust required at nominal and actual configurations. For a nonsingular six-channel allocation map, inverse allocation reduces robust wrench containment to twelve thrust margins tightened by an ellipsoidal reserve. Their complete derivatives yield a six-variable servo-rate quadratic program, followed by differential thrust allocation for tracking. A conditional forward-invariance result establishes actual-configuration feasibility under reserve coverage, feasible initialization, and admissible CBF inputs. A complementary MuJoCo study evaluates a measured-angle implementation against geometric, augmented differential, and normalized differential allocation in circular and cartwheel maneuvers, with and without disturbances. The proposed implementation achieves lower position error in all four recorded scenarios, with an attitude tradeoff in circular tracking. During the disturbed cartwheel, the desired wrench is feasible at all 1751 stored samples of continuous rotation; baseline violation rates range from 60.8% to 100%. These comparisons evaluate sampled feasibility; validation of the nominal-trajectory certificate requires nominal-state and reserve-coverage records.

</details>


### [206] [Onboard Lightweight LLM Reasoning for Human-Aware Safe UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22092)
*Siwei Cai,Yuwei Wu,Vijay Kumar,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种全机载模块化感知-规划流水线：用共享BLIP编码器做场景描述与文本条件分割，融合深度得到3D障碍物；用轻量LLM（Qwen-2.5-0.5B，RAG+语法约束解码输出JSON）按物体类别推理风险并缓存，配合目标持久化ID与遮挡保持，为无人机提供类别相关的安全约束。Gazebo实验显示碰撞降低约3倍，语义感知降落成功率34%→96%，感知10Hz于Jetson Orin NX，LLM回退约0.8s且不在控制关键路径上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统感知-规划栈把所有障碍物都简化为占据几何，导致规划器无法获得类别相关的安全间距（行人、停放的车辆、墙体的通过距离应不同），且临时遮挡会使行人相关约束从地图中消失，形成"人本安全鸿沟"。目标是让机载无人机实时获得语境相关的安全约束。

Method: 模块化全机载流水线：1) 共享BLIP编码器同时驱动场景字幕生成与文本条件分割；2) 分割结果与深度融合，将障碍物定位到3D；3) 轻量LLM（Qwen-2.5-0.5B）推断障碍物专属风险，通过检索增强生成（RAG）与语法约束解码稳定输出，输出被限制为规划器兼容的JSON schema；4) LLM按物体类别而非逐帧推理，判定结果按类缓存，已见类别直接复用、新类别触发新的检索接地查询；5) 检测赋予持久身份ID并在遮挡期间保留于地图中。

Result: 感知流水线在Jetson Orin NX上以10Hz运行，LLM回退查询约0.8s且不位于控制关键路径。Gazebo中，行人与车辆环境下的低空城市导航相比纯几何规划器碰撞事件减少约3倍；移动参与者场景下的语义感知降落成功率从34%提升到96%。局限：224像素编码器输入限制远距离小目标的可靠感知，导航中通过保守行人间距与短期持久化缓解。

Conclusion: 通过把视觉观测转为类别条件的、可缓存的、带持久身份的3D安全约束，该工作在不占用控制关键路径的前提下弥合了人本安全鸿沟，显著改善无人机在人群与车辆间的导航与降落安全；主要瓶颈在于编码器分辨率对远距离小目标的感知。

Abstract: Aerial robots operating near people must treat a pedestrian, a parked car, and a wall differently, yet conventional perception--planning stacks reduce all of them to occupied geometry---a \emph{human-aware safety gap} in which category-specific clearance is unavailable to the planner and temporary occlusions remove pedestrian-specific constraints from its map. We present a modular, fully onboard pipeline that closes this gap by turning visual observations into context-dependent safety constraints for real-time UAV navigation.
