A shared BLIP encoder drives scene captioning and text-conditioned segmentation, which is fused with depth to localize obstacles in 3D. Obstacle-specific risk is inferred by a lightweight LLM (Qwen-2.5-0.5B) stabilized with retrieval-augmented generation and grammar-constrained decoding that restricts its output to a planner-compatible JSON schema. The LLM reasons once per object category rather than per frame: its verdicts are cached per class, so previously seen categories are resolved without inference and new ones trigger a fresh retrieval-grounded query; detections are additionally given persistent identities and held in the map across occlusions. The perception pipeline runs at 10\,Hz on a Jetson Orin NX, while fallback LLM queries take approximately 0.8\,s and remain off the control-critical path. In Gazebo, low-altitude urban navigation among pedestrians and vehicles shows a roughly $3\times$ reduction in collision events over a geometric planner, and semantics-aware landing among moving actors raises success from 34\% to 96\%. The 224-pixel encoder input limits reliable small-object perception at range; conservative pedestrian margins and short-term persistence mitigate this limitation during navigation.

</details>


### [207] [CF-Splat: Closed-Form Triangle Splatting with Mesh Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22108)
*Yixin Fang,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CF-Splat 将已知网格上的多视角外观估计表述为关于逐顶点 SH 系数的全局线性最小二乘问题，用闭式解替代 3DGS 式迭代非线性优化，并配合自适应 SH 正则化与三角结构无矩阵 PCG 求解器，实现高效高质新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 splatting 的辐射场表示在机器人感知中广受欢迎，但现有方法依赖对百万级耦合基元参数的迭代非线性优化，导致计算耗时大、峰值显存高、易陷入局部极小。作者希望借助已知网格（既有或由上游建图/重建提供）来规避这一非线性优化瓶颈。

Method: 在给定网格几何与标定相机下，将渲染像素颜色写成逐顶点球谐（SH）系数的线性函数，从而把多视角外观估计转化为全局线性最小二乘（LLS）问题；针对多视角覆盖不均导致的不适定问题，提出自适应 SH 正则化，抑制支持不足的高阶 SH 系数以获得唯一全局极小解；为可扩展至大场景，设计三角结构化的无矩阵预处理共轭梯度（PCG）求解器，无需显式构造大规模稀疏法方程，仍得到相同 LLS 解。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上，CF-Splat 的新视角合成质量与 SOTA splatting 方法相当或更优；在不同规模场景下，消费级笔记本上计算时间比 3DGS 减少约 27.5–378.0 倍，峰值显存平均降低约 1.8 倍。

Conclusion: 通过闭式三角 splatting 与自适应 SH 正则化，CF-Splat 在已知网格设定下把外观优化从非线性迭代转为全局 LLS，显著提速降存且保持有竞争力的渲染质量，适合作为建图/重建下游的高效外观估计模块；其依赖网格几何与标定相机，泛化到未知几何场景仍需上游重建支撑。

Abstract: Splatting-based radiance-field representations have recently gained popularity in robotic perception, enabling high-quality novel-view synthesis (NVS) through explicit scene primitives. However, existing splatting methods typically rely on iterative nonlinear optimization that jointly optimizes millions of coupled primitive parameters, resulting in substantial computation time, peak GPU memory consumption, and susceptibility to local minima. In this paper, we propose CF-Splat, a novel closed-form triangle-splatting framework for efficient scene appearance estimation over a known mesh either pre-existing or provided by upstream mapping or reconstruction pipelines. Our key insight is that, by properly formulating the problem over the given mesh geometry and calibrated cameras, the rendered pixel colors can be expressed linearly with respect to the per-vertex spherical harmonics (SH) coefficients, allowing multi-view scene appearance estimation to be formulated as a global linear least-squares (LLS) problem without the need of iterative nonlinear optimization. To guarantee a well-posed solution under uneven multi-view observation coverage, we introduce adaptive SH regularization that constrains insufficiently supported high-order SH coefficients and yields a unique global minimizer. To ensure the scalability to large-scale scenes, we develop a triangle-structured matrix-free preconditioned conjugate-gradient solver that avoids explicitly constructing the large sparse normal system while producing the same LLS solution. Experiments on diverse real-world datasets demonstrate that CF-Splat achieves competitive or superior NVS quality compared with state-of-the-art splatting methods. While, CF-Splat requires approximately 27.5-378.0 times less computation time and 1.8 times lower peak GPU memory on average than 3DGS across scenes of different scales on a consumer-grade laptop.

</details>


### [208] [Compliant GMT: Making General Motion Tracking Compliant via Residual Reference Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22119)
*Venki Parsuram,Michelle Wang,Jin-Hyuk Hong,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模块化框架C-GMT，通过在预训练通用运动跟踪器（GMT）上叠加基于本体感受历史的残差适配器，实现全身柔顺响应，兼顾通用运动跟踪与柔顺性，并在仿真与G1真机上验证了安全性、鲁棒性与误差容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人进入真实部署后，既需执行多样运动，又需对外界交互力做出安全鲁棒的响应。现有通用运动跟踪策略虽能模仿多样动作，但训练得过于僵硬，受扰动时会出现意外且不安全的行为；而已有柔顺人形控制工作在柔顺性与通用性之间存在取舍——全身柔顺响应的例子往往只针对单一参考动作，而通用跟踪器则刚性地拟合大规模动捕数据。

Method: 核心洞察是复用预训练GMT的通用性负责运动跟踪，同时引入一个以本体感受历史为条件的残差适配器来提供柔顺性。C-GMT为模块化框架：冻结/复用预训练GMT作为基础策略，残差适配器在其输出上叠加柔顺修正，条件输入为历史本体感受信号，从而实现全身柔顺响应而不牺牲跟踪通用性。

Result: 与SOTA通用运动跟踪器及单动作柔顺策略相比，C-GMT在运动跟踪精度与柔顺度两方面均具有竞争力；在仿真和真实G1实验中展示了更好的安全性、鲁棒性与误差容忍度。

Conclusion: 以残差适配器解耦"通用跟踪"与"柔顺响应"，可在不牺牲运动通用性的前提下获得全身柔顺能力，为人形机器人真实部署中的安全交互提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We propose Compliant GMT (C-GMT), a modular framework for full-body compliant responses in general motion tracking. As humanoid robots move into real world deployment, they not only need to perform any motion but also respond safely and robustly to interaction forces. Existing general motion tracking policies can imitate diverse motions but are trained to be stiff, leading to unexpected and unsafe behaviors under disturbance. Prior work in compliant humanoid control trades off compliance and generality. There exist examples of full-body compliant responses, but these are heavily curated to single-reference policies. On the other hand, general motion trackers (GMT) are trained to rigidly imitate massive motion capture datasets. Our key insight is to leverage the generality of pretrained GMTs for motion tracking while introducing compliance from a residual adapter that conditions on a history of proprioception. This enables our approach, C-GMT, to achieve both general motion tracking and whole-body compliant responses. Compared to state-of-the-art general motion trackers and single-motion compliant policies, C-GMT demonstrates competitive performance in both motion tracking accuracy and compliance adherence. We further demonstrate improved safety, robustness, and error tolerance in simulation and real-world G1 experiments.

</details>


### [209] [FastVLA: Task-Conditioned and Temporally Adaptive Visual Token Compression for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22146)
*Jiuyi Xu,Chengyuan Qian,Zhengzhong Tu,Yang Sui,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FastVLA：免训练、任务感知、时序自适应的VLA视觉token压缩框架，用任务-视觉注意力打分并依重要性分布均匀度（逆Simpson指标+在线滑窗标定）动态调整压缩率，在多种VLA模型与仿真/真机上实现降FLOPs与降延迟且保持成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA闭环部署受推理效率限制：每步需编码高分辨率、多视角图像为数百个视觉token，主导Transformer计算与延迟。基于注意力的token压缩虽自然，但VLA控制场景存在两大难点：(1)决策关键token由任务决定而非通用视觉显著性；(2)各步token预算差异大，静态指标与固定预算要么浪费算力、要么丢弃控制关键token。

Method: FastVLA为免训练、任务感知、时序自适应的视觉token压缩框架。每步通过任务到视觉的注意力（task-to-vision attention）对token重要性打分；再由重要性分布的均匀度（inverse-Simpson指标）自适应决定压缩率，并用在线滑动窗口进行标定，从而按步动态分配token预算。

Result: 在OpenVLA、OpenVLA-OFT、CogACT上，跨LIBERO与SIMPLER仿真评测：LLM主干FLOPs降低35–59%，动作延迟降低9–32%（1.09×–1.46×加速），任务成功率保持；效率-性能权衡优于或可比现有即插即用token压缩/剪枝/复用基线。在实体Franka Research 3机器人上端到端延迟降低20%（1.25×加速），成功率相当。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 任务感知与时序自适应的视觉token压缩可在不训练、不改模型的前提下显著降低VLA推理成本，且跨模型、跨仿真与真机迁移有效，为VLA闭环实时部署提供即插即用的效率方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models present a promising paradigm for general-purpose robot manipulation, but closed-loop deployment is often limited by inference efficiency: at each timestep, high-resolution, often multi-view images are encoded into hundreds of visual tokens that dominate transformer computation and latency. Token compression via attention is a natural solution but is challenging for VLA control because (1) decision-critical tokens are determined by the task, not generic visual saliency, and (2) the token budget varies substantially across steps, so static metrics and budgets waste computation or discard control-critical tokens. We introduce FastVLA, a training-free, task-aware, and temporally adaptive visual token compression framework. At each step, FastVLA scores token importance via task-to-vision attention and adaptively sets the compression rate from the importance-distribution uniformity, measured by an inverse-Simpson metric with online sliding-window calibration. We evaluate FastVLA on OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, and CogACT across LIBERO and SIMPLER, and on a physical Franka Research 3 robot. In simulation, it reduces LLM-backbone FLOPs by 35-59\% and action latency by 9-32\% (a 1.09$\times$-1.46$\times$ speedup) while maintaining task success, achieving a comparable or better efficiency-performance trade-off than existing plug-and-play token compression/pruning/reuse baselines. On the real robot, it cuts end-to-end latency by 20\% (1.25$\times$ speedup) at comparable success. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [210] [Inertially Significant Humanoid Teleoperation via Whole Body Model Predictive Control and Online Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22176)
*James Foster,Beomyeong Park,Luigi Penco,Robert Griffin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将全身模型预测控制(WB-MPC)与扩展卡尔曼滤波(EKF)结合，实现在线估计物理一致的时变惯性参数（如载荷质量、质心），并把估计量嵌入WB-MPC的刚体动力学模型以生成更准确的前馈力矩；配合VR界面让操作者只给任务参考、平衡交给控制器。在仿真与人形硬件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在使用重型工具或携带显著载荷时，惯性参数会时变，且载荷占系统比重很大，仅靠反馈控制无法忽略其影响，因此需要在线感知并适应惯性参数变化以维持任务性能与平衡。

Method: 整体框架为WB-MPC + EKF：EKF在线估计完全物理一致（满足物理可行约束）的时变惯性参数；估计结果前馈进入WB-MPC的全刚体动力学模型，用于计算面向任务需求的外载荷前馈力矩；同时提供VR接口，操作者仅提供任务级参考，动态平衡由WB-MPC负责。

Result: 在一个人形机器人系统上通过仿真与硬件实验验证了该框架，表明在外部载荷/工具使用条件下可获得更准确的前馈力矩并完成任务，同时保持动态平衡。

Conclusion: 在线估计时变惯性参数并前馈到全身MPC，是处理人形机器人负载任务的一种通用且可落地的方案；该工作在真实硬件上得到验证，为负重/工具操作下的人形全身控制提供了思路。

Abstract: The effective deployment of humanoid robots in practical tasks of interest will require a capability of sensing and adapting to variations in inertial parameters, as occurs during heavy tool use or when carrying significant payload. Such payloads constitute a significant proportion of the combined payload-robot system, the effects of which cannot be ignored by delegating to feedback control. This paper showcases a general solution to this problem by integrating a whole body model predictive controller (WB-MPC) with an extended Kalman filter (EKF) for online estimation of fully physically consistent time-varying inertial parameters. These estimates are fed through to the WB-MPC's full rigid body dynamics model, resulting in more accurate feedforward torques for task demands under external load. Additionally, a virtual reality (VR) interface is provided that enables an operator to provide task-specific references without regard to payload while delegating dynamic balance to the WB-MPC. The framework is validated with simulation and hardware experiments on a humanoid system.

</details>


### [211] [REKI: Registering Knowledge in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22178)
*Juliette Marrie,Paul Pacaud,Adrien Ramanana Rahary,Shizhe Chen,Moritz Böhle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REKI 是一个 2.4B 的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA），仅用公开数据预训练，在多个仿真基准上达到新的性能-规模 Pareto 前沿，并通过动作专家中的少量寄存器 token 实现低成本的近期历史记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型往往依赖大规模私有数据与庞大参数量，性能-规模权衡不佳；同时策略缺乏对近期历史的记忆，导致真机与仿真中姿态振荡等问题。作者希望用公开数据和简洁设计实现高效且具竞争力的 VLA。

Method: REKI 由 VLM 加流匹配（flow-matching）动作专家构成，在公开机器人数据、第一人称人类视频与视觉-语言数据的精选混合上预训练。记忆机制上，用动作专家中的若干寄存器 token 在每个帧上汇总 VLM 输出并持久保存于专家缓存中，以极低开销提供近期历史访问。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin Clean2Rand 与 RoboCasa365 上，仅被约两倍规模的模型超越。记忆机制提升仿真与真机成功率，并使姿态间振荡频率降低约 4 倍。

Conclusion: REKI 以简洁的模型与训练范式、公开数据与开源全套代码及权重，在训练和推理效率上做出优化，为 VLA 的性能-规模权衡与记忆机制提供了实用且可复现的方案。

Abstract: We introduce REKI, a 2.4B vision-language-action model (VLA) pretrained exclusively on public data that sets a new Pareto frontier between performance and model size: on LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin Clean2Rand and RoboCasa365, it is outperformed only by models about twice its size. REKI consists of a vision-language model (VLM) followed by a flow-matching action expert, and is pretrained on a curated mixture of public robot, egocentric human and vision-language data. Additionally, we endow it with a memory that gives the policy access to its recent past at negligible cost: a few register tokens in the action expert summarise the output of the VLM at each frame and persist in the expert's cache. This memory improves success in simulation and on real robots, and makes oscillations between poses about four times rarer. REKI uses a simple model design and training paradigm, and is optimised to be efficient at training and inference. We release the full code across data curation, training, inference and evaluation, deployment, and all pretrained and finetuned model weights: this https URL .

</details>


### [212] [Whole-Body Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22184)
*Conor Mc Gartoll,An Dang,Pranay Thangeda,Arjun Gupta,Carolina Higuera,Nima Fazeli,Mustafa Mukadam,Manikantan Nambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出全身灵巧操作（WBD）框架，把躯干与手臂作为接触面，通过生成全身抓取数据集、训练RL专家并蒸馏为单一策略，零样本迁移到Unitree G1完成搬箱等长时程任务，真机成功率90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形灵巧操作局限于末端执行器与简单抓取，无法像人类那样用全身（手臂、躯干）抵靠、滑动并搬运大体积物体。作者希望赋予机器人以身体与肢体作为接触面来抓取和操作物体的全身灵巧能力。

Method: （1）sim-to-real流水线：首先生成可行的全身抓取数据集；（2）在该数据集上采样初始与目标抓取，针对通用的any-to-any重定位目标训练RL专家；（3）将多专家蒸馏为单一策略，仅使用本体感知与物体几何作为输入；控制器以人类遥操作者的粗粒度运动参考在线引导。

Result: 蒸馏策略零样本迁移至真机Unitree G1，可将篮球与纸箱重新定位到躯干周围区域，并完成长时程任务（把箱子夹在臂下、从货架搬到桌上并放置）。真机全身操作任务中，蒸馏控制器完成90%试验，直接重定向为18%，通用全身控制器仅4%。

Conclusion: 表明用身体和肢体作为接触面的全身灵巧操作在真实人形机器人上可行，且专家蒸馏到单一本体感知+几何策略能带来显著的 sim-to-real 迁移与任务成功率提升，优于直接重定向与通用全身控制基线。

Abstract: Current humanoid dexterous manipulation remains largely confined to end-effectors and simple grasps, falling short of humans' ability to engage the entire body by bracing and sliding bulky objects against the arms and torso. We introduce Whole-Body Dexterity (WBD), a general framework that uses the robot's body and appendages as contact surfaces to grasp and manipulate objects. Our sim-to-real pipeline first generates a dataset of feasible whole-body grasps, then trains reinforcement-learning experts on a general any-to-any repositioning objective by sampling initial and goal grasps from the dataset, and distills these experts into a single policy that uses proprioception and object geometry. We show that the distilled policy transfers zero-shot to a real Unitree G1, where it repositions a basketball and a cardboard box to regions around the torso and completes a long-horizon task that stows a box under one arm, carries it from a shelf to a table, and places it. The controller operates interactively, steered by coarse motion references from a human teleoperator. Across real-world whole-body manipulation tasks, the distilled controller completes 90% of trials, compared with 18% for direct retargeting and 4% for a generalist whole-body controller.

</details>


### [213] [MOSS: Mode-Oriented Source Shaping for Robot Flow Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22193)
*Zirun Ma,Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Jiaqi Bai,Jiahua Dong,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOSS 将多模态生成策略的模式选择转化为对源空间初始噪声的显式选择：通过确定性流传输在源空间形成模式相关结构，构造语义对齐的模式专属高斯源，并用模态强度估计器自适应调整源几何，从而在推理时通过采样指定分量来控制执行模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式策略（扩散/流匹配）能建模机器人操作中"同一观测对应多个可行动作"的多模态行为，但实际执行哪个模式由随机采样决定，无法主动控制。这在需要指定行为、或数据不平衡导致少数模式被淹没时构成关键缺陷。

Method: 1) 从经验与理论两方面揭示确定性流传输会在源空间诱导出模式相关结构：理想 ODE 传输下不同行为模式对应不同源空间原像，而近似误差（数据不平衡、模型容量受限、推理步数少）会模糊边界。2) 基于该结构，MOSS 构造语义对齐的"模式专属高斯源"，把模式身份编码进初始噪声。3) 模态强度估计器预测局部多模态程度，自适应控制源几何：行为相近时向共享高斯收缩，在行为分叉点附近则相互分离。4) 部署时只需从对应分量采样即可选定目标行为。

Result: 在 6 个仿真与 3 个真实机器人操作任务上：模式导向成功率最高达 100% 且任务成功率高；在 10:90 不平衡数据集下以超过 90% 的引导率恢复少数模式；真实机器人上引导率比模式条件流策略高出 43.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: MOSS 表明生成策略的模式可控性可以归结为源空间的结构设计问题，通过模式专属源与自适应几何实现无需重新训练推理阶段的显式模式选择，兼顾多模态保持与少数模式覆盖，并在仿真与真机上验证有效性。

Abstract: In robotic manipulation, the same observation often admits multiple feasible actions. Generative policies such as diffusion and flow matching naturally capture such multimodal behaviors, yet which mode is executed is left to random sampling and cannot be actively controlled. We propose Mode-Oriented Source Shaping (MOSS), which turns mode selection into an explicit choice of where generation starts. We first reveal, both empirically and theoretically, that deterministic flow transport induces mode-dependent structure in the source space. Under ideal deterministic ODE transport, distinct behavioral modes have distinct source-space preimages, while practical approximation errors can blur their boundaries due to imbalanced data, limited model capacity, and few inference steps. Motivated by this source-space structure, MOSS constructs semantically aligned mode-specific Gaussian sources that encode mode identity in the initial noise. A modality-strength estimator predicts the local degree of multimodality, which adaptively controls the geometry of the mode-specific sources. It contracts them toward a shared Gaussian where behaviors are similar and separates them near behavioral branching points. During deployment, the desired behavior is selected at inference simply by sampling from the corresponding component. Across 6 simulated and 3 real-world manipulation tasks, MOSS steers to the target mode up to 100% of the time with high task success, recovers minority modes at over 90% guidance rate under a 10:90 imbalance dataset, and surpasses mode-conditioned flow policy by 43.3 points of guidance rate on real robots.

</details>


### [214] [Safe and Fuel-Efficient Motion Planning for Lunar Propulsive Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22225)
*Elena Wittemyer,Graco Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向月球跳跃无人机的自主运动规划框架，结合语义分割避障、可达地形快速计算与MPC轨迹跟踪，在指定航点间生成安全省燃料的跳跃轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来月球任务需用推进式无人机通过多次跳跃拍摄下视图像以生成高分辨率地形模型，而航点间的安全、高效跳跃需要自主规划能力，尤其是航点不安全或不可达时需具备备降能力。

Method: 全局规划器生成航点，无人机规划抛物跳跃轨迹；定制语义分割模型从航拍图像识别障碍以评估着陆点有效性，并提出可达地形快速计算方法；当标称航点不安全或不可达时执行改航着陆；用模型预测控制（MPC）鲁棒跟踪跳跃轨迹。

Result: 在高保真月球仿真环境中测试最优跳跃规划器，结果表明统一运动规划框架能生成安全、省燃料的跳跃轨迹，且MPC可快速、低误差地跟踪。

Conclusion: 该视觉引导最优控制框架在仿真中验证了月球跳跃无人机的安全高效运动规划可行性，为后续月球高分辨率地形测绘提供了技术基础，但尚缺乏真实月面环境验证。

Abstract: Propulsive drones are a promising new technology for generating high-resolution terrain models of the Moon. In future lunar missions, propulsive drones will complete a series of hops across the lunar surface while capturing images with downward-facing cameras. We present an autonomous motion planning framework that uses a vision-guided optimal control strategy for performing safe, efficient hops between specified waypoints. The drone plans parabolic hops between waypoints generated by a global-level planner; if a nominal waypoint is unsafe or unreachable, the drone then completes a diversion maneuver to safely land. To evaluate the validity of potential landing sites, we develop a custom semantic segmentation model for identifying obstacles from aerial images, and we introduce an approach for rapidly calculating reachable terrain. We then use model predictive control (MPC) to robustly track hop trajectories that guide the drone towards safe landing sites. We test the optimal hop planner in a high-fidelity lunar simulation environment. Our results demonstrate that our unified motion planning framework yields safe, fuel-efficient hop trajectories which can be tracked rapidly and with low error via MPC.

</details>


### [215] [Asking Smarter Questions: Targeted Active Preference Learning for Underspecified Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22285)
*Helena Merker,Nick Walker,Andreea Bobu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出把"人工反馈覆盖不足"的负担从人转移到机器人：构建知情先验把偏好查询定向到监督薄弱特征，并用解释聚焦人类注意力，仿真低查询预算下比标准主动偏好学习提升30.8%，用户研究验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励学习假设人类输入覆盖任务所需全部信息，但人类注意力有限，教学天然对某些特征监督不足。已有工作能从教学数据中识别哪些特征监督差，但解决方式是让人类重做，加重了本已困难者的负担。

Method: 核心洞见：指示机器人"不知道什么"的同一信息可用于生成更好的提问。做法有两点：(1) 构建知情先验(informed prior)，将偏好查询定向到需要消除不确定性的特征；(2) 为每个查询附带解释，把人类注意力聚焦到这些特征上。不确定性来源为教学数据中对特征监督不足的估计。

Result: 仿真中在低查询预算(降低人类努力最关键)情形下，知情先验比标准主动偏好学习基线最高提升30.8%；用户研究中，知情先验与解释各自显著改善奖励恢复，且二者相互放大。

Conclusion: 将不确定性诊断转化为查询定位与解释设计，可让机器人主动承担弥补弱监督的责任，在不增加人类负担的前提下提升奖励学习效果，两者协同优于单独使用。

Abstract: Reward learning typically assumes the human's input covers everything that matters for the task. In practice, humans can only attend to so much at once, so their teaching naturally captures some features---or aspects of the task---better than others. Prior work has shown how the robot can identify which features were poorly supervised directly from its teaching data. But the current solution is to ask the human to try again, putting the burden on someone who already struggled to convey those features. We propose shifting this burden to the robot. Our key insight is that the same information that tells the robot what it doesn't know can help it ask better questions. Preference queries offer a low-effort channel for resolving this uncertainty, but only if the robot knows where to direct them and the human knows what to attend to when answering. We act on this insight in two ways: we build an informed prior that targets preference queries at the features that need resolving, and we accompany each query with an explanation that focuses the human's attention on exactly those features. In simulation, the informed prior beats the standard active preference learning baseline by up to $30.8\%$ in the low-query regime, where reducing human effort matters most. In a user study, the informed prior and explanations each significantly improve reward recovery and amplify each other.

</details>


### [216] [Mem4Act: Learning Effective Latent Memory for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22323)
*Yun Zhang,Zhiyu Huang,Tianhui Cai,Chen Tang,Wei Wang,Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mem4Act：把历史编码为定长隐记忆（多粒度时序聚合+问答/关键帧DINO重建监督+记忆优先两阶段训练），在RoboMME、RMBench与4个真机任务上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要记忆，但保留原始观测/压缩视觉token会让策略面对不断变长的历史，语言摘要又会丢失任务相关的时序、语义与感知信息；核心问题是如何在固定预算下保留关键信息并保证策略真正使用记忆。

Method: 提出了Mem4Act，学习固定大小的隐记忆表示：1）多粒度时序聚合高效覆盖长历史；2）互补监督——问答式语义监督+关键帧DINO特征重建保留感知细节；3）记忆优先的两阶段训练，强制动作策略依赖记忆。记忆同时编码高层任务状态与细粒度感知证据，并作为有界接口输入策略。

Result: 在RoboMME上成功率达49.88%，RMBench 61.0%，四个真机操作任务81.5%，均为依赖记忆控制任务的SOTA；消融验证了时序聚合、互补记忆监督与记忆优先训练的必要性。

Conclusion: 隐式定长记忆是长时程机器人操作中替代长上下文与语言摘要的有效方案，多粒度聚合与互补监督加记忆优先训练共同保证记忆的信息量与可用性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation fundamentally requires memory: agents must retain task-relevant information from past observations and interactions and use it to guide future actions. Existing approaches typically preserve raw historical observations, compressed visual tokens, or language summaries, but these representations either expose the action policy to increasingly long histories or risk discarding task-relevant temporal, semantic, and perceptual information. We introduce Mem4Act, a framework for learning effective latent memory as a fixed-size representation explicitly optimized for long-horizon robotic control. Mem4Act addresses three coupled challenges: how to efficiently cover long histories, what information memory should retain, and how to ensure the policy actually relies on memory. To this end, it organizes history via multi-granularity temporal aggregation, preserves complementary semantic and perceptual information through question-answering supervision and key-frame DINO feature reconstruction, and employs memory-first two-stage training to encourage greater use of memory by the action policy. The resulting memory captures both high-level task state and fine-grained perceptual evidence while providing a bounded interface to the action policy. Across RoboMME, RMBench, and four real-world manipulation tasks, Mem4Act achieves state-of-the-art performance on memory-dependent control, with success rates of 49.88%, 61.0%, and 81.5%, respectively. Extensive ablations further demonstrate the importance of temporal aggregation, complementary memory supervision, and memory-first training.

</details>


### [217] [Illumination-Aware Active Perception for Spacecraft Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22335)
*Sagarika Rao Valluri,Benjamin Riviere*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以分类VAE潜在方差作为"图像信息量"度量的航天器巡检规划框架，替代传统几何可见性指标，在物理真实感仿真中显著提升观测光照质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有巡检规划器依赖视线（line-of-sight）遮挡检测，将几何可见性等同于任务相关信息，忽略了食、自阴影与镜面反射等光照效应，导致图像信息不足却仍被判定为"已巡检"。

Method: 用渲染的航天器表面部件图块训练分类VAE；编码器做部件分类，其总潜在方差（total latent variance）被用来刻画部件光照变化，把二值可见性检查替换为连续、基于图像的信息量度量。在照片级真实物理仿真器中实现GNC架构，跨多条轨道变化光照与观测几何。

Result: 各方法均能最大化自身目标，但本方法光照质量更优：平均亮度0.57，81.6%观测亮度高于0.25；带光照带的几何规划为0.02（3.0%），纯几何规划为0.04（6.0%）。

Conclusion: 以学习到的图像信息量替代几何可见性可有效规避光照失效模式，为空间巡检规划提供了图像级、连续的目标函数。

Abstract: Harsh and rapidly varying orbital illumination introduces failure modes during spacecraft inspection. Existing planners rely on line-of-sight inspection metrics which perform occlusion checks against a known target and conflate geometric visibility with task-relevant information. These metrics overlook illumination effects including eclipse, self-shadowing, and specular reflection from spacecraft materials and tend to declare components as inspected despite the corresponding images lacking sufficient information. We propose an inspection-planning framework that replaces geometric visibility with a learned measure of camera information derived from a classifier variational autoencoder (VAE) that operates directly on the captured image. The encoder model is trained to classify spacecraft surface components from rendered patches, and its total latent variance is used to learn the varying illumination of said components, replacing binary visibility checks with a continuous, image-grounded quantity. We evaluate the approach by implementing a Guidance, Navigation, and Control architecture in a photorealistic physics simulator, and vary lighting and viewing geometries with multiple orbits. We show that, although all approaches successfully maximize their own objective, our approach results in better illumination: a mean brightness of $0.57$, with $81.6\%$ of observations above $0.25$, compared with $0.02$ ($3.0\%$) for geometric planning with an illumination band and $0.04$ ($6.0\%$) for purely geometric planning.

</details>


### [218] [Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Path-Safe Target Reaching in Robotic Harvesting of Hydroponic Lettuce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22337)
*Al Bashir,Partho Ghose,Yibin Wang,Robert G. Hardin,Joshua Peeples,Genhua Niu,Azlan Zahid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PSA-PPO：在标准clipped PPO基础上引入目标条件观测、走廊空间约束、轨迹历史安全编码与任务成功判据的局部到达策略，纯Isaac Lab训练后零微调迁移至UR10e真机，实现NFT水培生菜温室采摘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室NFT水培生菜采摘空间受限，末端执行器需在作物近端约束环境下精确定位；经典规划器（Cartesian+TOTG、RRT类）在受限走廊内难以兼顾路径安全与终点精度，且纯终点优化目标不足以保证采摘兼容性。

Method: PSA-PPO：保留标准clipped PPO目标，策略公式化融合（1）目标条件观测，（2）走廊感知空间约束，（3）基于轨迹历史的安全编码，（4）面向约束操作的任务专属成功判据。全部在Isaac Lab训练，直接迁移UR10e真机，无需真实域策略微调；另配RGB-D视觉模块与末端执行器构成完整采摘系统；并做200目标仿真消融分析路径安全与侧走廊整形项贡献。

Result: 物理基准150个目标：PSA-PPO到达率94.7%，优于Cartesian+TOTG(86.0%)、RRTConnect(88.0%)、RRT*(80.0%)，末段中位RMS jerk降低39.8%–73.0%。消融显示去掉路径安全或侧走廊项会显著降低严格成功率（即使终点精度保持或提升）。完整系统144次采摘：视觉引导到达94.4%，完整采摘成功86.1%。

Conclusion: 在受限作物近端操作中，仅以终点为优化目标不足以保证采摘兼容性；显式路径安全与走廊整形是性能主导因素。所提框架支持从仿真到真机的零微调迁移，并可直接集成为完整温室采摘系统。

Abstract: Robotic harvesting requires precise manipulation to ensure accurate positioning of the end-effector, especially in a constraint workspace. This study introduces the Path-Safe Approach Proximal Policy Optimization (PSA-PPO), a target-conditioned local reaching framework designed for robotic harvesting of nutrient film technique (NFT)-hydroponic lettuce in greenhouses. The proposed PSA-PPO preserves the standard clipped PPO objective while introducing a harvesting-oriented policy formulation that integrates target-conditioned observations, corridor-aware spatial constraints, trajectory-history-based safety encoding, and task-specific success criteria for constrained robotic manipulation. PSAPPO was trained entirely in Isaac Lab and successfully transferred to a physical UR10e robotic arm without any real-world policy fine-tuning. In a controlled physical benchmark against 150 scheduled targets, PSA-PPO achieved the highest reaching rate of 94.7%, outperforming Cartesian+TOTG (86.0%), RRTConnect (88.0%), and RRT* (80.0%), while reducing median RMS jerk by 39.8 to 73.0% relative to the classical planners during the final approach segment. A formulation ablation evaluated over 200 simulation targets demonstrates that path-safety and side-corridor shaping are the principal drivers of harvesting-compatible performance: removing either term substantially degrades strict success even when terminal endpoint accuracy is preserved or improved, confirming that optimization toward the endpoint alone is an insufficient objective for constrained crop-proximal manipulation. Finally, PSA-PPO was integrated with an RGB-D vision module and an end-effector and evaluated as a complete greenhouse harvesting system across 144 lettuce harvesting attempts, achieving 94.4% vision-guided target reaching and 86.1% complete harvesting success.

</details>


### [219] [Dexterity from Supervised Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22340)
*Botao He,Yiming Dou,Yiannis Aloimonos,Jitendra Malik,Akash Sharma,Ruoshi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DSP框架：机器人通过行为克隆学习人类"监督玩耍"策略，自主采集数小时交互数据训练世界模型，在线用MPC引导规划，并可在世界模型内自博弈蒸馏闭环目标条件策略；在欠驱动推动与多指手任意到任意物体姿态重定向任务上验证，真实世界成功率超事后行为克隆69.6%，仅用1/490交互数据达到与强化学习相当水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型灵巧操作需要大量交互经验，但人工演示昂贵、真实环境强化学习交互代价极高，如何让机器人自主采集高质量接触交互数据是关键瓶颈。

Method: 两阶段：先由数分钟人类玩耍数据行为克隆得到玩耍策略，自主部署采集数小时交互数据训练世界模型；推理时玩耍策略为MPC提出候选动作序列，由世界模型评估；并引入在世界模型内自博弈蒸馏出闭环、目标条件策略以加速推理。

Result: 在欠驱动推动与多指手任意到任意in-hand姿态重定向（仿真+真实）上验证；真实世界成功率较hindsight behavior cloning高69.6%，与强化学习相当但交互数据少490倍。

Conclusion: 监督玩耍可自主采集交互数据、引导基于模型的规划并支持策略学习，无需任务专用演示或sim-to-real迁移，为接触密集型灵巧操作提供可扩展的数据与学习范式。

Abstract: How can robots autonomously collect the contact-rich experience needed to learn dexterous manipulation? We present DSP: Dexterity from supervised play, a framework in which a robot learns a play policy from minutes of human play through behavior cloning, then deploys it autonomously to collect hours of interaction data to train a world model. During inference, the same play policy then guides model-predictive control (MPC) by proposing candidate action sequences for evaluation under the learned world model. To accelerate inference, we also introduce a method for distilling a closed-loop, goal-conditioned policy through self-play within the learned world model. We evaluate this framework on underactuated pushing and any-to-any in-hand pose reorientation with a multifingered hand, in simulation and real world. In real world experiment, our method outperforms hindsight behavior cloning by 69.6% in success rate and achieves comparable success to reinforcement learning with 490 times less interaction data. These results demonstrate that supervised play can autonomously collect interaction data, guide model-based planning, and enable policy learning without any task-specific demonstrations or sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [220] [Spark-0: Scaling Spatially Aligned Tactile Pretraining for Robot Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22342)
*Tianxing Chen,Wenwei Lin,Chuqiao Lyu,Peicheng Xiang,Junyuan Tang,Tian Nian,Zijian Cai,Kehe Ye,Chenze Yu,Wenxuan Zhu,Eric J. Chen,Yan Qin,Yue Chen,Tongyu Wu,Junhao Gong,Kai-Chong Lei,Jiahao Zhang,Kailun Su,Qiwei Liang,Renjing Xu,Ping Luo,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Spark-0 提出空间对齐的视觉-触觉-语言-动作（VTLA）模型，将触觉同时用作预训练监督（图像空间接触图 + 冻结 3D 几何教师特征）与推理时的动作条件输入，并构建 1000 小时全手触觉人类操作数据集 HumanTouch 与 SparkArena 评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略受益于大规模视觉预训练，但在接触丰富的灵巧操作中表现不佳，因为力、柔顺性和初期滑移难以从图像中观测；触觉信息在预训练阶段的可扩展利用方式尚不明确。

Method: 以触觉手套导出的图像空间接触图与冻结 3D 几何教师特征监督视觉 token（推理时移除该目标）；并行设置触觉通路编码区域力历史与 3D taxel 状态以条件化动作专家；单一 checkpoint 同时支持纯视觉与触觉条件部署；在 HumanTouch（1000 小时全手触觉人类操作）、自我中心视频、机器人数据与仿真上联合预训练。

Result: 在 SparkArena 两条赛道取得最高平均成功率，并在 Ego Humanoid Manipulation Benchmark 全部八项聚合指标上排名第一；同等数据与算力下，保留 1000 小时 HumanTouch 触觉使纯视觉成功率从 52.5% 提升至 71.7%，SparkArena-Real 成功率从 44.0% 提升至 52.0%。

Conclusion: 触觉不仅能作为策略输入，还可作为灵巧视觉运动预训练的可扩展监督信号；同时释放 HumanTouch 数据集与 SparkArena 基准以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies benefit from large-scale visual pretraining but often struggle with contact-rich dexterous manipulation, where force, compliance, and incipient slip are hard to observe from images. We present Spark-0, a spatially aligned vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) model that uses touch in two complementary ways. During pretraining, image-space contact maps derived from tactile gloves, together with features from a frozen 3D geometry teacher, supervise the visual tokens; these objectives are removed at inference. In parallel, a tactile pathway encodes regional force histories and 3D taxel states to condition the action expert. A single checkpoint therefore supports both vision-only and tactile-conditioned deployment. To scale this supervision, we build HumanTouch, a 1,000-hour dataset of full-hand tactile human manipulation, and pretrain Spark-0 on it together with egocentric video, robot data, and simulation. We also release SparkArena, which pairs vision-only simulated tasks with tactile-conditioned real-robot tasks on one dexterous embodiment. Spark-0 achieves the highest mean success rate on both SparkArena tracks and ranks first on all eight aggregate metrics of the Ego Humanoid Manipulation Benchmark. Compared with vision-only pretraining on the same data and compute with touch discarded, retaining the touch of 1,000 hours of HumanTouch raises vision-only success on Ego Humanoid from 52.5% to 71.7%, and success on SparkArena-Real, where touch is also used at deployment, from 44.0% to 52.0%, indicating that touch serves not only as a policy input but also as scalable supervision for dexterous visuomotor pretraining. The Website is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [221] [CoDA: Co-Evolution of Data Engines and Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22362)
*Tianjiao Yu,Zhilin Ou,Yifan Shen,Xinzhuo Li,Yuanzhe Liu,Onkar Kishor Susladkar,Fan Wang,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoDA 提出仿真数据引擎与动作策略通过共享异构记忆协同演化的框架：视觉语言评论器产生的诊断被编译为"生成程序"（重建困难环境）与"训练程序"（含 Text2potential 分阶段势函数，为 action-chunk 价值学习提供稠密监督），以学习进度驱动生成，OOD 成功率由 SFT 的 5.3% 提升至 27.6%，LIBERO-Pro 上位姿扰动 25%→84%、指令扰动 1%→76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式仿真可自动构建机器人环境，但"物理合法"不等于"训练有用"。核心难点在于识别能暴露策略失败的环境，并为策略提供克服这些失败的监督信号——即环境生成与监督生成需针对策略的失败模式，而非随机扩增。

Method: CoDA 框架：仿真数据引擎与动作策略通过共享的异构记忆（环境构建、策略失败、学习进度）协同演化；记忆将视觉语言评论器给出的、经校验的诊断编译为生成程序（重建困难场景）与训练程序（帮助策略克服困难）。进一步提出 Text2potential，把诊断转化为分阶段势函数，用于 action-chunk 价值学习，在标准 shaping 假设下保持任务最优策略不变，同时提供稠密监督；学习进度作为度量反向指导后续生成与程序精炼。

Result: 四个操作任务族上，OOD 成功率从监督微调后的 5.3% 提升至 27.6%，在相同生成预算下超出最强闭环基线 9.5 个百分点；LIBERO-Pro 上 15 轮生成与训练使位姿扰动成功率由 25% 升至 84%，指令扰动由 1% 升至 76%。

Conclusion: 将环境生成、失败诊断与奖励/监督生成统一到由学习进度驱动的闭环记忆系统中，可显著提升策略的分布外鲁棒性与泛化；Text2potential 表明分阶段势函数能在不改变最优策略的前提下提供稠密价值监督，为生成式 sim 与策略共演化提供了可扩展范式。

Abstract: Generative simulation can automate robot environment construction, but physically valid environments are not necessarily useful for training. The challenge is to identify environments that address a policy's failures and supervision that helps it overcome them. We introduce CoDA, a framework in which a simulation data engine and an action policy co-evolve through a shared heterogeneous memory of environment construction, policy failures, and learning progress. The memory compiles validated diagnoses from a vision-language critic into generation programs that recreate difficulties and training programs that help the policy overcome them. We further propose Text2potential to convert these diagnoses into staged potentials for action-chunk value learning, providing dense supervision for policy updates while preserving the task's optimal policy under standard shaping assumptions. Measured learning progress guides further generation and program refinement. Across four manipulation task families, CoDA improves out-of-distribution success from 5.3% after supervised fine-tuning to 27.6%, exceeding the strongest closed-loop baseline by 9.5 percentage points with the same generation budget. On LIBERO-Pro, 15 rounds of generation and training raise success from 25% to 84% under position perturbations and from 1% to 76% under instruction perturbations.

</details>


### [222] [T2-z: Test-Time Optimization of Environment Latents for Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22364)
*Chenyuan Zhou,Yining Hong,Huang Huang,Yunfan Jiang,Wenlong Huang,David Held,Vitor Campagnolo Guizilini,Yue Wang,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Leonidas Guibas,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 T2-Z，一种测试时优化环境隐变量的机器人世界模型方法：训练时交替优化世界模型与隐变量，测试时冻结模型、仅从少量交互轨迹中优化新初始化的环境隐变量，以捕捉摩擦、质量、环境规则等不可观测因素，在 7 个仿真设置和 4 个真实机器人任务上显著降低物体预测误差并提升动作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人预测动作后果时，仅凭视觉观测不足，还需考虑不可直接观测、也未被显式标注的环境因素（摩擦、质量、人为环境规则等）。现有世界模型缺乏对此类环境变化的显式建模与在线适应能力，难以泛化到未见过的环境配置。

Method: 为世界模型引入可学习的紧凑环境隐变量以条件化预测。训练分两阶段交替：(1) 固定隐变量、优化动作条件动力学世界模型；(2) 冻结世界模型、优化条件隐变量，使隐空间结构仅通过世界预测任务自发涌现，无需任何环境因素的监督标注。测试时冻结世界模型，用一条或多条测试交互轨迹从头初始化并优化环境隐变量，实现对新环境配置的适应。并用消融验证仅单条轨迹即可推断隐变量。

Result: 在 7 个仿真设置与 4 个真实机器人任务中，物体预测误差指标从 15.20 降至 7.00，动作成功率从 33.73% 提升至 72.96%；涌现的隐变量在多类视觉场景中对单一及复合环境因素形成结构化表征；消融显示仅需单条测试交互轨迹即可高效推断环境隐变量。

Conclusion: 无监督环境隐变量 + 测试时优化可作为机器人世界模型适应未见环境配置的通用机制，兼具仿真与真实任务的有效性，且样本效率高（单轨迹即可适应）。

Abstract: Predicting how the world responds to an action requires more than what can be seen: robots must account for environmental factors that are neither directly observable nor explicitly specified, from physical properties such as friction and mass to human-defined environmental rules. We introduce T2-Z for test-time optimization of environment latents, which augments a robotic world model with a learnable environment latent to condition world predictions. This compact latent captures underlying environmental variation without supervision of what environmental factors exist or what values they take. During training, T2-Z alternates between two stages: a world-model optimization stage that fixes the latents and learns general action-conditioned dynamics across environments; and an environment-latent optimization stage that freezes the world model and optimizes the conditioning latents, allowing latent structure to emerge purely through world prediction. At test time, the world model remains frozen and a newly initialized environment latent is optimized from one or more test-time interactions, adapting the model to previously unseen environmental configurations. Across 7 simulated settings and 4 real-world robotic tasks, T2-Z reduces object prediction error metric from 15.20 to 7.00 and raises action success from 33.73% to 72.96%. Its emergent latents capture structured representations of individual and compounded factors across diverse visual scenes. Ablations further show that T2-Z can efficiently infer environment latents from as little as a single test-time interaction trajectory.

</details>


### [223] [Value as Memory: Online Value-Guided Post-training for General Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22368)
*Wenye Yu,Yijin Chen,Kaiyi Xiong,Wenbo Han,Wendi Chen,Yang Jin,Han Xue,Jun Lv,Cewu Lu,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VaM 通过"价值缓存"把价值预测当作任务进度记忆来缓解部分可观测性，并结合在线真实世界 RL（离线示教+离策略干预+在策略回放）实现稀疏奖励下的自主适应，在长时程、可形变物体与接触丰富操作任务上平均绝对成功率提升 29.4%（价值缓存）与 32.8%（在线自主学习）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略的真实世界后训练面临两个瓶颈：一是部分可观测导致动作选择存在歧义；二是离线后训练方法使部署反馈被延迟，无法及时改进策略。作者希望既解决"记忆/状态估计"问题，又实现及时、样本高效的在线改进。

Method: 提出 Value as Memory (VaM) 框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 价值缓存（value caching）——将近期价值预测作为条件输入，充当任务进度记忆以消解部分可观测歧义；(2) 在线真实世界 RL——融合离线示教数据、离策略干预数据与在策略 rollout 进行更新，实现从离线 RL 预热到稀疏奖励下在线自主学习的平滑过渡。工程上采取数据采集与训练并发（concurrent data collection and training）的在线调度。

Result: 在涉及可形变物体与接触丰富操作的长时程任务上：价值缓存带来平均绝对成功率增益 29.4%，在线自主学习在匹配离线训练下增益 32.8%。在线调度在相同梯度步预算下完成 8 倍策略迭代，且并发采集与训练使每梯度步记录时间降低 2 倍以上。

Conclusion: 价值预测可作为有效的隐式记忆以应对部分可观测性；在线真实世界 RL 相比离线后训练能显著提升样本效率与最终成功率，表明"缓存价值 + 在线自主迭代"是通用机器人策略后训练的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Real-world post-training of generalist robot policies faces two challenges: partial observability makes action selection ambiguous, while offline post-training methods delay feedback from deployment experience. We present Value as Memory (VaM), a framework that addresses these challenges through value caching and online real-world reinforcement learning (RL). Value caching conditions actions on recent value predictions as task-progress memory, while online updates enable timely policy improvement by combining offline demonstrations, off-policy interventions, and on-policy rollouts for sample-efficient adaptation. As a result, VaM transitions from offline RL warmup to online autonomous learning with sparse rewards. Across long-horizon tasks involving deformable objects and contact-rich manipulation, VaM yields a mean absolute success-rate gain of $29.4\%$ with value caching, while achieving a $32.8\%$ gain with online autonomous learning over matched offline training. The evaluated online schedule performs $8\times$ as many policy iterations under the same gradient-step budget, with more than $2\times$ lower recorded time per gradient step with concurrent data collection and training.

</details>


### [224] [LIBERO-PeRM: Benchmarking Personalized Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22372)
*Zhixu Li,Keqian Tang,Litian Gong,Jingyu Yao,Wenqian Zhang,Tianze Xu,Zehao Wang,Yuping Wang,Junge Zhang,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LIBERO-PeRM 基准，将个性化操作中的"任务完成"与"偏好满足"解耦评估，基于 LIBERO 生成成对演示，覆盖 5 类研究问题（零样本偏好遵循、单偏好学习、演示效率、跨布局/任务泛化、偏好组合与冲突），含 5 个任务套件（312 任务）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作已被广泛研究，但个性化机器人操作（PeRM）缺乏受控基准，无法把任务完成与用户偏好满足分离开来评估，阻碍机器人进入家庭场景的实用化。

Method: 提出可观测偏好的受控形式化与分类（动作方式、技能、用户画像选择），限定为语言可表达、不改变任务目标、可自动评测、可跨任务复用的偏好；在 LIBERO 上构建程序化流水线，生成带/不带偏好的成对演示，配套高质量数据、丰富元数据与偏好满足度指标。

Result: 现有 VLA 策略零样本个性化表现差；SFT 能使偏好可学习；混合训练相比顺序适配更利于保持无偏好能力，并在上下文提示下优于仅偏好训练，但可能削弱动作偏好学习；偏好泛化、分解与冲突处理仍然困难。

Conclusion: LIBERO-PeRM 为具身智能体的偏好学习提供了受控评测平台，揭示个性化操作在泛化、组合与冲突场景下的关键缺口，指明未来 VLA 个性化研究的数据与训练策略方向。

Abstract: Personalization is essential for general-purpose robots to transition into household environments and achieve true practicality. While general robotic manipulation has been extensively studied, personalized robotic manipulation (PeRM) lacks a controlled benchmark for separating task completion from preference satisfaction. We introduce LIBERO-PeRM, a benchmark for preference learning in embodied agents. LIBERO-PeRM studies five questions: 1) how policies follow preferences in zero-shot settings; 2) how they learn single preferences from demonstrations; 3) how efficiently they acquire preferences as demonstrations increase; 4) how learned preferences generalize across layouts and tasks; and 5) how policies compose preferences and behave under conflicts. We propose a controlled formulation and taxonomy of observable preferences in simulation, covering motion, skill, and user-profile choices. This formulation is restricted to language-expressible preferences that can be instantiated in LIBERO without changing the task goal, evaluated automatically, and reused across tasks. Building on LIBERO, our procedural pipeline generates paired demonstrations with and without preferences. We create five task suites (312 tasks) with high-quality demonstrations, rich metadata, and preference-satisfaction metrics. Experiments show that current VLA policies struggle with zero-shot personalization, while SFT makes preferences learnable. Mixed training achieves better no-preference retention than sequential adaptation and improves performance with contextual prompts compared with preference-only training, but can weaken motion-preference learning; preference generalization, decomposition, and conflict handling remain difficult. The code, datasets, and demos can be found on the project website: this https URL .

</details>
